(set-logic ALL)

; =============================================================================
; Constr_PPort_Per_Event_7_11_3_7_2_12
; 每个已连接的诊断事件，都应当恰有一个向服务组件提供的 PPortPrototype
; =============================================================================
(declare-sort AtomicSwComponentType 0)
(declare-sort DiagEvent 0)
(declare-sort PPortPrototype 0)

(declare-fun Connected (AtomicSwComponentType DiagEvent) Bool)
(declare-fun ProvidesToService (AtomicSwComponentType PPortPrototype DiagEvent) Bool)

; 存在性
(assert
  (forall ((a AtomicSwComponentType) (e DiagEvent))
    (=> (Connected a e)
        (exists ((p PPortPrototype))
          (ProvidesToService a p e)))))

; 唯一性
(assert
  (forall ((a AtomicSwComponentType) (e DiagEvent) (p1 PPortPrototype) (p2 PPortPrototype))
    (=> (and (ProvidesToService a p1 e)
             (ProvidesToService a p2 e))
        (= p1 p2))))

; =============================================================================
; TPS_SWCT_01061 — 单位转换（全局约束）
; =============================================================================
(declare-sort Unit 0)
(declare-sort UnitGroup 0)
(declare-sort PhysDim 0)
(declare-sort Country 0)

(declare-fun Convert (Unit Country Unit) Bool)
(declare-fun Dim (Unit) PhysDim)
(declare-fun InGroup (Unit UnitGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun EquivUnits (UnitGroup) Bool)

; 维度一致方可转换
(assert
  (forall ((u1 Unit) (c Country) (u2 Unit))
    (=> (Convert u1 c u2)
        (= (Dim u1) (Dim u2)))))

; 若 u1 属于 EQUIV_UNITS 组，则目标也应在该组
(assert
  (forall ((u1 Unit) (c Country) (u2 Unit))
    (=> (and (Convert u1 c u2)
             (exists ((g UnitGroup)) (and (EquivUnits g) (InGroup u1 g))))
        (exists ((g UnitGroup)) (and (EquivUnits g)
                                     (InGroup u1 g)
                                     (InGroup u2 g))))))

; BSW 接口三选一

(declare-const uses_ComM_ClientServerInterface Bool)
(declare-const uses_ComM_ModeSwitchInterface Bool)
(declare-const uses_BswM_ModeSwitchInterface Bool)

(assert
  (= 1
     (+ (ite uses_ComM_ClientServerInterface 1 0)
        (ite uses_ComM_ModeSwitchInterface 1 0)
        (ite uses_BswM_ModeSwitchInterface 1 0))))

      (not (or has_CompuMethod has_Unit has_BaseType))))

; =============================================================================
; RunnableEntity 唯一 symbol（合并：同时支持互斥区字段）
; =============================================================================
(declare-datatypes ((ExclusiveArea 0)) ((EA (eaId Int))))
(declare-datatypes ((RunnableEntity 0))
  ((RE (reId Int) (symbol String) (exclusiveAreas (Set ExclusiveArea)))))

(declare-const allRunnables (Set RunnableEntity))

(assert
  (forall ((r1 RunnableEntity) (r2 RunnableEntity))
    (=> (and (set.member r1 allRunnables)
             (set.member r2 allRunnables)
             (= (symbol r1) (symbol r2)))
        (= r1 r2))))

(assert
  (forall ((c1 Curve) (c2 Curve))
    (=> (and (set.member c1 curveGroup)
             (set.member c2 curveGroup))
        (= (axisDefId c1) (axisDefId c2)))))

; =============================================================================
; 可变长数组结构定义链
; =============================================================================
(declare-datatypes ((IDTElement 0))
  ((IDTE (category String)
         (arraySizeSemantics String)
         (arraySize Int)
         (arraySizeHandling String))))

(declare-const rootElement IDTElement)
(declare-const childElement IDTElement)
(declare-const terminalElement IDTElement)

(assert (= (category rootElement) "STRUCTURE"))
(assert (= (arraySizeSemantics rootElement) "variableSize"))
(assert (> (arraySize rootElement) 0))
(assert (= (arraySizeHandling rootElement) "allIndicesDifferentArraySize"))

(assert (= (arraySizeSemantics childElement) ""))
(assert (= (category childElement) "ARRAY"))
(assert (= (arraySize childElement) 0))
(assert (= (arraySizeHandling childElement) ""))

(assert (= (arraySizeSemantics terminalElement) "variableSize"))
(assert (> (arraySize terminalElement) 0))
(assert (= (arraySizeHandling terminalElement) "allIndicesSameArraySize"))

; =============================================================================
; Merge Delegation：nfold / single
; =============================================================================
(declare-datatypes ((DataElement 0) (PPort 0))
  ((DE (elementId Int))
   (PP (portId Int) (providedElements (Set DataElement)))))

(declare-const outerPPortDataElements (Set DataElement))
(declare-const innerPPorts (Set PPort))
(declare-const signalFan_nfold Bool)
(declare-const signalFan_single Bool)

; nfold：每个外层元素，至少被某个内层端口提供
(assert
  (=> signalFan_nfold
      (forall ((de DataElement))
        (=> (set.member de outerPPortDataElements)
            (exists ((pp PPort))
              (and (set.member pp innerPPorts)
                   (set.member de (providedElements pp))))))))

; single：每个外层元素，恰被一个内层端口提供
(assert
  (=> signalFan_single
      (forall ((de DataElement))
        (=> (set.member de outerPPortDataElements)
            (exists ((pp PPort))
              (and (set.member pp innerPPorts)
                   (set.member de (providedElements pp))
                   (forall ((pp2 PPort))
                     (=> (and (set.member pp2 innerPPorts)
                              (set.member de (providedElements pp2)))
                         (= pp pp2)))))))))

(assert (or has_StbM_TimeBase_TriggerCustomer
            has_StbM_TimeBase_StateNotification))

; =============================================================================
; SwBaseType 简单兼容性（演示）
; =============================================================================
(declare-datatypes ((SwBaseType 0)) ((SBT (typeId Int) (conversionRule String))))
(define-fun compatible ((t1 SwBaseType) (t2 SwBaseType)) Bool
  (not (= (conversionRule t1) "")))
(declare-const type1 SwBaseType)
(declare-const type2 SwBaseType)
(assert (=> (compatible type1 type2) (not (= (conversionRule type1) ""))))

; AutosarParameterRef

; =============================================================================
; Timing Event Period 推荐（示例）
; =============================================================================
(declare-const nvBlockDescriptorPeriod Real)
(declare-datatypes ((NvBlockNeeds 0)) ((NBN (cyclicWritingPeriod Real))))
(declare-const mappedNvBlockNeeds (Set NvBlockNeeds))

(define-fun minCyclicWritingPeriod ((needs (Set NvBlockNeeds))) Real
  (ite (= needs (as set.empty (Set NvBlockNeeds)))
       1000.0
       1.0))

(assert (>= nvBlockDescriptorPeriod 0.0))
(assert
  (=> (not (= mappedNvBlockNeeds (as set.empty (Set NvBlockNeeds))))
      (<= nvBlockDescriptorPeriod (minCyclicWritingPeriod mappedNvBlockNeeds))))

; =============================================================================
; Type-Prototype 可达性
; =============================================================================
(declare-datatypes ((DataPrototype 0) (DataType 0))
  ((DP (protoId Int) (dataType DataType))
   (DT (typeId Int) (containedPrototypes (Set DataPrototype)))))

(declare-const rootDataPrototype DataPrototype)
(declare-const contextDataPrototypes (Set DataPrototype))
(declare-const g_defaultTargetPrototype DataPrototype)

(define-fun-rec reachable ((from DataPrototype) (via (Set DataPrototype)) (to DataPrototype)) Bool
  (or (= from to)
      (exists ((intermediate DataPrototype))
        (and (set.member intermediate (containedPrototypes (dataType from)))
             (or (= intermediate to)
                 (and (set.member intermediate via)
                      (reachable intermediate via to)))))))

(assert (reachable rootDataPrototype contextDataPrototypes g_defaultTargetPrototype))
(assert
  (forall ((ctx1 DataPrototype) (ctx2 DataPrototype))
    (=> (and (set.member ctx1 contextDataPrototypes)
             (set.member ctx2 contextDataPrototypes)
             (not (= ctx1 ctx2)))
        (or (reachable ctx1 contextDataPrototypes ctx2)
            (reachable ctx2 contextDataPrototypes ctx1)))))

; =============================================================================
; 量纲为“无量纲”的判定
; =============================================================================
(declare-datatypes ((PhysicalDimension 0))
  ((PD (lengthExp Int)
       (massExp Int)
       (timeExp Int)
       (currentExp Int)
       (temperatureExp Int)
       (molarAmountExp Int)
       (luminousIntensityExp Int))))
(declare-const unitDimension PhysicalDimension)
(declare-const isDimensionless Bool)
(assert
  (= isDimensionless
     (and (= (lengthExp unitDimension) 0)
          (= (massExp unitDimension) 0)
          (= (timeExp unitDimension) 0)
          (= (currentExp unitDimension) 0)
          (= (temperatureExp unitDimension) 0)
          (= (molarAmountExp unitDimension) 0)
          (= (luminousIntensityExp unitDimension) 0))))

; =============================================================================
; 组合/组件/内部行为（统一定义，避免重复声明）
; =============================================================================
(declare-datatypes ((SwComponentType 0)) ((SCT (typeId Int))))
(declare-datatypes ((CompositionSwComponentType 0))
  ((CSCT (id Int) (components (Set SwComponentType)) (hasInternalBehavior Bool))))
(declare-datatypes ((SwcInternalBehavior 0))
  ((SWCIB (behaviorId Int) (runnables (Set RunnableEntity)))))

(declare-const outerComposition CompositionSwComponentType)
(declare-const innerComposition CompositionSwComponentType)

(define-fun canBeNested ((inner CompositionSwComponentType) (outer CompositionSwComponentType)) Bool
  (set.member (SCT (id inner)) (components outer)))

(assert
  (=> (not (= outerComposition innerComposition))
      (or (canBeNested innerComposition outerComposition)
          (not (canBeNested innerComposition outerComposition)))))

; 无二进制 footprint
(declare-const compositionHasBinaryFootprint Bool)
(assert (not compositionHasBinaryFootprint))

; 组合不应有 RunnableEntity（由 hasInternalBehavior = false 推出）
(declare-const composition CompositionSwComponentType)
(assert (not (hasInternalBehavior composition)))
(declare-const compositionRunnables (Set RunnableEntity))
(assert (= compositionRunnables (as set.empty (Set RunnableEntity))))

; =============================================================================
; 互斥区（注：运行期由调度保证，这里仅占位）
; =============================================================================
(declare-const runnables (Set RunnableEntity))
(declare-const sharedExclusiveArea ExclusiveArea)
(assert
  (forall ((r1 RunnableEntity) (r2 RunnableEntity))
    (=> (and (set.member r1 runnables)
             (set.member r2 runnables)
             (not (= r1 r2))
             (set.member sharedExclusiveArea (exclusiveAreas r1))
             (set.member sharedExclusiveArea (exclusiveAreas r2)))
        true)))

; =============================================================================
; 多个 DelegationSwConnector 语义（移除 set.card，改为“存在两个不同元素”）
; =============================================================================
(declare-datatypes ((PortPrototype 0) (DelegationSwConnector 0) (AssemblySwConnector 0))
  ((PP (portId Int) (isConnected Bool))
   (DSC (dscId Int) (outerPort PortPrototype))
   (ASC (ascId Int) (referencedPort PortPrototype))))

(declare-const outerPort PortPrototype)
(declare-const delegationConnectors (Set DelegationSwConnector))
(declare-const assemblyConnectors (Set AssemblySwConnector))

(assert
  (=> (exists ((d1 DelegationSwConnector) (d2 DelegationSwConnector))
        (and (set.member d1 delegationConnectors)
             (set.member d2 delegationConnectors)
             (not (= d1 d2))
             (= (outerPort d1) outerPort)
             (= (outerPort d2) outerPort)))
      (forall ((dsc DelegationSwConnector))
        (=> (and (set.member dsc delegationConnectors)
                 (= (outerPort dsc) outerPort))
            (exists ((asc AssemblySwConnector))
              (and (set.member asc assemblyConnectors)
                   (= (referencedPort asc) outerPort)))))))

(declare-datatypes ((NvData 0)) ((NVD (hasReasonableValue Bool))))

(assert
  (=> (not (isConnected abstractRequiredPort))
      (hasReasonableValue g_defultNvData)))

; --- Constr_ClientServerOperation_SyncAsyncExclusivity ---
; title: Exclusive Synchronous or Asynchronous Invocation for ClientServerOperation Instance
; expression: It is not supported to invoke the same instance of a ClientServerOperation synchronously and asynchronously together.

(declare-const isInvokedSynchronously Bool)
(declare-const isInvokedAsynchronously Bool)

; An instance cannot be invoked both synchronously AND asynchronously.
(assert (not (and isInvokedSynchronously isInvokedAsynchronously)))


; --- Constr_ReentrantCodeCalledByRunnables_7_2_4_2 ---
; title: Reentrancy of code called by different RunnableEntitys
; expression: All code that is called by different RunnableEntitys (e.g., library routines) shall be reentrant.

(declare-sort Runnable 0)
(declare-sort Code 0)

; (calls r c) means Runnable 'r' calls Code 'c'
(declare-fun calls (Runnable Code) Bool)
; (isReentrant c) means Code 'c' is reentrant
(declare-fun isReentrant (Code) Bool)

(assert
  (forall ((r1 Runnable) (r2 Runnable) (c Code))
    (=> (and (calls r1 c) (calls r2 c) (not (= r1 r2)))
        (isReentrant c))))


; --- Figure_6_11 ---
; title: Illegal split of delegation connector: Data set partitioning
; expression: combined data elements must form an exact, non-overlapping partition


(declare-fun outer-port (DataElement) Bool)    ; element is in outer port
(declare-fun inner-port-1 (DataElement) Bool)  ; element is in inner port 1
(declare-fun inner-port-2 (DataElement) Bool)  ; element is in inner port 2

; 1) Non-overlap
(assert (forall ((d DataElement))
  (not (and (inner-port-1 d) (inner-port-2 d)))))

; 2) Exact cover: outer IFF (inner1 OR inner2)
(assert (forall ((d DataElement))
  (= (outer-port d) (or (inner-port-1 d) (inner-port-2 d)))))


; --- Figure_6_12 ---
; title: Illegal merge of delegation connector: Interface compatibility
; expression: target-required elements must be provided by ALL sources

(declare-sort DataElement_F12 0)
(declare-fun source-1 (DataElement_F12) Bool)
(declare-fun source-2 (DataElement_F12) Bool)
(declare-fun target-requires (DataElement_F12) Bool)

(assert (forall ((d DataElement_F12))
  (=> (target-requires d) (and (source-1 d) (source-2 d)))))


; --- Figure_6_13 ---
; title: Invalid end-to-end delegation of SwConnectors
; expression: RPort required set ⊆ PPort provided set

(declare-sort DataPrototype_F13 0)
(declare-fun rport-requires (DataPrototype_F13) Bool)
(declare-fun pport-provides (DataPrototype_F13) Bool)

(assert (forall ((dp DataPrototype_F13))
  (=> (rport-requires dp) (pport-provides dp))))


; --- TPS_SWCT_01000 ---
; title: Usage of attribute symbol of the symbolProps
; expression: IFF symbolProps is defined, its symbol is used; else use shortName

(declare-sort AtomicSwComponentType 0)
(declare-fun get-symbol-props (AtomicSwComponentType) String)
(declare-fun get-short-name (AtomicSwComponentType) String)
(declare-fun get-final-symbol (AtomicSwComponentType) String)
(declare-fun exists-symbol-props (AtomicSwComponentType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((swc AtomicSwComponentType))
  (= (get-final-symbol swc)
     (ite (exists-symbol-props swc)
          (get-symbol-props swc)
          (get-short-name swc)))))


; --- TPS_SWCT_01001 ---
; title: Prefix symbols generated for the RunnableEntity
; expression: IFF symbolProps exists on owning SWC, prefix runnable name with it


(declare-fun get-owner-swc (RunnableEntity) AtomicSwComponentType)
(declare-fun get-runnable-symbol (RunnableEntity) String)
(declare-fun get-base-runnable-name (RunnableEntity) String)

(assert (forall ((r RunnableEntity))
  (let ((swc (get-owner-swc r)))
    (= (get-runnable-symbol r)
       (ite (exists-symbol-props swc)
            (str.++ (get-symbol-props swc) (get-base-runnable-name r))
            (get-base-runnable-name r))))))


; --- TPS_SWCT_01002 ---
; title: SwComponentTypes may only interact by means of their PortPrototypes
; expression: hidden interactions are forbidden

(declare-sort Interaction 0)

(declare-fun is-hidden-communication (Interaction) Bool)
(declare-fun is-mediated-by-port (Interaction PortPrototype) Bool)

; An interaction is NOT hidden IFF it is mediated by at least one PortPrototype
(assert (forall ((i Interaction))
  (= (not (is-hidden-communication i))
     (exists ((p PortPrototype)) (is-mediated-by-port i p)))))


; --- TPS_SWCT_01003 ---
; title: Inconsistencies regarding PortInterface.serviceKind
; expression: implementation kind wins

(declare-sort PortInterface 0)
(declare-fun get-implementation-kind (PortInterface) String)
(declare-fun get-attribute-service-kind (PortInterface) String)
(declare-fun get-final-service-kind (PortInterface) String)

(assert (forall ((pi PortInterface))
  (= (get-final-service-kind pi) (get-implementation-kind pi))))


; --- TPS_SWCT_01005 ---
; title: SwcServiceDependency for vendor-specific services
; expression: if vendor-specific, it shall not contain standardized ServiceNeeds

(declare-sort SwcServiceDependency 0)
(declare-sort ServiceNeeds 0)
(declare-fun is-vendor-specific (SwcServiceDependency) Bool)
(declare-fun contains-need (SwcServiceDependency ServiceNeeds) Bool)
(declare-fun is-standardized (ServiceNeeds) Bool)

(assert (forall ((d SwcServiceDependency) (n ServiceNeeds))
  (=> (and (is-vendor-specific d) (contains-need d n))
      (not (is-standardized n)))))


; --- TPS_SWCT_01007 ---
; title: Semantics of array index
; expression: valid index i iff 0 <= i < arraySize

(declare-sort ImplementationDataType 0)
(declare-fun get-array-size (ImplementationDataType) Int)
(declare-fun is-valid-index (ImplementationDataType Int) Bool)

(assert (forall ((adt ImplementationDataType) (i Int))
  (= (is-valid-index adt i)
     (and (>= i 0) (< i (get-array-size adt))))))


; --- TPS_SWCT_01098 ---
; title: Only AtomicSwComponentType can have RunnableEntitys
; expression: components with runnables must be atomic


(declare-fun hasRunnable (SwComponentType RunnableEntity) Bool)
(declare-fun isAtomic (SwComponentType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((c SwComponentType) (r RunnableEntity))
  (=> (hasRunnable c r) (isAtomic c))))


; --- TPS_SWCT_01100 ---
; title: Precedence of PortInterfaceMapping
; expression: PIM mapping overrides shortName mapping

(declare-sort Mapping 0)
(declare-sort Connector 0)
(declare-fun getMappingByPIM (Connector) Mapping)
(declare-fun getMappingByShortName (Connector) Mapping)
(declare-fun hasPIM (Connector) Bool)
(declare-fun getFinalMapping (Connector) Mapping)

(assert (forall ((c Connector))
  (= (getFinalMapping c)
     (ite (hasPIM c) (getMappingByPIM c) (getMappingByShortName c)))))


; --- TPS_SWCT_01101 ---
; title: Unmapped elements of PortInterfaces
; expression: elements connected by a SwConnector must be mapped

(declare-sort Element 0)
; NOTE: avoid overloading: use a distinct predicate name for (Element, Connector)
(declare-fun isConnectedEC (Element Connector) Bool)
(declare-fun isMapped (Element) Bool)

(assert (forall ((e Element) (c Connector))
  (=> (isConnectedEC e c) (isMapped e))))


; --- TPS_SWCT_01113 ---
; title: Connecting two PortPrototypes
; expression: if connected, PPort and RPort must be compatible

(declare-sort PPort 0)
(declare-sort RPort 0)
; NOTE: avoid overloading: separate predicate for (PPort, RPort)
(declare-fun isConnectedPR (PPort RPort) Bool)
(declare-fun isCompatible (PPort RPort) Bool)

(assert (forall ((p PPort) (r RPort))
  (=> (isConnectedPR p r) (isCompatible p r))))


; --- TPS_SWCT_01120 ---
; title: Client must provide values for in/inout ArgumentDataPrototypes
; expression: for arg of direction in|inout, client provides a value

(declare-sort Argument 0)
(declare-sort Value 0)
(declare-fun getDirection (Argument) String)
(declare-fun clientProvidesValue (Argument Value) Bool)

(assert (forall ((a Argument))
  (=> (or (= (getDirection a) "in")
          (= (getDirection a) "inout"))
      (exists ((v Value)) (clientProvidesValue a v)))))

(declare-fun needs_writeVerification (NvBlockNeeds) Bool)
(declare-fun needs_writingFrequency (NvBlockNeeds) Int)
(declare-fun needs_writingPriority (NvBlockNeeds) PriorityClass)
(declare-fun needs_reliability (NvBlockNeeds) ReliabilityClass)

; Helpers
(define-fun ge_prio ((a PriorityClass) (b PriorityClass)) Bool
  (or (= a b)
      (and (= a PRIO_MAX) (or (= b PRIO_LOW) (= b PRIO_MED) (= b PRIO_HIGH)))
      (and (= a PRIO_HIGH) (or (= b PRIO_LOW) (= b PRIO_MED)))
      (and (= a PRIO_MED) (= b PRIO_LOW))))
(define-fun ge_rel ((a ReliabilityClass) (b ReliabilityClass)) Bool
  (or (= a b)
      (and (= a REL_MAX) (or (= b REL_LOW) (= b REL_MED) (= b REL_HIGH)))
      (and (= a REL_HIGH) (or (= b REL_LOW) (= b REL_MED)))
      (and (= a REL_MED) (= b REL_LOW))))

; ---------- “additional_not_binding” recommendations as detectable violations ----------

; Any-request ⇒ should be true
(define-fun violation_ramBlockStatusControl ((d NvBlockDescriptor)) Bool
  (exists ((p PortPrototype))
    (and (mappedNvPort d p)
         (req_ramBlockAPI p)
         (not (needs_ramBlockStatusControl (needsOf d))))))

(define-fun violation_storeAtShutdown ((d NvBlockDescriptor)) Bool
  (exists ((p PortPrototype))
    (and (mappedNvPort d p)
         (req_storeAtShutdown p)
         (not (needs_storeAtShutdown (needsOf d))))))

(define-fun violation_storeCyclic ((d NvBlockDescriptor)) Bool
  (exists ((p PortPrototype))
    (and (mappedNvPort d p)
         (req_storeCyclic p)
         (not (needs_storeCyclic (needsOf d))))))

(define-fun violation_storeEmergency ((d NvBlockDescriptor)) Bool
  (exists ((p PortPrototype))
    (and (mappedNvPort d p)
         (req_storeEmergency p)
         (not (needs_storeEmergency (needsOf d))))))

(define-fun violation_storeImmediate ((d NvBlockDescriptor)) Bool
  (exists ((p PortPrototype))
    (and (mappedNvPort d p)
         (req_storeImmediate p)
         (not (needs_storeImmediate (needsOf d))))))

(define-fun violation_writeVerification ((d NvBlockDescriptor)) Bool
  (exists ((p PortPrototype))
    (and (mappedNvPort d p)
         (req_writeVerification p)
         (not (needs_writeVerification (needsOf d))))))

; Equality-to-request for every mapped port
(define-fun violation_readonly_eq ((d NvBlockDescriptor)) Bool
  (exists ((p PortPrototype))
    (and (mappedNvPort d p)
         (not (= (needs_readonly (needsOf d)) (req_readonly p))))))

(define-fun violation_resistant_eq ((d NvBlockDescriptor)) Bool
  (exists ((p PortPrototype))
    (and (mappedNvPort d p)
         (not (= (needs_resistantToChangedSw (needsOf d)) (req_resistantToChangedSw p))))))

(define-fun violation_restoreAtStart_eq ((d NvBlockDescriptor)) Bool
  (exists ((p PortPrototype))
    (and (mappedNvPort d p)
         (not (= (needs_restoreAtStart (needsOf d)) (req_restoreAtStart p))))))

(define-fun violation_writeOnlyOnce_eq ((d NvBlockDescriptor)) Bool
  (exists ((p PortPrototype))
    (and (mappedNvPort d p)
         (not (= (needs_writeOnlyOnce (needsOf d)) (req_writeOnlyOnce p))))))

; Highest-of-all requests (>= every requested; and optionally == some maximum)
(define-fun violation_writingFreq_max ((d NvBlockDescriptor)) Bool
  (exists ((p PortPrototype))
    (and (mappedNvPort d p)
         (> (req_writingFrequency p) (needs_writingFrequency (needsOf d))))))

(define-fun violation_writingPrio_max ((d NvBlockDescriptor)) Bool
  (exists ((p PortPrototype))
    (and (mappedNvPort d p)
         (not (ge_prio (needs_writingPriority (needsOf d)) (req_writingPriority p))))))

(define-fun violation_reliability_max ((d NvBlockDescriptor)) Bool
  (exists ((p PortPrototype))
    (and (mappedNvPort d p)
         (not (ge_rel (needs_reliability (needsOf d)) (req_reliability p))))))

; ---------- PortPrototype-context consistency recommendations ----------
(declare-fun connected (PortPrototype PortPrototype) Bool)

(define-fun violation_ports_readonly_consistent () Bool
  (exists ((p1 PortPrototype) (p2 PortPrototype))
    (and (connected p1 p2)
         (not (= (req_readonly p1) (req_readonly p2))))))

(define-fun violation_ports_resistant_consistent () Bool
  (exists ((p1 PortPrototype) (p2 PortPrototype))
    (and (connected p1 p2)
         (not (= (req_resistantToChangedSw p1) (req_resistantToChangedSw p2))))))

(define-fun violation_ports_restoreAtStart_consistent () Bool
  (exists ((p1 PortPrototype) (p2 PortPrototype))
    (and (connected p1 p2)
         (not (= (req_restoreAtStart p1) (req_restoreAtStart p2))))))

(define-fun violation_ports_storeAtShutdown_consistent () Bool
  (exists ((p1 PortPrototype) (p2 PortPrototype))
    (and (connected p1 p2)
         (not (= (req_storeAtShutdown p1) (req_storeAtShutdown p2))))))

(define-fun violation_ports_writeOnlyOnce_consistent () Bool
  (exists ((p1 PortPrototype) (p2 PortPrototype))
    (and (connected p1 p2)
         (not (= (req_writeOnlyOnce p1) (req_writeOnlyOnce p2))))))

; ---------- RptContainer categories (binding) ----------
(declare-sort RptContainer 0)
(declare-sort RptHook 0)

(declare-datatypes () ((RptCategory ACCESS_POINTS DATA_PROTOTYPE RUNNABLE_ENTITY SW_COMPONENT_PROTOTYPE)))
(declare-fun g_defultCategoryOf (RptContainer) RptCategory)

; Bypass/hook type predicates (your resolver should set these)
(declare-fun hasBypass_VariableAccess (RptContainer) Bool)
(declare-fun hasBypass_ParameterAccess (RptContainer) Bool)
(declare-fun hasBypass_ServerCallPoint (RptContainer) Bool)
(declare-fun hasBypass_AsynchServerCallResultPoint (RptContainer) Bool)
(declare-fun hasBypass_InternalTriggeringPoint (RptContainer) Bool)
(declare-fun hasBypass_ModeSwitchPoint (RptContainer) Bool)
(declare-fun hasBypass_ModeAccessPoint (RptContainer) Bool)
(declare-fun hasBypass_ExternalTriggeringPoint (RptContainer) Bool)
(declare-fun hasBypass_DataPrototype (RptContainer) Bool)
(declare-fun hasBypass_RunnableEntity (RptContainer) Bool)
(declare-fun hasBypass_SwComponentPrototype (RptContainer) Bool)

(declare-fun hook_VariableAccess (RptContainer) Bool)
(declare-fun hook_ParameterAccess (RptContainer) Bool)
(declare-fun hook_ServerCallPoint (RptContainer) Bool)
(declare-fun hook_AsynchServerCallResultPoint (RptContainer) Bool)
(declare-fun hook_InternalTriggeringPoint (RptContainer) Bool)
(declare-fun hook_ModeSwitchPoint (RptContainer) Bool)
(declare-fun hook_ModeAccessPoint (RptContainer) Bool)
(declare-fun hook_ExternalTriggeringPoint (RptContainer) Bool)
(declare-fun hook_DataPrototype (RptContainer) Bool)
(declare-fun hook_RunnableEntity (RptContainer) Bool)
(declare-fun hook_SwComponentPrototype (RptContainer) Bool)

; ACCESS_POINTS category requires bypass & hook of allowed kinds
(assert
  (forall ((c RptContainer))
    (=> (= (g_defultCategoryOf c) ACCESS_POINTS)
        (and
          (or (hasBypass_VariableAccess c) (hasBypass_ParameterAccess c)
              (hasBypass_ServerCallPoint c) (hasBypass_AsynchServerCallResultPoint c)
              (hasBypass_InternalTriggeringPoint c) (hasBypass_ModeSwitchPoint c)
              (hasBypass_ModeAccessPoint c) (hasBypass_ExternalTriggeringPoint c))
          (or (hook_VariableAccess c) (hook_ParameterAccess c)
              (hook_ServerCallPoint c) (hook_AsynchServerCallResultPoint c)
              (hook_InternalTriggeringPoint c) (hook_ModeSwitchPoint c)
              (hook_ModeAccessPoint c) (hook_ExternalTriggeringPoint c))))))

; DATA_PROTOTYPE
(assert
  (forall ((c RptContainer))
    (=> (= (g_defultCategoryOf c) DATA_PROTOTYPE)
        (and (hasBypass_DataPrototype c) (hook_DataPrototype c)))))

; RUNNABLE_ENTITY
(assert
  (forall ((c RptContainer))
    (=> (= (g_defultCategoryOf c) RUNNABLE_ENTITY)
        (and (hasBypass_RunnableEntity c) (hook_RunnableEntity c)))))

; SW_COMPONENT_PROTOTYPE
(assert
  (forall ((c RptContainer))
    (=> (= (g_defultCategoryOf c) SW_COMPONENT_PROTOTYPE)
        (and (hasBypass_SwComponentPrototype c) (hook_SwComponentPrototype c)))))

; ---------- Default InvalidationPolicy behavior (binding) ----------
(declare-sort SenderReceiverInterface 0)
(declare-sort VariableDataPrototype 0)
(declare-sort InvalidationPolicy 0)

; Enumerate full HandleInvalidEnum per Table 4.3
(declare-datatypes () ((HandleInvalidEnum
  dontInvalidate
  replaceWithExternalReplacement
  appHandlesViaErrorCodeOrEvent
  replaceWithInitValue)))

; Relationship
(declare-fun sriHasData (SenderReceiverInterface VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun hasPolicyFor (SenderReceiverInterface VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun policyOf (SenderReceiverInterface VariableDataPrototype) InvalidationPolicy)
(declare-fun handleInvalid (InvalidationPolicy) HandleInvalidEnum)

; Effective default: if no policy for a data element, behaves as dontInvalidate
(declare-fun effectiveHandleInvalid (SenderReceiverInterface VariableDataPrototype) HandleInvalidEnum)
(assert
  (forall ((s SenderReceiverInterface) (v VariableDataPrototype))
    (=> (sriHasData s v)
        (= (effectiveHandleInvalid s v)
           (ite (hasPolicyFor s v)
                (handleInvalid (policyOf s v))
                dontInvalidate)))))

; ---------- AssemblySwConnector matrix for SR/NvData (binding) ----------
(declare-sort AssemblySwConnector 0)
(declare-datatypes () ((PortKind RKIND PKIND PRKIND)))
(declare-datatypes () ((IfKind SR NV OTHER_IF)))

(declare-fun providerKind (AssemblySwConnector) PortKind)
(declare-fun requesterKind (AssemblySwConnector) PortKind)
(declare-fun providerIf (AssemblySwConnector) IfKind)
(declare-fun requesterIf (AssemblySwConnector) IfKind)

(define-fun bothSRorNv ((a IfKind) (b IfKind)) Bool
  (and (or (= a SR) (= a NV))
       (or (= b SR) (= b NV))))

; Allowed matrix (Requester -> Provider):
; R->R: No, R->P: Yes, R->PR: Yes
; P->R: Yes, P->P: No,  P->PR: Yes
; PR->R: Yes, PR->P: Yes, PR->PR: No
(define-fun allowed ((req PortKind) (prov PortKind)) Bool
  (or (and (= req RKIND) (= prov PKIND))
      (and (= req RKIND) (= prov PRKIND))
      (and (= req PKIND) (= prov RKIND))
      (and (= req PKIND) (= prov PRKIND))
      (and (= req PRKIND) (= prov RKIND))
      (and (= req PRKIND) (= prov PKIND))))

(assert
  (forall ((c AssemblySwConnector))
    (=> (bothSRorNv (providerIf c) (requesterIf c))
        (allowed (requesterKind c) (providerKind c)))))

; ---------- Notes ----------
; - All NvBlockNeeds “violation_*” are recommendations. To enforce, add:
;   (assert (forall ((d NvBlockDescriptor)) (not (violation_ramBlockStatusControl d))))  ... etc.
; - PortPrototype-context “violation_ports_*_consistent” are recommendations across connected nv-data ports.
; - The full HandleInvalidEnum above supersedes earlier stubs.





; ========= TPS_SWCT_01182 — Conceptual levels for the definition of initial values (priority 3 > 2 > 1) =========
; Sorts
(declare-sort DataItem 0)
(declare-sort VariableDataPrototype 0)
(declare-sort ParameterDataPrototype 0)
(declare-sort NonqueuedSenderComSpec 0)
(declare-sort NonqueuedReceiverComSpec 0)
(declare-sort ParameterProvideComSpec 0)
(declare-sort ParameterRequireComSpec 0)
(declare-sort NvRequireComSpec 0)
(declare-sort CalibrationParameterValue 0)

; Binding relations (which ARXML instances relate to the same conceptual data item)
(declare-fun bindsVDP (DataItem VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun bindsPDP (DataItem ParameterDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun bindsNQSend (DataItem NonqueuedSenderComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun bindsNQRecv (DataItem NonqueuedReceiverComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun bindsParProv (DataItem ParameterProvideComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun bindsParReq (DataItem ParameterRequireComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun bindsNvReq (DataItem NvRequireComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun bindsCPV (DataItem CalibrationParameterValue) Bool)

; Presence of definitions at each level
(declare-fun hasInitValueVDP (VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun hasInitValuePDP (ParameterDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun hasInitValueNQSend (NonqueuedSenderComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun hasInitValueNQRecv (NonqueuedReceiverComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun hasInitValueParProv (ParameterProvideComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun hasInitValueParReq (ParameterRequireComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun hasInitValueNvReq (NvRequireComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun hasApplInitValue (CalibrationParameterValue) Bool)
(declare-fun hasImplInitValue (CalibrationParameterValue) Bool)

; Effective source of initial value per DataItem
(declare-datatypes () ((InitSource none level1 level2 level3)))
(declare-fun effectiveInitSource (DataItem) InitSource)

; Convenience predicates for levels
(define-fun L1 ((i DataItem)) Bool
  (or (exists ((v VariableDataPrototype)) (and (bindsVDP i v) (hasInitValueVDP v)))
      (exists ((p ParameterDataPrototype)) (and (bindsPDP i p) (hasInitValuePDP p)))))

(define-fun L2 ((i DataItem)) Bool
  (or (exists ((s NonqueuedSenderComSpec)) (and (bindsNQSend i s) (hasInitValueNQSend s)))
      (exists ((r NonqueuedReceiverComSpec)) (and (bindsNQRecv i r) (hasInitValueNQRecv r)))
      (exists ((pp ParameterProvideComSpec)) (and (bindsParProv i pp) (hasInitValueParProv pp)))
      (exists ((pr ParameterRequireComSpec)) (and (bindsParReq i pr) (hasInitValueParReq pr)))
      (exists ((nv NvRequireComSpec)) (and (bindsNvReq i nv) (hasInitValueNvReq nv)))))

(define-fun L3 ((i DataItem)) Bool
  (exists ((c CalibrationParameterValue))
    (and (bindsCPV i c) (or (hasApplInitValue c) (hasImplInitValue c)))))

; Priority: level3 > level2 > level1 > none (does not forbid lower levels to co-exist; only defines the effective source)
(assert
  (forall ((i DataItem))
    (or (and (L3 i) (= (effectiveInitSource i) level3))
        (and (not (L3 i)) (L2 i) (= (effectiveInitSource i) level2))
        (and (not (L3 i)) (not (L2 i)) (L1 i) (= (effectiveInitSource i) level1))
        (and (not (L3 i)) (not (L2 i)) (not (L1 i)) (= (effectiveInitSource i) none)))))

; ========= TPS_SWCT_01183 — Interpret initValue according to the AutosarDataType =========
(declare-sort ApplicationDataType 0)
(declare-sort ImplementationDataType 0)

; Typing relations
(declare-fun typedByApp_PDP (ParameterDataPrototype ApplicationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun typedByImpl_PDP (ParameterDataPrototype ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun typedByApp_VDP (VariableDataPrototype ApplicationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun typedByImpl_VDP (VariableDataPrototype ImplementationDataType) Bool)

; Interpretation predicates
(declare-fun initIsPhysical_PDP (ParameterDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun initIsNumeric_PDP (ParameterDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun initIsPhysical_VDP (VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun initIsNumeric_VDP (VariableDataPrototype) Bool)

; Rules
(assert (forall ((p ParameterDataPrototype))
          (=> (exists ((a ApplicationDataType)) (typedByApp_PDP p a))
              (initIsPhysical_PDP p))))
(assert (forall ((p ParameterDataPrototype))
          (=> (exists ((t ImplementationDataType)) (typedByImpl_PDP p t))
              (initIsNumeric_PDP p))))
(assert (forall ((v VariableDataPrototype))
          (=> (exists ((a ApplicationDataType)) (typedByApp_VDP v a))
              (initIsPhysical_VDP v))))
(assert (forall ((v VariableDataPrototype))
          (=> (exists ((t ImplementationDataType)) (typedByImpl_VDP v t))
              (initIsNumeric_VDP v))))

; ========= TPS_SWCT_01184 — ApplicationPrimitiveDataTypes with category VALUE ⇒ physical init values only =========
(declare-sort ApplicationPrimitiveDataType 0)
(declare-fun categoryVALUE (ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun typedByAPT_PDP (ParameterDataPrototype ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun typedByAPT_VDP (VariableDataPrototype ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((p ParameterDataPrototype) (apt ApplicationPrimitiveDataType))
          (=> (and (typedByAPT_PDP p apt) (categoryVALUE apt))
              (initIsPhysical_PDP p))))
(assert (forall ((v VariableDataPrototype) (apt ApplicationPrimitiveDataType))
          (=> (and (typedByAPT_VDP v apt) (categoryVALUE apt))
              (initIsPhysical_VDP v))))

; ========= TPS_SWCT_01195 — Mapping of composite element to primitive DataPrototype =========
(declare-sort SubElementMapping 0)
(declare-sort FirstElement 0)
(declare-sort SecondElement 0)

; Sets of elements per mapping
(declare-fun firstElements (SubElementMapping) (Set FirstElement))
(declare-fun secondElements (SubElementMapping) (Set SecondElement))

; Exactly one firstElement
(assert
  (forall ((m SubElementMapping))
    (exists ((x FirstElement))
      (and (set.member x (firstElements m))
           (forall ((y FirstElement))
             (=> (set.member y (firstElements m)) (= y x)))))))

; Zero secondElements
(assert
  (forall ((m SubElementMapping))
    (= (secondElements m) (as set.empty (Set SecondElement)))))





; ========= TPS_SWCT_01269 — In PortInterfaces, initial values defined for DataPrototypes are ignored =========
(declare-sort PortInterface 0)


(declare-fun inPortInterface (DataPrototype PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun g_defultHasInitValue (DataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun initIgnoredInPI (DataPrototype) Bool)

; If a DataPrototype is within a PortInterface, its own initValue is ignored (superseded by ComSpec).
(assert
  (forall ((pi PortInterface) (dp DataPrototype))
    (=> (and (inPortInterface dp pi) (g_defultHasInitValue dp))
        (initIgnoredInPI dp))))

; ========= TPS_SWCT_01273 — Precedence rules for the application of SwDataDefProps (7 > 6 > ... > 1) =========
(declare-sort Item 0) ; abstract subject to which properties apply

(declare-sort ApplicationDataType 0)
(declare-sort ImplementationDataType 0)
(declare-sort InstantiationDataDefProps 0)
(declare-sort ParameterAccess 0)
(declare-sort FlatInstanceDescriptor 0)
(declare-sort McDataInstance 0)

(declare-fun bindsAppDT (Item ApplicationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun bindsImplDT (Item ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun bindsDP    (Item DataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun bindsIDDP  (Item InstantiationDataDefProps) Bool)
(declare-fun bindsPA    (Item ParameterAccess) Bool)
(declare-fun bindsFID   (Item FlatInstanceDescriptor) Bool)
(declare-fun bindsMCDI  (Item McDataInstance) Bool)

(declare-fun hasSwDDP_AppDT (ApplicationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun hasSwDDP_ImplDT (ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun hasSwDDP_DP    (DataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun hasSwDDP_IDDP  (InstantiationDataDefProps) Bool)
(declare-fun hasSwDDP_PA    (ParameterAccess) Bool)
(declare-fun hasSwDDP_FID   (FlatInstanceDescriptor) Bool)
(declare-fun hasSwDDP_MCDI  (McDataInstance) Bool)

(declare-datatypes () ((Level none lvl1 lvl2 lvl3 lvl4 lvl5 lvl6 lvl7)))
(declare-fun effectiveSwDDPLevel (Item) Level)

(define-fun L1 ((x Item)) Bool
  (exists ((a ApplicationDataType)) (and (bindsAppDT x a) (hasSwDDP_AppDT a))))
(define-fun L2 ((x Item)) Bool
  (exists ((i ImplementationDataType)) (and (bindsImplDT x i) (hasSwDDP_ImplDT i))))
(define-fun L3 ((x Item)) Bool
  (exists ((d DataPrototype)) (and (bindsDP x d) (hasSwDDP_DP d))))
(define-fun L4 ((x Item)) Bool
  (exists ((p InstantiationDataDefProps)) (and (bindsIDDP x p) (hasSwDDP_IDDP p))))
(define-fun L5 ((x Item)) Bool
  (exists ((pa ParameterAccess)) (and (bindsPA x pa) (hasSwDDP_PA pa))))
(define-fun L6 ((x Item)) Bool
  (exists ((f FlatInstanceDescriptor)) (and (bindsFID x f) (hasSwDDP_FID f))))
(define-fun L7 ((x Item)) Bool
  (exists ((m McDataInstance)) (and (bindsMCDI x m) (hasSwDDP_MCDI m))))

; Precedence: attributes on higher levels overwrite lower ones
(assert
  (forall ((x Item))
    (or (and (L7 x) (= (effectiveSwDDPLevel x) lvl7))
        (and (not (L7 x)) (L6 x) (= (effectiveSwDDPLevel x) lvl6))
        (and (not (L7 x)) (not (L6 x)) (L5 x) (= (effectiveSwDDPLevel x) lvl5))
        (and (not (L7 x)) (not (L6 x)) (not (L5 x)) (L4 x) (= (effectiveSwDDPLevel x) lvl4))
        (and (not (L7 x)) (not (L6 x)) (not (L5 x)) (not (L4 x)) (L3 x) (= (effectiveSwDDPLevel x) lvl3))
        (and (not (L7 x)) (not (L6 x)) (not (L5 x)) (not (L4 x)) (not (L3 x)) (L2 x) (= (effectiveSwDDPLevel x) lvl2))
        (and (not (L7 x)) (not (L6 x)) (not (L5 x)) (not (L4 x)) (not (L3 x)) (not (L2 x)) (L1 x) (= (effectiveSwDDPLevel x) lvl1))
        (and (not (L7 x)) (not (L6 x)) (not (L5 x)) (not (L4 x)) (not (L3 x)) (not (L2 x)) (not (L1 x)) (= (effectiveSwDDPLevel x) none)))))

; ========= TPS_SWCT_01287 — ASAP2 standard vs extended limits selection from DataConstrRule =========
(declare-sort DataConstr 0)
(declare-sort DataConstrRule 0)

(declare-fun belongs (DataConstrRule DataConstr) Bool)
(declare-fun constrLevel (DataConstrRule) Int) ; expected 0 or 1
(declare-fun hasExtendedLimits (DataConstr) Bool)
(declare-fun standardRuleOf (DataConstr DataConstrRule) Bool)
(declare-fun extendedRuleOf (DataConstr DataConstrRule) Bool)

; Helper macros: exactly one rule at level k for a given constraint
(define-fun existsUniqueLevel ((k Int) (dc DataConstr)) Bool
  (exists ((r DataConstrRule))
    (and (belongs r dc) (= (constrLevel r) k)
         (forall ((s DataConstrRule))
           (=> (and (belongs s dc) (= (constrLevel s) k)) (= s r))))))

; Case A: Exactly one rule with level 0 and none with level 1 → standard only, no extended
(assert
  (forall ((dc DataConstr))
    (=> (and (existsUniqueLevel 0 dc)
             (not (exists ((r1 DataConstrRule)) (and (belongs r1 dc) (= (constrLevel r1) 1)))))
        (and (not (hasExtendedLimits dc))
             (exists ((r0 DataConstrRule))
               (and (belongs r0 dc) (= (constrLevel r0) 0) (standardRuleOf dc r0)))))))

; Case B: Exactly one rule at level 0 and exactly one at level 1 → 1 = standard, 0 = extended
(assert
  (forall ((dc DataConstr))
    (=> (and (existsUniqueLevel 0 dc) (existsUniqueLevel 1 dc))
        (and (hasExtendedLimits dc)
             (exists ((r0 DataConstrRule) (r1 DataConstrRule))
               (and (belongs r0 dc) (= (constrLevel r0) 0)
                    (belongs r1 dc) (= (constrLevel r1) 1)
                    (extendedRuleOf dc r0)
                    (standardRuleOf dc r1)))))))





; ========= Common sorts for variable-size array constraints =========
(declare-sort ImplementationDataType 0)
(declare-sort ApplicationArrayDataType 0)
(declare-sort ImplementationDataTypeElement 0)

(declare-datatypes () ((DynProfile VSA_LINEAR VSA_SQUARE VSA_FULLY_FLEXIBLE VSA_RECTANGULAR OTHER)))
(declare-datatypes () ((Category ARRAY STRUCTURE OTHER_CAT)))

(declare-fun mapped (ImplementationDataType ApplicationArrayDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun idtProfile (ImplementationDataType) DynProfile)
(declare-fun aadProfile (ApplicationArrayDataType) DynProfile)

(declare-fun firstSubElement (ImplementationDataType) ImplementationDataTypeElement)
(declare-fun secondSubElement (ImplementationDataType) ImplementationDataTypeElement)
(declare-fun categoryOf (ImplementationDataTypeElement) Category)
(declare-fun arraySize (ImplementationDataTypeElement) Int)
(declare-fun isIntegerElement (ImplementationDataTypeElement) Bool)
(declare-fun capacityOf (ImplementationDataTypeElement) Int)

(declare-fun maxArraySize (ApplicationArrayDataType) Int)
(declare-fun firstDimSize (ApplicationArrayDataType) Int)
(declare-fun maxArraySize_ITD (ImplementationDataType) Int)

; TPS_SWCT_01618 — Size Indicator for AADT profiles VSA_LINEAR/SQUARE/FULLY_FLEXIBLE
(assert
  (forall ((t ImplementationDataType) (a ApplicationArrayDataType))
    (=> (and (mapped t a)
             (or (= (aadProfile a) VSA_LINEAR)
                 (= (aadProfile a) VSA_SQUARE)
                 (= (aadProfile a) VSA_FULLY_FLEXIBLE)))
        (and (isIntegerElement (firstSubElement t))
             (>= (capacityOf (firstSubElement t)) (maxArraySize a))))))

; TPS_SWCT_01647 — Same requirement when only IDT is present
(assert
  (forall ((t ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (or (= (idtProfile t) VSA_LINEAR)
            (= (idtProfile t) VSA_SQUARE)
            (= (idtProfile t) VSA_FULLY_FLEXIBLE))
        (and (isIntegerElement (firstSubElement t))
             (>= (capacityOf (firstSubElement t)) (maxArraySize_ITD t))))))

; TPS_SWCT_01619 — Rectangular profile: first sub-element is ARRAY with arraySize = according dimension
(assert
  (forall ((t ImplementationDataType) (a ApplicationArrayDataType))
    (=> (and (mapped t a) (= (aadProfile a) VSA_RECTANGULAR))
        (and (= (categoryOf (firstSubElement t)) ARRAY)
             (= (arraySize (firstSubElement t)) (firstDimSize a))))))

; TPS_SWCT_01620 — Size Indicator array element type/size (rectangular case)
(assert
  (forall ((t ImplementationDataType) (a ApplicationArrayDataType))
    (=> (and (mapped t a) (= (aadProfile a) VSA_RECTANGULAR))
        (and (isIntegerElement (firstSubElement t))
             (>= (capacityOf (firstSubElement t)) (maxArraySize a))))))

; TPS_SWCT_01621 — Payload element: second sub-element is ARRAY sized to maxArraySize
(assert
  (forall ((t ImplementationDataType) (a ApplicationArrayDataType))
    (=> (and (mapped t a)
             (not (= (aadProfile a) OTHER)))
        (and (= (categoryOf (secondSubElement t)) ARRAY)
             (= (arraySize (secondSubElement t)) (maxArraySize a))))))

; ========= TPS_SWCT_01609 — Rule FILL_UNTIL_MAX_SIZE semantics =========
(declare-sort RuleBasedValueSpecification 0)
(declare-sort RuleArguments 0)
(declare-sort ValueArray 0)

(declare-fun ruleIsFill (RuleBasedValueSpecification) Bool)
(declare-fun argsOf (RuleBasedValueSpecification) RuleArguments)
(declare-fun countArgs (RuleArguments) Int)
(declare-fun lastArgValue (RuleBasedValueSpecification) Int)
(declare-fun appliedArray (RuleBasedValueSpecification) ValueArray)
(declare-fun arrLen (ValueArray) Int)
(declare-fun maxSizeToFill (RuleBasedValueSpecification) Int)
(declare-fun valueAt (RuleBasedValueSpecification Int) Int)

; If argsCount < array length and < maxSizeToFill, fill from argsCount..min(maxSizeToFill, arrayLen)-1 with the last argument value.
(assert
  (forall ((r RuleBasedValueSpecification) (i Int))
    (=> (and (ruleIsFill r)
             (< (countArgs (argsOf r)) (arrLen (appliedArray r)))
             (< (countArgs (argsOf r)) (maxSizeToFill r))
             (<= 0 i)
             (<= (countArgs (argsOf r)) i)
             (< i (arrLen (appliedArray r)))
             (< i (maxSizeToFill r)))
        (= (valueAt r i) (lastArgValue r)))))

; ========= TPS_SWCT_01624 — Hard error prevents start of RunnableEntity =========

(declare-fun hardErrorInServerChain (RunnableEntity) Bool)
(declare-fun hardErrorInTriggerSink (RunnableEntity) Bool)
(declare-fun canStart (RunnableEntity) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((r RunnableEntity))
    (=> (or (hardErrorInServerChain r) (hardErrorInTriggerSink r))
        (not (canStart r)))))

; ========= TPS_SWCT_01625 — Coherence vs. intermediary values semantics =========

(declare-sort DataPrototypeGroup 0)
(declare-sort RunnableEntityGroup 0)

(declare-fun ownsDataPrototypeGroup (SwComponentType DataPrototypeGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun requiresCoherence (DataPrototypeGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun ownsRunnableGroup (SwComponentType RunnableEntityGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun groupDoesNotRequireStability (RunnableEntityGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun inGroup (RunnableEntity RunnableEntityGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun readCoherent (RunnableEntity) Bool)

(assert
  (forall ((s SwComponentType) (d DataPrototypeGroup) (g RunnableEntityGroup) (r RunnableEntity))
    (=> (and (ownsDataPrototypeGroup s d)
             (requiresCoherence d)
             (ownsRunnableGroup s g)
             (groupDoesNotRequireStability g)
             (inGroup r g))
        (not (readCoherent r)))))

; ========= TPS_SWCT_01626 — Extended transformer error notification requirement =========
(declare-sort PortAPIOption 0)


(declare-datatypes () ((DataTransformationErrorHandling transformerErrorHandling otherHandling)))
(declare-fun errorHandling (PortAPIOption) DataTransformationErrorHandling)
(declare-fun portRef (PortAPIOption) PortPrototype)

(declare-fun accesses (RunnableEntity PortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun handlesExtendedTransformerError (RunnableEntity) Bool)

(assert
  (forall ((o PortAPIOption) (r RunnableEntity))
    (=> (and (= (errorHandling o) transformerErrorHandling)
             (accesses r (portRef o)))
        (handlesExtendedTransformerError r))))

; ========= TPS_SWCT_01638 — PR↔PR connections are direction-neutral =========
(declare-sort AssemblySwConnector 0)
(declare-sort PRPortPrototype 0)

(declare-fun provider (AssemblySwConnector) PRPortPrototype)
(declare-fun requester (AssemblySwConnector) PRPortPrototype)
(declare-fun sameConnection (AssemblySwConnector AssemblySwConnector) Bool)

; If connectors cross-link the same PR ports but with swapped roles, they are the same undirected connection.
(assert
  (forall ((a AssemblySwConnector) (b AssemblySwConnector))
    (=> (and (= (provider a) (requester b))
             (= (requester a) (provider b)))
        (sameConnection a b))))

; ========= TPS_SWCT_01646 — Invalidation handled by RTE/Com ⇒ dontInvalidate =========
(declare-sort SenderReceiverInterface 0)
(declare-sort InvalidationPolicy 0)
(declare-datatypes () ((HandleInvalidEnum dontInvalidate otherHI)))
(declare-fun invalidationAppliedByRTECom (SenderReceiverInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun invalidationPolicyOf (SenderReceiverInterface) InvalidationPolicy)
(declare-fun handleInvalid (InvalidationPolicy) HandleInvalidEnum)

(assert
  (forall ((s SenderReceiverInterface))
    (=> (invalidationAppliedByRTECom s)
        (= (handleInvalid (invalidationPolicyOf s)) dontInvalidate))))

; TPS_SWCT_01219 - Queued vs non-queued communication constraint
(declare-sort VariableDataPrototype 0)
(declare-sort ReceiverComSpec 0)
(declare-sort SenderComSpec 0)
(declare-sort SwDataDefPropsContent 0)

(declare-fun dataElementReceiver (ReceiverComSpec) VariableDataPrototype)
(declare-fun dataElementSender (SenderComSpec) VariableDataPrototype)
(declare-fun swDataDefProps (VariableDataPrototype) SwDataDefPropsContent)
(declare-fun swImplPolicy (SwDataDefPropsContent) String)
(declare-fun isQueuedReceiverComSpec (ReceiverComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isNonqueuedReceiverComSpec (ReceiverComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isQueuedSenderComSpec (SenderComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isNonqueuedSenderComSpec (SenderComSpec) Bool)

(assert (forall ((rc ReceiverComSpec))
  (=> (= (swImplPolicy (swDataDefProps (dataElementReceiver rc))) "queued")
      (isQueuedReceiverComSpec rc))))

(assert (forall ((rc ReceiverComSpec))
  (=> (not (= (swImplPolicy (swDataDefProps (dataElementReceiver rc))) "queued"))
      (isNonqueuedReceiverComSpec rc))))

(assert (forall ((sc SenderComSpec))
  (=> (= (swImplPolicy (swDataDefProps (dataElementSender sc))) "queued")
      (isQueuedSenderComSpec sc))))

(assert (forall ((sc SenderComSpec))
  (=> (not (= (swImplPolicy (swDataDefProps (dataElementSender sc))) "queued"))
      (isNonqueuedSenderComSpec sc))))

; TPS_SWCT_01221 - DataFilter existence constraint
(declare-sort RPortPrototype 0)
(declare-sort NonqueuedReceiverComSpec 0)
(declare-sort DataFilter 0)
(declare-sort SenderReceiverInterface 0)

(declare-fun requiredInterface (RPortPrototype) SenderReceiverInterface)
(declare-fun hasDataFilter (NonqueuedReceiverComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isSenderReceiverInterface (SenderReceiverInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun isNonqueuedComm (RPortPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((rp RPortPrototype) (nqrc NonqueuedReceiverComSpec))
  (=> (and (isSenderReceiverInterface (requiredInterface rp))
           (isNonqueuedComm rp))
      (hasDataFilter nqrc))))

; TPS_SWCT_01225 - Queue length consistency for RunnableEntity
(declare-sort ClientServerOperation 0)

(declare-sort OperationInvokedEvent 0)

(declare-fun queueLength (ClientServerOperation) Int)
(declare-fun referencedBy (RunnableEntity) OperationInvokedEvent)
(declare-fun invokesOperation (OperationInvokedEvent) ClientServerOperation)

(assert (forall ((re RunnableEntity) (op1 ClientServerOperation) (op2 ClientServerOperation)
                 (ev1 OperationInvokedEvent) (ev2 OperationInvokedEvent))
  (=> (and (= (referencedBy re) ev1)
           (= (referencedBy re) ev2)
           (= (invokesOperation ev1) op1)
           (= (invokesOperation ev2) op2)
           (not (= op1 op2)))
      (= (queueLength op1) (queueLength op2)))))

; TPS_SWCT_01226 - initValue priority constraint
(declare-sort ParameterDataPrototype 0)
(declare-sort ParameterProvideComSpec 0)
(declare-sort ParameterRequireComSpec 0)
(declare-sort PortPrototypeParam 0)
(declare-sort InitValue 0)

(declare-fun initValueProto (ParameterDataPrototype) InitValue)
(declare-fun initValueProvide (ParameterProvideComSpec) InitValue)
(declare-fun initValueRequire (ParameterRequireComSpec) InitValue)
(declare-fun relevantInitValue (PortPrototypeParam) InitValue)
(declare-fun hasProvideComSpec (PortPrototypeParam ParameterProvideComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun hasRequireComSpec (PortPrototypeParam ParameterRequireComSpec) Bool)

(assert (forall ((pp PortPrototypeParam) (ppc ParameterProvideComSpec))
  (=> (hasProvideComSpec pp ppc)
      (= (relevantInitValue pp) (initValueProvide ppc)))))

(assert (forall ((pp PortPrototypeParam) (prc ParameterRequireComSpec))
  (=> (hasRequireComSpec pp prc)
      (= (relevantInitValue pp) (initValueRequire prc)))))

; TPS_SWCT_01249 - shortName uniqueness within ApplicationRecordDataType
(declare-sort ApplicationRecordDataType 0)
(declare-sort ApplicationRecordElement 0)

(declare-fun g_defultShortName (ApplicationRecordElement) String)
(declare-fun belongsTo (ApplicationRecordElement ApplicationRecordDataType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((ardt ApplicationRecordDataType) (e1 ApplicationRecordElement) (e2 ApplicationRecordElement))
  (=> (and (belongsTo e1 ardt)
           (belongsTo e2 ardt)
           (= (g_defultShortName e1) (g_defultShortName e2)))
      (= e1 e2))))



; TPS_SWCT_01307 - supportsMultipleInstantiation vs canBeInvokedConcurrently



(declare-fun canBeInvokedConcurrently (RunnableEntity) Bool)
(declare-fun supportsMultipleInstantiation (SwcInternalBehavior) Bool)
(declare-fun behaviorContains (SwcInternalBehavior RunnableEntity) Bool)

(assert (forall ((swcib SwcInternalBehavior) (re RunnableEntity))
  (=> (behaviorContains swcib re)
      (or
        (and (supportsMultipleInstantiation swcib) 
             (canBeInvokedConcurrently re))
        (and (supportsMultipleInstantiation swcib) 
             (not (canBeInvokedConcurrently re)))
        (and (not (supportsMultipleInstantiation swcib)) 
             (canBeInvokedConcurrently re))
        (and (not (supportsMultipleInstantiation swcib)) 
             (not (canBeInvokedConcurrently re)))))))

; TPS_SWCT_01312 - RunnableEntity mapping to BswModuleEntry
(declare-sort BswModuleEntry 0)
(declare-sort RunnableEntityArgument 0)
(declare-sort SwServiceArg 0)

(declare-fun hasMappingToBsw (RunnableEntity BswModuleEntry) Bool)
(declare-fun hasArgument (RunnableEntity RunnableEntityArgument) Bool)
(declare-fun hasSwServiceArg (BswModuleEntry SwServiceArg) Bool)
(declare-fun shouldIgnoreArgument (RunnableEntityArgument) Bool)

(assert (forall ((re RunnableEntity) (bme BswModuleEntry) (rea RunnableEntityArgument))
  (=> (and (hasMappingToBsw re bme) (hasArgument re rea))
      (shouldIgnoreArgument rea))))

; TPS_SWCT_01324 - Mode switches need finite time
(declare-sort WaitPoint 0)
(declare-sort ModeSwitch 0)

(declare-fun hasWaitPoint (RunnableEntity WaitPoint) Bool)
(declare-fun involvedInModeSwitch (RunnableEntity ModeSwitch) Bool)
(declare-fun canGuaranteeFiniteTime (RunnableEntity) Bool)

(assert (forall ((re RunnableEntity) (wp WaitPoint))
  (=> (hasWaitPoint re wp)
      (not (canGuaranteeFiniteTime re)))))

(assert (forall ((re RunnableEntity) (ms ModeSwitch))
  (=> (involvedInModeSwitch re ms)
      (canGuaranteeFiniteTime re))))

; TPS_SWCT_01325 - Read/write access only for category 1 RunnableEntitys
(declare-sort VariableAccess 0)

(declare-fun category (RunnableEntity) String)
(declare-fun hasDataReadAccess (RunnableEntity VariableAccess) Bool)
(declare-fun hasDataWriteAccess (RunnableEntity VariableAccess) Bool)

(assert (forall ((re RunnableEntity) (va VariableAccess))
  (=> (or (hasDataReadAccess re va) (hasDataWriteAccess re va))
      (= (category re) "1"))))

; TPS_SWCT_01331 - dataSendPoint implies category 1B
(declare-fun hasDataSendPoint (RunnableEntity VariableAccess) Bool)

(assert (forall ((re RunnableEntity) (va VariableAccess))
  (=> (hasDataSendPoint re va)
      (= (category re) "1B"))))

; TPS_SWCT_01334 - Category 1 with dataReceivePoints becomes 1B
(declare-fun hasDataReceivePointByValue (RunnableEntity VariableAccess) Bool)
(declare-fun hasDataReceivePointByArgument (RunnableEntity VariableAccess) Bool)

(assert (forall ((re RunnableEntity) (va VariableAccess))
  (=> (and (= (category re) "1")
           (or (hasDataReceivePointByValue re va)
               (hasDataReceivePointByArgument re va)))
      (= (category re) "1B"))))

; TPS_SWCT_01335 - DataReceivedEvent with WaitPoint restriction
(declare-sort DataReceivedEvent 0)

(declare-fun isNonQueuedCommunication (VariableAccess) Bool)
(declare-fun dataReceivedEventUsedWith (DataReceivedEvent WaitPoint) Bool)
(declare-fun waitPointInRunnable (WaitPoint RunnableEntity) Bool)
(declare-fun dataReceivePointInRunnable (VariableAccess RunnableEntity) Bool)

(assert (forall ((dre DataReceivedEvent) (wp WaitPoint) (re RunnableEntity) (va VariableAccess))
  (=> (and (isNonQueuedCommunication va)
           (dataReceivedEventUsedWith dre wp)
           (waitPointInRunnable wp re)
           (dataReceivePointInRunnable va re))
      false)))

(assert (forall ((re RunnableEntity) (va VariableAccess) (wp WaitPoint))
  (=> (and (or (hasDataReceivePointByValue re va)
               (hasDataReceivePointByArgument re va))
           (hasWaitPoint re wp))
      (or (not (isNonQueuedCommunication va))
          (exists ((dre DataReceivedEvent)) 
                  (not (dataReceivedEventUsedWith dre wp)))))))



; TPS_SWCT_01370 - VariationPointProxy post-build constraint
(declare-sort VariationPointProxy 0)
(declare-sort PostBuildVariantCriterion 0)
(declare-sort PostBuildVariantCondition 0)

(declare-fun bindingTime (VariationPointProxy) String)
(declare-fun hasPostBuildValueAccess (VariationPointProxy PostBuildVariantCriterion) Bool)
(declare-fun hasPostBuildVariantCondition (VariationPointProxy PostBuildVariantCondition) Bool)

(assert (forall ((vpp VariationPointProxy))
  (=> (= (bindingTime vpp) "postBuild")
      (and 
        (exists ((pbvc PostBuildVariantCriterion)) 
                (hasPostBuildValueAccess vpp pbvc))
        (exists ((pbvco PostBuildVariantCondition)) 
                (hasPostBuildVariantCondition vpp pbvco))))))

; TPS_SWCT_01374 - AutosarParameterRef implementation

(declare-sort AutosarDataTypeParam 0)
(declare-sort ParameterInAtomicSWCTypeInstanceRef 0)
(declare-sort ParameterDataPrototype 0)

(declare-fun autosarParameterIref (AutosarParameterRef) ParameterInAtomicSWCTypeInstanceRef)
(declare-fun dataTypeOfParameter (ParameterDataPrototype) AutosarDataTypeParam)
(declare-fun isCompositeDataType (AutosarDataTypeParam) Bool)
(declare-fun isPrimitiveDataType (AutosarDataTypeParam) Bool)
(declare-fun hasRootParameterRef (ParameterInAtomicSWCTypeInstanceRef) Bool)
(declare-fun hasTargetDataPrototypeRef (ParameterInAtomicSWCTypeInstanceRef) Bool)
(declare-fun rootParameterDataPrototype (ParameterInAtomicSWCTypeInstanceRef) ParameterDataPrototype)

(assert (forall ((dt AutosarDataTypeParam))
  (=> (isCompositeDataType dt)
      (not (isPrimitiveDataType dt)))))

(assert (forall ((apr AutosarParameterRef) (piref ParameterInAtomicSWCTypeInstanceRef) 
                 (pdp ParameterDataPrototype))
  (=> (and (= (autosarParameterIref apr) piref)
           (= (rootParameterDataPrototype piref) pdp)
           (isCompositeDataType (dataTypeOfParameter pdp)))
      (hasRootParameterRef piref))))

(assert (forall ((apr AutosarParameterRef) (piref ParameterInAtomicSWCTypeInstanceRef) 
                 (pdp ParameterDataPrototype))
  (=> (and (= (autosarParameterIref apr) piref)
           (= (rootParameterDataPrototype piref) pdp)
           (isPrimitiveDataType (dataTypeOfParameter pdp)))
      (and (hasTargetDataPrototypeRef piref)
           (not (hasRootParameterRef piref))))))

(assert (forall ((piref ParameterInAtomicSWCTypeInstanceRef))
  (hasTargetDataPrototypeRef piref)))

; TPS_SWCT_01375 - AutosarVariableRef implementation
(declare-sort AutosarVariableRef 0)
(declare-sort AutosarDataTypeVar 0)
(declare-sort VariableInAtomicSWCTypeInstanceRef 0)
(declare-sort VariableDataPrototype 0)

(declare-fun autosarVariableIref (AutosarVariableRef) VariableInAtomicSWCTypeInstanceRef)
(declare-fun dataTypeOfVariable (VariableDataPrototype) AutosarDataTypeVar)
(declare-fun isCompositeDataTypeVar (AutosarDataTypeVar) Bool)
(declare-fun isPrimitiveDataTypeVar (AutosarDataTypeVar) Bool)
(declare-fun hasRootVariableRef (VariableInAtomicSWCTypeInstanceRef) Bool)
(declare-fun hasTargetDataPrototypeRefVar (VariableInAtomicSWCTypeInstanceRef) Bool)
(declare-fun rootVariableDataPrototype (VariableInAtomicSWCTypeInstanceRef) VariableDataPrototype)

(assert (forall ((dt AutosarDataTypeVar))
  (=> (isCompositeDataTypeVar dt)
      (not (isPrimitiveDataTypeVar dt)))))

(assert (forall ((avr AutosarVariableRef) (viref VariableInAtomicSWCTypeInstanceRef) 
                 (vdp VariableDataPrototype))
  (=> (and (= (autosarVariableIref avr) viref)
           (= (rootVariableDataPrototype viref) vdp)
           (isCompositeDataTypeVar (dataTypeOfVariable vdp)))
      (hasRootVariableRef viref))))

(assert (forall ((avr AutosarVariableRef) (viref VariableInAtomicSWCTypeInstanceRef) 
                 (vdp VariableDataPrototype))
  (=> (and (= (autosarVariableIref avr) viref)
           (= (rootVariableDataPrototype viref) vdp)
           (isPrimitiveDataTypeVar (dataTypeOfVariable vdp)))
      (and (hasTargetDataPrototypeRefVar viref)
           (not (hasRootVariableRef viref))))))

(assert (forall ((viref VariableInAtomicSWCTypeInstanceRef))
  (hasTargetDataPrototypeRefVar viref)))




; TPS_SWCT_01431 - CompuScale symbol priority selection
(declare-sort CompuScale 0)
(declare-sort CompuConstTextContent 0)

(declare-fun symbol (CompuScale) String)
(declare-fun shortLabel (CompuScale) String)
(declare-fun vt (CompuConstTextContent) String)
(declare-fun hasSymbol (CompuScale) Bool)
(declare-fun hasShortLabel (CompuScale) Bool)
(declare-fun hasVt (CompuConstTextContent) Bool)
(declare-fun isValidCIdentifier (String) Bool)
(declare-fun selectedSymbolName (CompuScale) String)
(declare-fun textContent (CompuScale) CompuConstTextContent)

(assert (forall ((cs CompuScale))
  (=> (hasSymbol cs)
      (= (selectedSymbolName cs) (symbol cs)))))

(assert (forall ((cs CompuScale))
  (=> (and (not (hasSymbol cs))
           (hasVt (textContent cs))
           (isValidCIdentifier (vt (textContent cs))))
      (= (selectedSymbolName cs) (vt (textContent cs))))))

(assert (forall ((cs CompuScale))
  (=> (and (not (hasSymbol cs))
           (or (not (hasVt (textContent cs)))
               (not (isValidCIdentifier (vt (textContent cs)))))
           (hasShortLabel cs))
      (= (selectedSymbolName cs) (shortLabel cs)))))

; TPS_SWCT_01442/01443 - TYPE_REFERENCE property inheritance
(declare-sort ImplementationDataType 0)
(declare-sort SwDataDefProps 0)

(declare-fun g_defultCategory2 (ImplementationDataType) String)
(declare-fun refinedType (ImplementationDataType) ImplementationDataType)
(declare-fun ownProperties (ImplementationDataType) SwDataDefProps)
(declare-fun effectiveProperties (ImplementationDataType) SwDataDefProps)
(declare-fun hasOwnProperties (ImplementationDataType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((idt ImplementationDataType))
  (=> (and (= (g_defultCategory2 idt) "TYPE_REFERENCE")
           (not (hasOwnProperties idt)))
      (= (effectiveProperties idt) 
         (effectiveProperties (refinedType idt))))))

(assert (forall ((idt ImplementationDataType))
  (=> (and (= (g_defultCategory2 idt) "TYPE_REFERENCE")
           (hasOwnProperties idt))
      (= (effectiveProperties idt) 
         (ownProperties idt)))))

; TPS_SWCT_01445 - SwDataDefProps applicability
(declare-sort AutosarDataType 0)


(declare-fun categoryDataType (AutosarDataType) String)
(declare-fun categoryPrototype (DataPrototype) String)
(declare-fun swDataDefPropsApplicable (DataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun typedBy (DataPrototype) AutosarDataType)

(assert (forall ((dp DataPrototype) (adt AutosarDataType))
  (=> (= (typedBy dp) adt)
      (= (swDataDefPropsApplicable dp)
         (swDataDefPropsApplicable dp)))))

; TPS_SWCT_01448 - VariationPointProxy category constraints
(declare-sort VariationPointProxy 0)
(declare-sort ConditionByFormula 0)
(declare-sort PostBuildVariantCriterion 0)
(declare-sort PostBuildVariantCondition 0)
(declare-sort ValueAccess 0)

(declare-fun categoryVPP (VariationPointProxy) String)
(declare-fun conditionAccess (VariationPointProxy) ConditionByFormula)
(declare-fun postBuildValueAccess (VariationPointProxy) PostBuildVariantCriterion)
(declare-fun valueAccess (VariationPointProxy) ValueAccess)
(declare-fun hasConditionAccess (VariationPointProxy) Bool)
(declare-fun g_defultHasPostBuildValueAccess (VariationPointProxy) Bool)
(declare-fun hasValueAccess (VariationPointProxy) Bool)
(declare-fun yieldsInteger (ValueAccess) Bool)
(declare-fun yieldsBoolean (ConditionByFormula) Bool)

(assert (forall ((vpp VariationPointProxy))
  (=> (= (categoryVPP vpp) "VALUE")
      (or (and (hasValueAccess vpp) (yieldsInteger (valueAccess vpp)))
          (g_defultHasPostBuildValueAccess vpp)))))

(assert (forall ((vpp VariationPointProxy))
  (=> (= (categoryVPP vpp) "CONDITION")
      (or (and (hasConditionAccess vpp) 
               (yieldsBoolean (conditionAccess vpp)))
          (exists ((pbvc PostBuildVariantCondition)) 
                  (hasPostBuildVariantCondition vpp pbvc))))))

; TPS_SWCT_01458 - ExclusiveArea coverage by NestingOrders
(declare-sort ExclusiveAreaNestingOrder 0)
(declare-sort InternalBehavior 0)

(declare-fun exclusiveAreaInBehavior (ExclusiveArea InternalBehavior) Bool)
(declare-fun referencedByNestingOrder (ExclusiveArea ExclusiveAreaNestingOrder) Bool)
(declare-fun nestingOrderInBehavior (ExclusiveAreaNestingOrder InternalBehavior) Bool)
(declare-fun lockingFullyDescribed (InternalBehavior) Bool)

(assert (forall ((ib InternalBehavior))
  (= (lockingFullyDescribed ib)
     (forall ((ea ExclusiveArea))
       (=> (exclusiveAreaInBehavior ea ib)
           (exists ((eano ExclusiveAreaNestingOrder))
                   (and (nestingOrderInBehavior eano ib)
                        (referencedByNestingOrder ea eano))))))))

; TPS_SWCT_01459 - Incomplete locking description
(assert (forall ((ib InternalBehavior))
  (=> (exists ((ea ExclusiveArea))
              (and (exclusiveAreaInBehavior ea ib)
                   (not (exists ((eano ExclusiveAreaNestingOrder))
                                (and (nestingOrderInBehavior eano ib)
                                     (referencedByNestingOrder ea eano))))))
      (not (lockingFullyDescribed ib)))))



; TPS_SWCT_01501 - Calibration access precedence (2 levels)
(declare-sort SwDataDefPropsContent 0)
(declare-sort ApplicationPrimitiveDataType 0)

(declare-fun swCalibrationAccessDirect (SwDataDefPropsContent) String)
(declare-fun valueAxisDataType (SwDataDefPropsContent) ApplicationPrimitiveDataType)
(declare-fun swDataDefPropsOfType (ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) SwDataDefPropsContent)
(declare-fun swCalibrationAccessInferred (ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) String)
(declare-fun effectiveCalibrationAccess (SwDataDefPropsContent) String)
(declare-fun hasDirectCalibrationAccess (SwDataDefPropsContent) Bool)
(declare-fun hasValueAxisDataType (SwDataDefPropsContent) Bool)

(assert (forall ((sdp SwDataDefPropsContent))
  (=> (hasDirectCalibrationAccess sdp)
      (= (effectiveCalibrationAccess sdp) 
         (swCalibrationAccessDirect sdp)))))

(assert (forall ((sdp SwDataDefPropsContent))
  (=> (and (not (hasDirectCalibrationAccess sdp))
           (hasValueAxisDataType sdp))
      (= (effectiveCalibrationAccess sdp)
         (swCalibrationAccessInferred (valueAxisDataType sdp))))))

; TPS_SWCT_01502 - Unit precedence (5 levels)

(declare-sort Unit 0)
(declare-sort CompuMethod 0)
(declare-sort AutosarVariable 0)

(declare-fun unitDirect (SwAxisIndividual) Unit)
(declare-fun compuMethodOfAxis (SwAxisIndividual) CompuMethod)
(declare-fun unitOfCompuMethod (CompuMethod) Unit)
(declare-fun inputVariableType (SwAxisIndividual) ApplicationPrimitiveDataType)
(declare-fun swVariableRef (SwAxisIndividual) AutosarVariable)
(declare-fun typeOfVariable (AutosarVariable) ApplicationPrimitiveDataType)
(declare-fun effectiveUnit (SwAxisIndividual) Unit)
(declare-fun hasUnitDirect (SwAxisIndividual) Bool)
(declare-fun hasCompuMethod (SwAxisIndividual) Bool)
(declare-fun hasInputVariableType (SwAxisIndividual) Bool)
(declare-fun hasSwVariableRef (SwAxisIndividual) Bool)

(assert (forall ((sai SwAxisIndividual))
  (=> (hasUnitDirect sai)
      (= (effectiveUnit sai) (unitDirect sai)))))

(assert (forall ((sai SwAxisIndividual))
  (=> (and (not (hasUnitDirect sai))
           (hasCompuMethod sai))
      (= (effectiveUnit sai) 
         (unitOfCompuMethod (compuMethodOfAxis sai))))))

; TPS_SWCT_01503 - DataConstr precedence (3 levels)
(declare-sort DataConstr 0)

(declare-fun dataConstrDirect (SwAxisIndividual) DataConstr)
(declare-fun dataConstrOfInputType (ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) DataConstr)
(declare-fun effectiveDataConstr (SwAxisIndividual) DataConstr)
(declare-fun hasDataConstrDirect (SwAxisIndividual) Bool)

(assert (forall ((sai SwAxisIndividual))
  (=> (hasDataConstrDirect sai)
      (= (effectiveDataConstr sai) (dataConstrDirect sai)))))

(assert (forall ((sai SwAxisIndividual))
  (=> (and (not (hasDataConstrDirect sai))
           (hasInputVariableType sai))
      (= (effectiveDataConstr sai)
         (dataConstrOfInputType (inputVariableType sai))))))

; TPS_SWCT_01506 - Input axis calibration access precedence (2 levels)
(declare-sort SwCalprmAxis 0)

(declare-fun swCalibrationAccessProps (SwDataDefPropsContent) String)
(declare-fun swCalibrationAccessAxis (SwCalprmAxis) String)
(declare-fun effectiveAxisCalibrationAccess (SwCalprmAxis) String)
(declare-fun hasCalibrationAccessProps (SwDataDefPropsContent) Bool)
(declare-fun hasCalibrationAccessAxis (SwCalprmAxis) Bool)
(declare-fun propsForAxis (SwCalprmAxis) SwDataDefPropsContent)

(assert (forall ((sca SwCalprmAxis))
  (=> (hasCalibrationAccessProps (propsForAxis sca))
      (= (effectiveAxisCalibrationAccess sca)
         (swCalibrationAccessProps (propsForAxis sca))))))

(assert (forall ((sca SwCalprmAxis))
  (=> (and (not (hasCalibrationAccessProps (propsForAxis sca)))
           (hasCalibrationAccessAxis sca))
      (= (effectiveAxisCalibrationAccess sca)
         (swCalibrationAccessAxis sca)))))

; TPS_SWCT_01520 - ClientServerOperation compatibility
(declare-sort ClientServerOperation 0)
(declare-sort ApplicationError 0)

(declare-fun hasPossibleError (ClientServerOperation) Bool)
(declare-fun compatibleOperations (ClientServerOperation ClientServerOperation) Bool)

(assert (forall ((op1 ClientServerOperation) (op2 ClientServerOperation))
  (=> (and (hasPossibleError op1) (not (hasPossibleError op2)))
      (not (compatibleOperations op1 op2)))))

(assert (forall ((op1 ClientServerOperation) (op2 ClientServerOperation))
  (=> (and (not (hasPossibleError op1)) (hasPossibleError op2))
      (not (compatibleOperations op1 op2)))))

; TPS_SWCT_01522 - Inter-runnable variable initialization

(declare-sort VariableDataPrototype 0)

(declare-fun readsVariable (RunnableEntity VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun writesVariable (RunnableEntity VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun hasInitValue (VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun isInterRunnableVariable (VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun behaviorUndefined (RunnableEntity VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun executionOrder (RunnableEntity RunnableEntity) Bool)

(assert (forall ((re1 RunnableEntity) (vdp VariableDataPrototype))
  (=> (and (isInterRunnableVariable vdp)
           (not (hasInitValue vdp))
           (readsVariable re1 vdp)
           (not (exists ((re2 RunnableEntity))
                        (and (writesVariable re2 vdp)
                             (executionOrder re2 re1)))))
      (behaviorUndefined re1 vdp))))

; TPS_SWCT_01529 - EndToEndDescription default value
(declare-sort EndToEndDescription 0)

(declare-fun categoryE2E (EndToEndDescription) String)
(declare-fun dataIdMode (EndToEndDescription) Int)
(declare-fun dataIdNibbleOffset (EndToEndDescription) Int)
(declare-fun hasDataIdNibbleOffset (EndToEndDescription) Bool)
(declare-fun effectiveDataIdNibbleOffset (EndToEndDescription) Int)

(assert (forall ((e2e EndToEndDescription))
  (=> (and (= (dataIdMode e2e) 3)
           (= (categoryE2E e2e) "PROFILE_01")
           (not (hasDataIdNibbleOffset e2e)))
      (= (effectiveDataIdNibbleOffset e2e) 12))))

(assert (forall ((e2e EndToEndDescription))
  (=> (hasDataIdNibbleOffset e2e)
      (= (effectiveDataIdNibbleOffset e2e) 
         (dataIdNibbleOffset e2e)))))

      (hasDefaultMode meb))))

; TPS_SWCT_01572 - Application Mode Manager interaction rules
(declare-sort AbstractRequiredPortPrototype 0)
(declare-sort AbstractProvidedPortPrototype 0)
(declare-sort SenderReceiverInterface 0)
(declare-sort ModeSwitchInterface 0)
(declare-sort BswM 0)
(declare-sort ApplicationSwComponentType 0)

(declare-fun isServiceSRI (SenderReceiverInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun isServiceMSI (ModeSwitchInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun typedBySRI (AbstractRequiredPortPrototype) SenderReceiverInterface)
(declare-fun typedByMSI (AbstractProvidedPortPrototype) ModeSwitchInterface)
(declare-fun connectsToBswM (AbstractRequiredPortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun connectsToAppSwc (AbstractRequiredPortPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((arr AbstractRequiredPortPrototype))
  (=> (connectsToBswM arr)
      (isServiceSRI (typedBySRI arr)))))

(assert (forall ((arr AbstractRequiredPortPrototype))
  (=> (connectsToAppSwc arr)
      (not (isServiceSRI (typedBySRI arr))))))

(assert (forall ((app AbstractProvidedPortPrototype))
  (=> (exists ((bswm BswM) (appswc ApplicationSwComponentType))
              (and (connectsToBswM app) (connectsToAppSwc app)))
      (not (isServiceMSI (typedByMSI app))))))

; TPS_SWCT_01577/01578 - serviceRequestCallbackType value restrictions
(declare-sort DiagnosticCommunicationManagerNeeds 0)

(declare-fun serviceRequestCallbackType (DiagnosticCommunicationManagerNeeds) String)
(declare-fun isManufacturerContext (DiagnosticCommunicationManagerNeeds) Bool)
(declare-fun isSupplierContext (DiagnosticCommunicationManagerNeeds) Bool)

(assert (forall ((dcmn DiagnosticCommunicationManagerNeeds))
  (=> (isManufacturerContext dcmn)
      (= (serviceRequestCallbackType dcmn) "requestCallbackTypeManufacturer"))))

(assert (forall ((dcmn DiagnosticCommunicationManagerNeeds))
  (=> (isSupplierContext dcmn)
      (= (serviceRequestCallbackType dcmn) "requestCallbackTypeSupplier"))))

; TPS_SWCT_01585 - NvBlockDescriptor period precedence
