(declare-fun cyclicWritingPeriod (NvBlockNeeds) Int)
(declare-fun period (TimingEvent) Int)
(declare-fun effectiveWritingPeriod (NvBlockDescriptor) Int)
(declare-fun hasTimingEvent (NvBlockDescriptor) Bool)

(assert (forall ((nbd NvBlockDescriptor))
  (=> (hasTimingEvent nbd)
      (= (effectiveWritingPeriod nbd) 
         (period (timingEvent nbd))))))

; TPS_SWCT_01607 - dynamicArraySizeProfile valid values
(declare-sort ApplicationArrayDataType 0)

(declare-fun dynamicArraySizeProfile (ApplicationArrayDataType) String)
(declare-fun isValidProfile (String) Bool)

(assert (forall ((profile String))
  (= (isValidProfile profile)
     (or (= profile "VSA_LINEAR")
         (= profile "VSA_SQUARE")
         (= profile "VSA_RECTANGULAR")
         (= profile "VSA_FULLY_FLEXIBLE")))))

(assert (forall ((aadt ApplicationArrayDataType))
  (=> (not (= (dynamicArraySizeProfile aadt) ""))
      (or (isValidProfile (dynamicArraySizeProfile aadt))
          (exists ((prefix String))
                  (and (str.prefixof prefix (dynamicArraySizeProfile aadt))
                       (not (isValidProfile prefix))))))))

; TPS_SWCT_01608 - Custom profiles require company prefix
(declare-fun hasCompanyPrefix (String) Bool)

(assert (forall ((aadt ApplicationArrayDataType))
  (=> (and (not (= (dynamicArraySizeProfile aadt) ""))
           (not (isValidProfile (dynamicArraySizeProfile aadt))))
      (hasCompanyPrefix (dynamicArraySizeProfile aadt)))))



; TPS_SWCT_01648 - VSA_RECTANGULAR structure constraints
(declare-sort ImplementationDataType 0)
(declare-sort ImplementationDataTypeElement 0)

(declare-fun g_defultDynamicArraySizeProfile (ImplementationDataType) String)
(declare-fun subElements (ImplementationDataType) (Array Int ImplementationDataTypeElement))
(declare-fun g_defultCategory (ImplementationDataTypeElement) String)
(declare-fun arraySize (ImplementationDataTypeElement) Int)
(declare-fun numSubElements (ImplementationDataType) Int)
(declare-fun rectangularDimensionSize (ImplementationDataType) Int)

(assert (forall ((idt ImplementationDataType))
  (=> (and (= (g_defultDynamicArraySizeProfile idt) "VSA_RECTANGULAR")
           (> (numSubElements idt) 0))
      (and (= (g_defultCategory (select (subElements idt) 0)) "ARRAY")
           (= (arraySize (select (subElements idt) 0))
              (rectangularDimensionSize idt))))))

; TPS_SWCT_01649 - Second ARRAY element requirement
(assert (forall ((idt ImplementationDataType))
  (=> (not (= (g_defultDynamicArraySizeProfile idt) ""))
      (and (>= (numSubElements idt) 2)
           (= (g_defultCategory (select (subElements idt) 1)) "ARRAY")))))

; TPS_SWCT_01650 - VSA structure (Size Indicator + Payload)
(declare-fun isVSASizeIndicator (ImplementationDataTypeElement) Bool)
(declare-fun isVSAPayload (ImplementationDataTypeElement) Bool)

(assert (forall ((idt ImplementationDataType))
  (=> (not (= (g_defultDynamicArraySizeProfile idt) ""))
      (and (= (numSubElements idt) 2)
           (isVSASizeIndicator (select (subElements idt) 0))
           (isVSAPayload (select (subElements idt) 1))))))

; TPS_SWCT_01653 - UTF-16 BOM restriction
(declare-sort BaseTypeDirectDefinition 0)


(declare-fun baseTypeEncoding (BaseTypeDirectDefinition) String)
(declare-fun dataValue (DataPrototype) String)
(declare-fun startsWithBOM (String) Bool)
(declare-fun baseTypeDefOf (DataPrototype) BaseTypeDirectDefinition)

(assert (forall ((dp DataPrototype))
  (=> (= (baseTypeEncoding (baseTypeDefOf dp)) "UTF-16")
      (not (startsWithBOM (dataValue dp))))))

; TPS_SWCT_01656/57/58 - IOControl naming conventions
(declare-sort PortInterface 0)
(declare-sort RPortPrototype 0)
(declare-sort PPortPrototype 0)
(declare-sort SwcServiceDependency 0)
(declare-sort RoleBasedPortAssignment 0)

(declare-fun shortNamePI (PortInterface) String)
(declare-fun shortNameRPort (RPortPrototype) String)
(declare-fun shortNamePPort (PPortPrototype) String)
(declare-fun shortNameSvcDep (SwcServiceDependency) String)
(declare-fun role (RoleBasedPortAssignment) String)
(declare-fun referencesRPort (RoleBasedPortAssignment) RPortPrototype)
(declare-fun referencesPPort (RoleBasedPortAssignment) PPortPrototype)
(declare-fun serviceDependencyContext (RoleBasedPortAssignment) SwcServiceDependency)

(assert (forall ((rbpa RoleBasedPortAssignment) (rport RPortPrototype))
  (=> (and (= (role rbpa) "IOControlRequest")
           (= (referencesRPort rbpa) rport))
      (= (shortNameRPort rport)
         (str.++ "IOControlRequest_" 
                 (shortNameSvcDep (serviceDependencyContext rbpa)))))))

(assert (forall ((rbpa RoleBasedPortAssignment) (pport PPortPrototype))
  (=> (and (= (role rbpa) "IOControlResponse")
           (= (referencesPPort rbpa) pport))
      (= (shortNamePPort pport)
         (str.++ "IOControlResponse_" 
                 (shortNameSvcDep (serviceDependencyContext rbpa)))))))

; TPS_SWCT_01659 - NvBlockDescriptor mapping alternatives
(declare-sort AutosarVariableRef 0)
(declare-sort NvBlockDescriptor 0)
(declare-sort VariableDataPrototype 0)
(declare-sort NvDataInterface 0)

(declare-fun hasAutosarVariable (AutosarVariableRef) Bool)
(declare-fun hasAutosarVariableInImplDatatype (AutosarVariableRef) Bool)
(declare-fun targetDataPrototype (AutosarVariableRef) VariableDataPrototype)
(declare-fun ramBlock (NvBlockDescriptor) VariableDataPrototype)
(declare-fun nvData (NvDataInterface) VariableDataPrototype)
(declare-fun isLeafElement (VariableDataPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((avr AutosarVariableRef) (nvi NvDataInterface))
  (or 
    ; Direct complete mapping
    (and (hasAutosarVariable avr)
         (= (targetDataPrototype avr) (nvData nvi)))
    ; Individual leaf element mapping
    (and (or (hasAutosarVariableInImplDatatype avr)
             (hasAutosarVariable avr))
         (isLeafElement (targetDataPrototype avr)))
    ; Sub-element mapping (not leaf)
    (and (or (hasAutosarVariableInImplDatatype avr)
             (hasAutosarVariable avr))
         (not (isLeafElement (targetDataPrototype avr)))))))

; TPS_SWCT_02003 - Port offering rules for diagnostic read/write
(declare-sort AtomicSwComponentType 0)
(declare-sort AbstractProvidedPortPrototype 0)
(declare-sort AbstractRequiredPortPrototype 0)

(declare-fun requiresReadSignal (AtomicSwComponentType) Bool)
(declare-fun requiresWriteSignal (AtomicSwComponentType) Bool)
(declare-fun hasProvidedPort (AtomicSwComponentType AbstractProvidedPortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun hasRequiredPort (AtomicSwComponentType AbstractRequiredPortPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((asc AtomicSwComponentType))
  (=> (requiresReadSignal asc)
      (exists ((app AbstractProvidedPortPrototype))
              (hasProvidedPort asc app)))))

(assert (forall ((asc AtomicSwComponentType))
  (=> (requiresWriteSignal asc)
      (exists ((arr AbstractRequiredPortPrototype))
              (hasRequiredPort asc arr)))))

; TPS_SWCT_02014 - ROE port role differentiation
(declare-fun isServerPort (RPortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun isClientPort (RPortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun portRole (RPortPrototype) String)

(assert (forall ((rport RPortPrototype))
  (=> (isServerPort rport)
      (= (portRole rport) "ROEServices"))))

(assert (forall ((rport RPortPrototype))
  (=> (isClientPort rport)
      (= (portRole rport) "Dcm_Roe"))))



; TPS_SWCT_02047 - rptHook algorithm reference alternatives
(declare-sort RptHook 0)

(declare-fun hasRptArHook (RptHook) Bool)
(declare-fun hasCodeLabel (RptHook) Bool)
(declare-fun hasMcdIdentifier (RptHook) Bool)

(assert (forall ((rh RptHook))
  (or 
    ; AUTOSAR SW Component algorithm
    (hasRptArHook rh)
    ; Non-AUTOSAR algorithm with codeLabel
    (and (hasCodeLabel rh)
         (or (hasMcdIdentifier rh) (not (hasMcdIdentifier rh)))))))

; TPS_SWCT_02500 - Port role matching between Application and Service
(declare-sort RPortPrototype 0)
(declare-sort PPortPrototype 0)
(declare-sort ApplicationSwComponentType 0)
(declare-sort ServiceSwComponentType 0)

(declare-fun hasRPortApp (ApplicationSwComponentType RPortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun hasPPortSvc (ServiceSwComponentType PPortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun matchesInterface (RPortPrototype PPortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun g_defultConnected (RPortPrototype PPortPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((asc ApplicationSwComponentType) (ssc ServiceSwComponentType)
                 (rport RPortPrototype) (pport PPortPrototype))
  (=> (and (hasRPortApp asc rport)
           (hasPPortSvc ssc pport)
           (matchesInterface rport pport))
      (g_defultConnected rport pport))))

; TPS_SWCT_02501 - NVM Permanent RAM Block role requirements
(declare-sort RoleBasedDataAssignment 0)
(declare-sort RoleBasedPortAssignment 0)
(declare-sort PerInstanceMemory 0)
(declare-sort VariableDataPrototype 0)
(declare-sort ParameterDataPrototype 0)

(declare-fun roleData (RoleBasedDataAssignment) String)
(declare-fun rolePort (RoleBasedPortAssignment) String)
(declare-fun usedPim (RoleBasedDataAssignment) PerInstanceMemory)
(declare-fun usedDataElement (RoleBasedDataAssignment) VariableDataPrototype)
(declare-fun usedParameterElement (RoleBasedDataAssignment) ParameterDataPrototype)
(declare-fun hasUsedPim (RoleBasedDataAssignment) Bool)
(declare-fun hasUsedDataElement (RoleBasedDataAssignment) Bool)
(declare-fun hasUsedParameterElement (RoleBasedDataAssignment) Bool)

; ramBlock role is mandatory and must reference either usedPim or usedDataElement
(assert (forall ((rbda RoleBasedDataAssignment))
  (=> (= (roleData rbda) "ramBlock")
      (or (hasUsedPim rbda) (hasUsedDataElement rbda)))))

; defaultValue role is optional and references usedParameterElement
(assert (forall ((rbda RoleBasedDataAssignment))
  (=> (= (roleData rbda) "defaultValue")
      (hasUsedParameterElement rbda))))

; Port roles for NVM services
(define-fun validNvmPortRole ((role String)) Bool
  (or (= role "NvmService")
      (= role "NvMNotifyJobFinished")
      (= role "NvMNotifyInitBlock")
      (= role "NvMAdmin")))

; TPS_SWCT_02502 - Temporary RAM Block data type assignment
(declare-sort RoleBasedDataTypeAssignment 0)

(declare-fun roleDataType (RoleBasedDataTypeAssignment) String)
(declare-fun usedImplementationDataType (RoleBasedDataTypeAssignment) String)

(assert (forall ((rbdta RoleBasedDataTypeAssignment))
  (=> (= (roleDataType rbdta) "temporaryRamBlock")
      (not (= (usedImplementationDataType rbdta) "")))))

; TPS_SWCT_02504 - Mirror Interface parameter references


(declare-fun perInstanceParameter (SwcInternalBehavior) ParameterDataPrototype)
(declare-fun sharedParameter (SwcInternalBehavior) ParameterDataPrototype)
(declare-fun hasPerInstanceParameter (SwcInternalBehavior) Bool)
(declare-fun hasSharedParameter (SwcInternalBehavior) Bool)

(assert (forall ((rbda RoleBasedDataAssignment) (sib SwcInternalBehavior))
  (=> (and (= (roleData rbda) "defaultValue")
           (hasUsedParameterElement rbda))
      (or (hasPerInstanceParameter sib)
          (hasSharedParameter sib)))))

; Table_11_7 - NvBlockNeeds CRC recommendation
(declare-sort NvBlockDescriptor 0)



(declare-fun nvBlockNeeds (NvBlockDescriptor) NvBlockNeeds)
(declare-fun calcRamBlockCrc (NvBlockNeeds) Bool)
(declare-fun checkStaticBlockId (NvBlockNeeds) Bool)
(declare-fun anyPortRequestsCRC (NvBlockDescriptor) Bool)
(declare-fun anyPortRequestsStaticBlockIdCheck (NvBlockDescriptor) Bool)

(assert (forall ((nbd NvBlockDescriptor))
  (=> (anyPortRequestsCRC nbd)
      (calcRamBlockCrc (nvBlockNeeds nbd)))))

(assert (forall ((nbd NvBlockDescriptor))
  (=> (anyPortRequestsStaticBlockIdCheck nbd)
      (checkStaticBlockId (nvBlockNeeds nbd)))))

; TPS_SWCT_02048/49 - RptContainer structure for implicit selection
(declare-sort RptContainer 0)
(declare-sort SwComponentPrototype 0)


(declare-fun hasSubRptContainer (RptContainer) Bool)
(declare-fun byPassPointReferencesSwComponent (RptContainer SwComponentPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun byPassPointReferencesRunnable (RptContainer RunnableEntity) Bool)
(declare-fun allRTEInterfacesSupported (SwComponentPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun allRunnableInterfacesSupported (RunnableEntity) Bool)

(assert (forall ((rc RptContainer) (scp SwComponentPrototype))
  (=> (and (byPassPointReferencesSwComponent rc scp)
           (not (hasSubRptContainer rc)))
      (allRTEInterfacesSupported scp))))

(assert (forall ((rc RptContainer) (re RunnableEntity))
  (=> (and (byPassPointReferencesRunnable rc re)
           (not (hasSubRptContainer rc)))
      (allRunnableInterfacesSupported re))))




; --- Sort Declarations ---
(declare-sort TextTableMapping)
(declare-sort TextTableValuePair)

(declare-sort MappingDirectionEnum)
(declare-sort ControlPort)
(declare-sort PortGroup)


; --- TPS_SWCT_01165: Invertible mapping ---
; "This requires that the list of all firstValues and the list of all secondValues do not contain identical values inside a list."
(declare-const bidirectional MappingDirectionEnum)
(declare-fun get-mapping-direction (TextTableMapping) MappingDirectionEnum)
(declare-fun get-value-pairs (TextTableMapping) (Set TextTableValuePair))
(declare-fun get-first-value (TextTableValuePair) Value)
(declare-fun get-second-value (TextTableValuePair) Value)

(assert (forall ((ttm TextTableMapping))
  ; IF mappingDirection is bidirectional
  (=> (= (get-mapping-direction ttm) bidirectional)
      (let ((pairs (get-value-pairs ttm)))
        (and
          ; THEN: all firstValues in the set must be unique
          (forall ((p1 TextTableValuePair) (p2 TextTableValuePair))
            (=> (and (set.member p1 pairs)
                     (set.member p2 pairs)
                     (= (get-first-value p1) (get-first-value p2)))
                (= p1 p2)
            )
          )
          ; AND: all secondValues in the set must be unique
          (forall ((p1 TextTableValuePair) (p2 TextTableValuePair))
            (=> (and (set.member p1 pairs)
                     (set.member p2 pairs)
                     (= (get-second-value p1) (get-second-value p2)))
                (= p1 p2)
            )
          )
        )
      )
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01172: Requesting and releasing partial networks (Choice) ---
; "interfaced in two alternative (i.e. either one or the other) ways"
(declare-fun is-ComM-based (ControlPort) Bool)
(declare-fun is-BswM-based (ControlPort) Bool)
; A control port must use exactly one strategy (XOR)
(assert (forall ((cp ControlPort))
  (xor (is-ComM-based cp) (is-BswM-based cp))
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01173: Control port shall not become a part of the PortGroup ---
(declare-fun get-ports (PortGroup) (Set PortPrototype))
; Identifies if p is the control port associated with pg (via SwcServiceDependency)
(declare-fun is-control-port-for (PortPrototype PortGroup) Bool)

(assert (forall ((pg PortGroup) (p PortPrototype))
  ; IF p is the control port for pg
  (=> (is-control-port-for p pg)
      ; THEN p must NOT be a member of the ports in pg
      (not (set.member p (get-ports pg)))
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01174: Status port shall not become a member of the PortGroup ---
(declare-fun is-status-port-for (PortPrototype PortGroup) Bool)
(assert (forall ((pg PortGroup) (p PortPrototype))
  ; IF p is the status port for pg
  (=> (is-status-port-for p pg)
      ; THEN p must NOT be a member of the ports in pg
      (not (set.member p (get-ports pg)))
  )
))



; --- Sort Declarations ---
(declare-sort DataReceiveErrorEvent)


(declare-sort SwImplPolicyEnum)
(declare-sort ServerCallPoint)

(declare-sort TimeValue)
(declare-sort AsynchronousServerCallReturnsEvent)
(declare-sort AtomicSwComponentType 0)


; --- TPS_SWCT_01340: DataReceiveErrorEvent cannot be combined with a WaitPoint ---
(declare-const queued SwImplPolicyEnum)
(declare-fun get-data-ref (DataReceiveErrorEvent) VariableDataPrototype)
(declare-fun get-sw-impl-policy (VariableDataPrototype) SwImplPolicyEnum)
(declare-fun is-associated-with-waitpoint (DataReceiveErrorEvent) Bool)

; Assertion 1: A DataReceiveErrorEvent cannot be associated with a WaitPoint.
(assert (forall ((e DataReceiveErrorEvent))
  (=> (is-associated-with-waitpoint e) false)
))

; Assertion 2: Its data reference must be to a VariableDataPrototype whose swImplPolicy is not 'queued'.
(assert (forall ((e DataReceiveErrorEvent))
  (let ((dp (get-data-ref e)))
    (not (= (get-sw-impl-policy dp) queued))
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01344: Consistency of values of timeout ---
(declare-fun get-referenced-operation (ServerCallPoint) ClientServerOperation)
(declare-fun get-timeout (ServerCallPoint) TimeValue)

; Assertion: If two ServerCallPoints reference the same operation, their timeouts must be equal.
(assert (forall ((scp1 ServerCallPoint) (scp2 ServerCallPoint))
  (=>
    (= (get-referenced-operation scp1) (get-referenced-operation scp2))
    (= (get-timeout scp1) (get-timeout scp2))
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01347: Blocking access to operation result in an asynchronous operation invocation ---
(declare-fun get-event-ref (WaitPoint) AsynchronousServerCallReturnsEvent)
(declare-fun has-start-on-event (AsynchronousServerCallReturnsEvent) Bool)

; Assertion: If an AsynchronousServerCallReturnsEvent is referenced by a WaitPoint (implying a block),
; that event must not define a startOnEvent reference.
(assert (forall ((wp WaitPoint))
  (let ((evt (get-event-ref wp)))
    (=> (has-start-on-event evt) false)
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01359: Private memory per instance ---
(declare-fun get-behavior (AtomicSwComponentType) SwcInternalBehavior)
(declare-fun get-supports-multiple-inst (SwcInternalBehavior) Bool)
(declare-fun has-private-memory (AtomicSwComponentType) Bool)

; Assertion: If an AtomicSwComponentType supports multiple instantiation, it must have private memory.
(assert (forall ((swc AtomicSwComponentType))
  (let ((beh (get-behavior swc)))
    (=>
      (= (get-supports-multiple-inst beh) true)
      (= (has-private-memory swc) true)
    )
  )
))



; --- Sort Declarations ---
(declare-sort Service)



(declare-sort SwcImplementation)
(declare-sort EcuInstance)

; --- Function Declarations ---
(declare-fun get-type (SwComponentPrototype) SwComponentType)
(declare-fun is-ServiceSwComponentType (SwComponentType) Bool)
(declare-fun is-ApplicationSwComponentType (SwComponentType) Bool)
(declare-fun get-service (SwComponentType) Service) ; Assumes ServiceSwComponentType maps to a Service
(declare-fun get-behavior-ref (SwcImplementation) SwcInternalBehavior)
(declare-fun get-mapping (SwComponentPrototype) EcuInstance)
(declare-fun communicates-with (SwComponentPrototype SwComponentPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun has-rport-GeneralDiagnosticInfo (SwComponentPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun has-needs-DiagnosticEventInfo (SwComponentPrototype) Bool)

; --- TPS_SWCT_01406: Per Service exactly one SwComponentPrototype typed by a ServiceSwComponentType ---
(assert (forall ((p1 SwComponentPrototype) (p2 SwComponentPrototype))
  (let ((t1 (get-type p1)) (t2 (get-type p2)))
    ; IF p1 and p2 are typed by ServiceSwComponentTypes
    ; AND those types map to the same Service
    (=> (and (is-ServiceSwComponentType t1)
             (is-ServiceSwComponentType t2)
             (= (get-service t1) (get-service t2)))
        ; THEN p1 and p2 must be the same instance
        (= p1 p2)
    )
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01408: For each SwcInternalBehavior one SwcImplementation is being created (1-to-1) ---
; At least one Implementation exists for each Behavior
(assert (forall ((b SwcInternalBehavior))
  (exists ((i SwcImplementation))
    (= (get-behavior-ref i) b)
  )
))
; At most one Implementation exists for each Behavior (uniqueness)
(assert (forall ((i1 SwcImplementation) (i2 SwcImplementation))
  (=> (= (get-behavior-ref i1) (get-behavior-ref i2))
      (= i1 i2)
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01412: exactly one SwComponentPrototype per ServiceSwComponentType per ECU ---
(assert (forall ((p1 SwComponentPrototype) (p2 SwComponentPrototype))
  (let ((t1 (get-type p1)) (t2 (get-type p2)))
    ; IF p1 and p2 are typed by the *same* ServiceSwComponentType
    ; AND p1 and p2 are mapped to the *same* ECU
    (=> (and (is-ServiceSwComponentType t1)
             (is-ServiceSwComponentType t2)
             (= t1 t2)
             (= (get-mapping p1) (get-mapping p2)))
        ; THEN p1 and p2 must be the same instance
        (= p1 p2)
    )
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01413: Application software components ... communicate ... only locally on an ECU ---
(assert (forall ((p1 SwComponentPrototype) (p2 SwComponentPrototype))
  (let ((t1 (get-type p1)) (t2 (get-type p2)))
    ; IF p1 is an ApplicationSWC
    ; AND p2 is a ServiceSWC
    ; AND p1 communicates with p2
    (=> (and (is-ApplicationSwComponentType t1)
             (is-ServiceSwComponentType t2)
             (communicates-with p1 p2))
        ; THEN their ECU mappings must be identical
        (= (get-mapping p1) (get-mapping p2))
    )
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01426: If ... (needs) GeneralDiagnosticInfo ... It shall also specify DiagnosticEventInfoNeeds ---
(assert (forall ((p SwComponentPrototype))
  ; IF a prototype has the GeneralDiagnosticInfo RPort
  (=> (has-rport-GeneralDiagnosticInfo p)
      ; THEN it must also have the DiagnosticEventInfoNeeds
      (has-needs-DiagnosticEventInfo p)
  )
))




; --- Sorts ---




(declare-sort ConsistencyNeeds)
(declare-sort Time) ; Using Int for Age/Time

(declare-sort ExecutionStep)


(declare-sort Array)
(declare-sort CompoundPrimitiveDataType)





; --- Functions (Coherence/Stability) ---
(declare-fun dpg-requires-coherence (SwComponentType DataPrototypeGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun reg-requires-stability (SwComponentType RunnableEntityGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun sending-dpg-requires-coherence (SwComponentType DataPrototypeGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun dpg-member (VariableDataPrototype DataPrototypeGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun reg-member (RunnableEntity RunnableEntityGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun implicit-read (RunnableEntity VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun get-age (VariableDataPrototype) Int)
(declare-fun get-value-at-step (VariableDataPrototype ExecutionStep) Value)
(declare-fun steps-in-group-execution (ExecutionStep RunnableEntityGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun get-propagation-time (VariableDataPrototype) Int)
(declare-fun in-same-group (RunnableEntity RunnableEntity RunnableEntityGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun produces (RunnableEntity VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun consumes (RunnableEntity VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun update-is-immediate (VariableDataPrototype RunnableEntity RunnableEntity) Bool)
(declare-fun applies-to-runnable (ConsistencyNeeds) RunnableEntity)
(declare-fun uses-implicit-comm (RunnableEntity) Bool)

; --- Functions (RuleBasedValueSpec) ---
(declare-fun get-rule (RuleBasedValueSpecification) String)
(declare-fun get-args (RuleBasedValueSpecification) RuleArguments)
(declare-fun get-target-array (RuleBasedValueSpecification) Array)
(declare-fun nth-arg (RuleArguments Int) Value)
(declare-fun nth-element (Array Int) Value)
(declare-fun last-arg (RuleArguments) Value)
(declare-fun arg-count (RuleArguments) Int)
(declare-fun array-size (Array) Int)

; --- Functions (InvalidValue) ---
(declare-fun has-invalid-value (CompoundPrimitiveDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun is-APDT (CompoundPrimitiveDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun get-category (CompoundPrimitiveDataType) String)
(declare-fun get-invalid-value-apdt (ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) Value)
(declare-fun get-invalid-value-idt (ImplementationDataType) Value)
(declare-fun is-compatible (ApplicationPrimitiveDataType ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun values-correspond (Value Value) Bool)

; --- Functions (AppError) ---
(declare-fun get-owner-interface (ApplicationError) PortInterface)
(declare-fun is-ClientServerInterface (PortInterface) Bool)


; --- TPS_SWCT_01472: dpgRequiresCoherence (Same Age) ---
(assert (forall ((swc SwComponentType) (dpg DataPrototypeGroup) (vdp1 VariableDataPrototype) (vdp2 VariableDataPrototype))
  (=> (and (dpg-requires-coherence swc dpg) (dpg-member vdp1 dpg) (dpg-member vdp2 dpg))
      (= (get-age vdp1) (get-age vdp2))
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01473: regRequiresStability (Stable Value) ---
(assert (forall ((swc SwComponentType) (reg RunnableEntityGroup) (re RunnableEntity) (vdp VariableDataPrototype) (s1 ExecutionStep) (s2 ExecutionStep))
  (=> (and (reg-requires-stability swc reg) (reg-member re reg) (implicit-read re vdp) (steps-in-group-execution s1 reg) (steps-in-group-execution s2 reg))
      (= (get-value-at-step vdp s1) (get-value-at-step vdp s2))
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01474: Coherence and Stability ---
(assert (forall ((swc SwComponentType) (reg RunnableEntityGroup) (dpg DataPrototypeGroup) (vdp1 VariableDataPrototype) (vdp2 VariableDataPrototype) (re RunnableEntity) (s1 ExecutionStep) (s2 ExecutionStep))
  (=> (and (reg-requires-stability swc reg)
           (dpg-requires-coherence swc dpg)
           (dpg-member vdp1 dpg) (dpg-member vdp2 dpg)
           (reg-member re reg) (implicit-read re vdp1)
           (steps-in-group-execution s1 reg) (steps-in-group-execution s2 reg))
      (and
        (= (get-age vdp1) (get-age vdp2))
        (= (get-value-at-step vdp1 s1) (get-value-at-step vdp1 s2))
      )
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01475: Sending dpgRequiresCoherence (Same Propagation Time) ---
(assert (forall ((swc SwComponentType) (dpg DataPrototypeGroup) (vdp1 VariableDataPrototype) (vdp2 VariableDataPrototype))
  (=> (and (sending-dpg-requires-coherence swc dpg) (dpg-member vdp1 dpg) (dpg-member vdp2 dpg))
      (= (get-propagation-time vdp1) (get-propagation-time vdp2))
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01476: Sender/Receiver in same group (Immediate Visibility) ---
(assert (forall ((vdp VariableDataPrototype) (re_prod RunnableEntity) (re_cons RunnableEntity) (reg RunnableEntityGroup))
  (=> (and (in-same-group re_prod re_cons reg) (produces re_prod vdp) (consumes re_cons vdp))
      (update-is-immediate vdp re_prod re_cons)
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01479: Applicability of ConsistencyNeeds ---
(assert (forall ((cn ConsistencyNeeds))
  (uses-implicit-comm (applies-to-runnable cn))
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01485/01493: RuleArguments Order ---
; 01485: Order corresponds
(assert (forall ((r RuleBasedValueSpecification) (i Int))
  (let ((args (get-args r)) (arr (get-target-array r)))
    (=> (and (>= i 0) (< i (arg-count args)) (< i (array-size arr)))
        (= (nth-element arr i) (nth-arg args i))
    )
  )
))
; 01493: Ignore extra args (This is implicit in the bounded quantification of 01485)

; --- TPS_SWCT_01494: Rule FILL_UNTIL_END ---
(assert (forall ((r RuleBasedValueSpecification))
  (let ((args (get-args r)) (arr (get-target-array r)) (n_args (arg-count args)) (n_arr (array-size arr)))
    (=> (and (= (get-rule r) "FILL_UNTIL_END") (< n_args n_arr) (> n_args 0))
        (let ((last_val (last-arg args)))
          (forall ((i Int))
            (=> (and (>= i n_args) (< i n_arr))
                (= (nth-element arr i) last_val)
            )
          )
        )
    )
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01486: InvalidValue restriction ---
(assert (forall ((cpdt CompoundPrimitiveDataType))
  (=> (has-invalid-value cpdt)
      (and (is-APDT cpdt) (= (get-category cpdt) "STRING"))
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01487: InvalidValue Correspondence ---
(assert (forall ((apdt ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) (idt ImplementationDataType))
  (=> (is-compatible apdt idt)
      (values-correspond (get-invalid-value-apdt apdt) (get-invalid-value-idt idt))
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01490: ApplicationErrors only for ClientServerInterfaces ---
(assert (forall ((ae ApplicationError))
  (is-ClientServerInterface (get-owner-interface ae))
))




; --- Sorts ---



(declare-sort ModeUser)
(declare-sort ModeManager)

(declare-sort PortInterfaceMapping)


(declare-sort ARPackage)

(declare-sort DataWriteCompletedEvent)
(declare-sort RteEvent)

(declare-sort SwCalibrationAccessEnum)



; --- Functions ---
(declare-fun get-initial-mode (ModeDeclarationGroup) ModeDeclaration)
(declare-fun get-mode-user-error-behavior (ModeDeclarationGroup) ModeErrorBehavior)
(declare-fun has-mode-user-error-behavior (ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun get-current-mode (ModeUser) ModeDeclaration)
(declare-fun is-mode-user-out-of-sync (ModeUser ModeManager) Bool)
(declare-fun get-connected-users (ModeManager) (Set ModeUser))
(declare-fun get-mode-manager (ModeUser) ModeManager)
(declare-fun get-error-behavior (ModeUser) ModeErrorBehavior)
(declare-fun has-port-interface-mapping (SwConnector) Bool)
(declare-fun get-role (RoleBasedPortAssignment) String)
(declare-fun get-applicable-interface (RoleBasedPortAssignment) PortInterface)
(declare-fun get-shortName (PortInterface) String)
(declare-fun get-package (PortInterface) ARPackage)
(declare-fun g_gefultGet-category (ARPackage) String)
(declare-fun get-shortName-pkg (ARPackage) String)
(declare-fun get-parent-package (ARPackage) ARPackage)
(declare-fun is-top-level-package (ARPackage) Bool)
(declare-fun get-trigger (WaitPoint) RteEvent)
(declare-fun is-DataWriteCompletedEvent (RteEvent) Bool)
(declare-fun get-swCalibrationAccess (SwDataDefProps) SwCalibrationAccessEnum)
(declare-fun has-swCalibrationAccess (SwDataDefProps) Bool)
(declare-const notAccessible SwCalibrationAccessEnum)
(declare-fun get-category-compu (CompuMethod) String)
(declare-fun get-compu-method (AutosarDataType) CompuMethod)
(declare-fun is-composite (AutosarDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun get-leaf-elements (AutosarDataType) (Set AutosarDataType))
(declare-fun conversion-applies (AutosarDataType) Bool)

; --- TPS_SWCT_01534: Default error behavior ---
(assert (forall ((mdg ModeDeclarationGroup) (mu ModeUser))
  (let ((mm (get-mode-manager mu)))
    ; IF modeUserErrorBehavior is NOT defined
    ; AND the mode user gets out of sync
    (=> (and (not (has-mode-user-error-behavior mdg))
             (is-mode-user-out-of-sync mu mm))
        ; THEN the current mode of the user becomes the initialMode of the group
        (= (get-current-mode mu) (get-initial-mode mdg))
    )
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01536: Coherent error behavior ---
(assert (forall ((mm ModeManager) (u1 ModeUser) (u2 ModeUser) (c1 SwConnector) (c2 SwConnector))
  ; IF u1 and u2 are connected to the same manager mm
  ; AND their connectors (c1, c2) use PortInterfaceMappings
  (=> (and (set.member u1 (get-connected-users mm))
           (set.member u2 (get-connected-users mm))
           (has-port-interface-mapping c1) ; Simplified: assumes c1/c2 are connectors for u1/u2
           (has-port-interface-mapping c2))
      ; THEN their error behavior must be identical
      (= (get-error-behavior u1) (get-error-behavior u2))
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01556: Rule for setting RoleBasedPortAssignment.role ---
(declare-fun get-top-package (ARPackage) ARPackage)
(define-fun-rec get-top-package ((pkg ARPackage)) ARPackage
  (ite (is-top-level-package pkg)
       pkg
       (get-top-package (get-parent-package pkg))
  )
)

(assert (forall ((rbpa RoleBasedPortAssignment))
  (let ((pi (get-applicable-interface rbpa))
        (pkg (get-package pi))
        (top-pkg (get-top-package pkg)))
    ; IF the interface's package category is BLUEPRINT
    ; AND the top-most package shortName is AUTOSAR
    (=> (and (= (g_gefultGet-category pkg) "BLUEPRINT")
             (= (get-shortName-pkg top-pkg) "AUTOSAR"))
        ; THEN the role must equal the interface's shortName
        (= (get-role rbpa) (get-shortName pi))
    )
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01558: DataWriteCompletedEvent cannot be combined with a WaitPoint ---
(assert (forall ((wp WaitPoint))
  (not (is-DataWriteCompletedEvent (get-trigger wp)))
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01559: Default value for swCalibrationAccess ---
(assert (forall ((props SwDataDefProps))
  ; IF swCalibrationAccess is not explicitly defined
  (=> (not (has-swCalibrationAccess props))
      ; THEN its value is notAccessible
      (= (get-swCalibrationAccess props) notAccessible)
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01560: Supported CompuMethods for data conversion ---
(assert (forall ((adt AutosarDataType))
  (=> (conversion-applies adt)
      (let ((category (get-category-compu (get-compu-method adt))))
        (or (= category "LINEAR")
            (= category "IDENTICAL")
            (= category "SCALE_LINEAR_AND_TEXTTABLE")
            (= category "TEXTTABLE")
            (= category "BITFIELD_TEXTTABLE")
        )
      )
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_01561: Data conversion for composite AutosarDataTypes ---
(assert (forall ((adt_composite AutosarDataType))
  (=> (and (is-composite adt_composite) (conversion-applies adt_composite))
      (forall ((adt_leaf AutosarDataType))
        (=> (set.member adt_leaf (get-leaf-elements adt_composite))
            (conversion-applies adt_leaf)
        )
      )
  )
))

; --- Functions ---
(declare-fun get-service-needs (SwcServiceDependency) ServiceNeeds)
(declare-fun get-assigned-ports (SwcServiceDependency) (Set RoleBasedPortAssignment))
(declare-fun get-role (RoleBasedPortAssignment) String)
(declare-fun is-DiagnosticCommunicationSecurityNeeds (ServiceNeeds) Bool)
(declare-fun is-DiagnosticCommunicationManagerNeeds (ServiceNeeds) Bool)

; --- TPS_SWCT_02015: DiagnosticsCommunicationSecurityNeeds ---
(assert (forall ((dep SwcServiceDependency) (port RoleBasedPortAssignment))
  (=> (and (is-DiagnosticCommunicationSecurityNeeds (get-service-needs dep))
           (set.member port (get-assigned-ports dep)))
      ; RoleBasedPortAssignment valid roles: SecurityAccess [1]
      (and (= (get-role port) "SecurityAccess")
           ; Check for exactly 1 instance
           (forall ((port2 RoleBasedPortAssignment))
             (=> (and (set.member port2 (get-assigned-ports dep))
                      (= (get-role port2) "SecurityAccess"))
                 (= port port2)
             )
           )
      )
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_02016: DiagnosticCommunicationManagerNeeds ---
(assert (forall ((dep SwcServiceDependency) (port RoleBasedPortAssignment))
  (=> (and (is-DiagnosticCommunicationManagerNeeds (get-service-needs dep))
           (set.member port (get-assigned-ports dep)))
      ; RoleBasedPortAssignment valid roles: CallbackDCMRequestServices [1]
      (and (= (get-role port) "CallbackDCMRequestServices")
           ; Check for exactly 1 instance
           (forall ((port2 RoleBasedPortAssignment))
             (=> (and (set.member port2 (get-assigned-ports dep))
                      (= (get-role port2) "CallbackDCMRequestServices"))
                 (= port port2)
             )
           )
      )
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_02018: Supervised Entity ---

  ; Assuming 'dep' is the dependency for a Supervised Entity
  (let ((ports (get-assigned-ports dep)))
    (and
      ; WdgM_AliveSupervision [1]
      (exists ((p1 RoleBasedPortAssignment))
        (and (set.member p1 ports) (= (get-role p1) "WdgM_AliveSupervision"))
      )
      (forall ((p1 RoleBasedPortAssignment) (p2 RoleBasedPortAssignment))
        (=> (and (set.member p1 ports) (set.member p2 ports)
                 (= (get-role p1) "WdgM_AliveSupervision")
                 (= (get-role p2) "WdgM_AliveSupervision"))
            (= p1 p2)
        )
      )
      ; WdgM_IndividualMode [0..1]
      (forall ((p1 RoleBasedPortAssignment) (p2 RoleBasedPortAssignment))
        (=> (and (set.member p1 ports) (set.member p2 ports)
                 (= (get-role p1) "WdgM_IndividualMode")
                 (= (get-role p2) "WdgM_IndividualMode"))
            (= p1 p2)
        )
      )
    )
  )
))

; --- TPS_SWCT_02024: CsmRandomGenerate ---
(assert (forall ((dep SwcServiceDependency))
  ; Assuming 'dep' is the dependency for CsmRandomGenerate
  (let ((ports (get-assigned-ports dep)))
    (and
      ; CsmRandomGenerate [1]
      (exists ((p1 RoleBasedPortAssignment))
        (and (set.member p1 ports) (= (get-role p1) "CsmRandomGenerate"))
      )
      (forall ((p1 RoleBasedPortAssignment) (p2 RoleBasedPortAssignment))
        (=> (and (set.member p1 ports) (set.member p2 ports)
                 (= (get-role p1) "CsmRandomGenerate")
                 (= (get-role p2) "CsmRandomGenerate"))
            (= p1 p2)
        )
      )
      ; CsmCallback [1]
      (exists ((p1 RoleBasedPortAssignment))
        (and (set.member p1 ports) (= (get-role p1) "CsmCallback"))
      )
      (forall ((p1 RoleBasedPortAssignment) (p2 RoleBasedPortAssignment))
        (=> (and (set.member p1 ports) (set.member p2 ports)
                 (= (get-role p1) "CsmCallback")
                 (= (get-role p2) "CsmCallback"))
            (= p1 p2)
        )
      )
    )
  )
))




; === constr_1004 — Mapping of ApplicationDataTypes (uniqueness within atomic component) ===
(declare-sort ApplicationDataType 0)
(declare-sort ImplementationDataType 0)
(declare-sort AtomicSwComponent 0)
(declare-fun mappedImplsAC (ApplicationDataType AtomicSwComponent) (Set ImplementationDataType))
(assert
  (forall ((a ApplicationDataType) (c AtomicSwComponent) (i1 ImplementationDataType) (i2 ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (and (set.member i1 (mappedImplsAC a c))
             (set.member i2 (mappedImplsAC a c)))
        (= i1 i2))))

; === constr_1005 — Compatibility of ImplementationDataTypes mapped to the same ApplicationDataType ===
(declare-fun mappedImplsForApp (ApplicationDataType) (Set ImplementationDataType))
(declare-fun CompatibleImpl (ImplementationDataType ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((a ApplicationDataType) (i1 ImplementationDataType) (i2 ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (and (set.member i1 (mappedImplsForApp a))
             (set.member i2 (mappedImplsForApp a)))
        (CompatibleImpl i1 i2))))

; === constr_1010 — If nativeDeclaration does not exist... ===

(declare-fun baseTypeOf (ImplementationDataType) SwBaseType)
(declare-fun HasNativeDecl (SwBaseType) Bool)
(declare-fun ShortName (ImplementationDataType) String)
(declare-fun PredefinedPlatformOrStandardName (String) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((idt ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (not (HasNativeDecl (baseTypeOf idt)))
        (PredefinedPlatformOrStandardName (ShortName idt)))))

; === constr_1012 / constr_1013 — FIXED_LENGTH vs VARIABLE_LENGTH exclusivity and required attributes ===
(declare-fun IsFixedLength (SwBaseType) Bool)
(declare-fun IsVariableLength (SwBaseType) Bool)
(declare-fun HasBaseTypeSize (SwBaseType) Bool)
(declare-fun HasMaxBaseTypeSize (SwBaseType) Bool)
; exclusivity of category flags (optional but helpful)
(assert (forall ((t SwBaseType)) (=> (IsFixedLength t) (not (IsVariableLength t)))))
(assert (forall ((t SwBaseType)) (=> (IsVariableLength t) (not (IsFixedLength t)))))
; FIXED_LENGTH → baseTypeSize present, maxBaseTypeSize absent
(assert (forall ((t SwBaseType))
  (=> (IsFixedLength t)
      (and (HasBaseTypeSize t) (not (HasMaxBaseTypeSize t))))))
; VARIABLE_LENGTH → maxBaseTypeSize present, baseTypeSize absent
(assert (forall ((t SwBaseType))
  (=> (IsVariableLength t)
      (and (HasMaxBaseTypeSize t) (not (HasBaseTypeSize t))))))

; === constr_1015 — Prioritization of SwDataDefProps (abstract) ===
(declare-sort SwDataDefProps 0)
(declare-fun IsPrioritizedOK (SwDataDefProps) Bool)
(assert (forall ((p SwDataDefProps)) (IsPrioritizedOK p)))

; === constr_1017 — Supported combinations of swImplPolicy and swCalibrationAccess ===
(declare-sort SwDataDefPropsContent 0)
(declare-sort SwImplPolicy 0)
(declare-sort SwCalibrationAccess 0)
(declare-fun PolicyOf (SwDataDefPropsContent) SwImplPolicy)
(declare-fun CalibrationAccessOf (SwDataDefPropsContent) SwCalibrationAccess)
(declare-fun AllowedPolicyAccess (SwImplPolicy SwCalibrationAccess) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((d SwDataDefPropsContent))
    (AllowedPolicyAccess (PolicyOf d) (CalibrationAccessOf d))))

; === constr_1018 — measurementPoint not allowed for RunnableEntity.dataReadAccess VariableAccess ===

(declare-sort VariableAccess 0)

(declare-fun RoleDataReadAccess (VariableAccess) Bool)
(declare-fun TargetData (VariableAccess) DataElement)
(declare-fun IsMeasurementPoint (DataElement) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((va VariableAccess))
    (not (and (RoleDataReadAccess va) (IsMeasurementPoint (TargetData va))))))

; === constr_1021 — A CompuMethod shall specify instructions for both directions (or auto-invertible) ===
(declare-sort CompuMethod 0)
(declare-fun HasForwardDirection (CompuMethod) Bool)   ; COMPU-INTERNAL-TO-PHYS present
(declare-fun HasInverseDirection (CompuMethod) Bool)   ; COMPU-PHYS-TO-INTERNAL present
(declare-fun AutoInvertible (CompuMethod) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((cm CompuMethod))
    (or (and (HasForwardDirection cm) (HasInverseDirection cm))
        (AutoInvertible cm))))

; === constr_1022 — If both domains are specified then limits shall be explicitly defined ===
(declare-sort CompuScale 0)
(declare-fun DomainsSpecified (CompuMethod) Bool)
(declare-fun LimitsDefined (CompuMethod) Bool) ; both LOWER-LIMIT and UPPER-LIMIT exist for each direction
(assert
  (forall ((cm CompuMethod))
    (=> (DomainsSpecified cm) (LimitsDefined cm))))

; === constr_1024 — Only stepwise definition (CompuScales) is used ===
(declare-fun IsStepwise (CompuMethod) Bool)
(assert (forall ((cm CompuMethod)) (IsStepwise cm)))

; === constr_1025 — Avoid division by zero in rational formula ===
(declare-sort CompuRationalCoeffs 0)
(declare-fun NoZeroDenominator (CompuRationalCoeffs) Bool)
(assert (forall ((c CompuRationalCoeffs)) (NoZeroDenominator c)))

; === constr_1032 — DelegationSwConnector connects PortPrototypes of the same kind ===
(declare-sort DelegationSwConnector 0)

(declare-fun Src (DelegationSwConnector) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun Dst (DelegationSwConnector) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun IsPPort (PortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun IsRPort (PortPrototype) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((d DelegationSwConnector))
    (or (and (IsPPort (Src d)) (IsPPort (Dst d)))
        (and (IsRPort (Src d)) (IsRPort (Dst d))))))

; === constr_1033 — For sender/receiver, forbid n>1 and m>1 ===
(declare-sort CommunicationScenario 0)
(declare-sort Sender 0)
(declare-sort Receiver 0)
(declare-fun ConnectedSender (CommunicationScenario Sender) Bool)
(declare-fun ConnectedReceiver (CommunicationScenario Receiver) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((sc CommunicationScenario))
    (not (exists ((s1 Sender) (s2 Sender) (r1 Receiver) (r2 Receiver))
           (and (set.member s1 (as set.empty (Set Sender))) false ; helper to keep ALL logic — ignore
                (distinct s1 s2)
                (distinct r1 r2)
                (ConnectedSender sc s1)
                (ConnectedSender sc s2)
                (ConnectedReceiver sc r1)
                (ConnectedReceiver sc r2))))))




; === Sorts ===
(declare-sort AutosarDataType 0)

(declare-sort DataPrototypeMapping 0)

(declare-sort AssemblySwConnector 0)
(declare-sort DelegationSwConnector 0)
(declare-sort VariableAndParameterInterfaceMapping 0)
(declare-sort ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype 0)
(declare-sort ModeDeclarationGroup 0)
(declare-sort ModeInterfaceMapping 0)
(declare-sort ModeDeclarationGroupPrototypeMapping 0)
(declare-sort ClientServerOperation 0)
(declare-sort ClientServerInterface 0)
(declare-sort ClientServerOperationMapping 0)
(declare-sort ClientServerInterfaceMapping 0)
(declare-sort ArgumentDataPrototype 0)
(declare-sort ApplicationError 0)

; === Basic functions / predicates ===
(declare-fun TypedByDP (DataPrototype AutosarDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun CompatibleADT (AutosarDataType AutosarDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun ShortNameDP (DataPrototype) String)
(declare-fun SwImplPolicyQueued (DataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun IsVariable (DataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun IsParameter (DataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun IsPrimitiveOrImplValueBoolString (AutosarDataType) Bool)

(declare-fun FirstDP (DataPrototypeMapping) DataPrototype)
(declare-fun SecondDP (DataPrototypeMapping) DataPrototype)

(declare-fun RequiredPort (AssemblySwConnector) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun ProvidedPort (AssemblySwConnector) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun RequiredInnerPort (DelegationSwConnector) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun RequiredOuterPort (DelegationSwConnector) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun ProvidedInnerPort (DelegationSwConnector) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun ProvidedOuterPort (DelegationSwConnector) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun PIOf (PortPrototype) DataInterface)
(declare-fun IsServicePI (DataInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun MappingOf (SwConnector) VariableAndParameterInterfaceMapping)

(declare-fun ContainsDP (DataInterface DataPrototype) Bool)

; === constr_1068: compatibility for variable/parameter of primitive / Impl VALUE|BOOLEAN|STRING ===
(declare-fun CompatibleDP (DataPrototype DataPrototype) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((x DataPrototype) (y DataPrototype) (tx AutosarDataType) (ty AutosarDataType))
    (=> (and (or (IsVariable x) (IsParameter x))
             (or (IsVariable y) (IsParameter y))
             (TypedByDP x tx) (TypedByDP y ty)
             (or (IsPrimitiveOrImplValueBoolString tx)
                 (IsPrimitiveOrImplValueBoolString ty)))
        (= (CompatibleDP x y)
           (or (and (CompatibleADT tx ty)
                    (= (ShortNameDP x) (ShortNameDP y))
                    (= (SwImplPolicyQueued x) (SwImplPolicyQueued y)))
               (exists ((m DataPrototypeMapping))
                 (or (and (= x (FirstDP m)) (= y (SecondDP m)))
                     (and (= y (FirstDP m)) (= x (SecondDP m))))))))))

; === constr_1069: PortPrototypes of different DataInterfaces compatible (Assembly) ===
(declare-fun CompatiblePortsAssembly (PortPrototype PortPrototype) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((r PortPrototype) (p PortPrototype))
    (= (CompatiblePortsAssembly r p)
       (and (= (IsServicePI (PIOf r)) (IsServicePI (PIOf p)))
            (or
              ; pairwise match by shortName using CompatibleDP
              (forall ((d DataPrototype))
                (=> (ContainsDP (PIOf r) d)
                    (exists ((e DataPrototype))
                      (and (ContainsDP (PIOf p) e)
                           (= (ShortNameDP d) (ShortNameDP e))
                           (CompatibleDP d e)))))
              ; or via mapping referenced by connector
              (exists ((c AssemblySwConnector))
                (and (= r (RequiredPort c)) (= p (ProvidedPort c))
                     (exists ((map VariableAndParameterInterfaceMapping))
                       (= map (MappingOf c))))))))))

; === constr_1070: PortPrototypes compatibility in Delegation context ===
(declare-fun CompatiblePortsDelegation (PortPrototype PortPrototype) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((ri PortPrototype) (ro PortPrototype) (pi PortPrototype) (po PortPrototype))
    (= (CompatiblePortsDelegation ri ro)
       (and (= (IsServicePI (PIOf ri)) (IsServicePI (PIOf ro)))
            (or
              (forall ((d DataPrototype))
                (=> (ContainsDP (PIOf ri) d)
                    (exists ((e DataPrototype))
                      (and (ContainsDP (PIOf ro) e)
                           (= (ShortNameDP d) (ShortNameDP e))
                           (CompatibleDP d e)))))
              (exists ((c DelegationSwConnector))
                (and (= ri (RequiredInnerPort c)) (= ro (RequiredOuterPort c))
                     (exists ((map VariableAndParameterInterfaceMapping))
                       (= map (MappingOf c))))))))))

; === constr_1071: table-based allowed combinations (abstract) ===
(declare-fun AllowedCombination_Table61 (ParameterDataPrototype DataPrototype) Bool)
(assert (forall ((p ParameterDataPrototype) (v DataPrototype))
         (=> (AllowedCombination_Table61 p v) true)))

; === constr_1072/1073: ModeSwitchInterfaces compatibility requires mapping or one-to-one compatible mode groups + isService equality ===
(declare-fun MdgpOf (PortPrototype) ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype)
(declare-fun CompatibleMDGP (ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun ModeMappingOf (SwConnector) ModeInterfaceMapping)
(assert
  (forall ((r PortPrototype) (p PortPrototype))
    (=> true
      (and
        (=> (exists ((c AssemblySwConnector)) (and (= r (RequiredPort c)) (= p (ProvidedPort c))))
            (and (= (IsServicePI (PIOf r)) (IsServicePI (PIOf p)))
                 (or (CompatibleMDGP (MdgpOf r) (MdgpOf p))
                     (exists ((c AssemblySwConnector))
                       (and (= r (RequiredPort c)) (= p (ProvidedPort c))
                            (exists ((mm ModeInterfaceMapping)) (= mm (ModeMappingOf c)))))))))
        (=> (exists ((c DelegationSwConnector)) (and (= r (RequiredOuterPort c)) (= p (RequiredInnerPort c))))
            (and (= (IsServicePI (PIOf r)) (IsServicePI (PIOf p)))
                 (or (CompatibleMDGP (MdgpOf r) (MdgpOf p))
                     (exists ((c DelegationSwConnector))
                       (and (= r (RequiredOuterPort c)) (= p (RequiredInnerPort c))
                            (exists ((mm ModeInterfaceMapping)) (= mm (ModeMappingOf c))))))))))))

; === constr_1074: MDGPs compatible if typed groups compatible or a prototype mapping exists ===
(declare-fun GroupOf (ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype) ModeDeclarationGroup)
(declare-fun ExistsMDGPMapping (ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((a ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype) (b ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype))
    (= (CompatibleMDGP a b)
       (or (CompatibleADT (as (GroupOf a) AutosarDataType) (as (GroupOf b) AutosarDataType))
           (ExistsMDGPMapping a b)))))

; === constr_1075: ModeDeclarationGroups compatibility (abstract detailed checks) ===
(declare-fun NumModes (ModeDeclarationGroup) Int)
(declare-fun ModeByName (ModeDeclarationGroup String) Bool)
(declare-fun InitialModeName (ModeDeclarationGroup) String)
(declare-fun UserErrorPolicy (ModeDeclarationGroup) String)
(declare-fun ManagerErrorPolicy (ModeDeclarationGroup) String)
(declare-fun DefaultModeName_User (ModeDeclarationGroup) (Option String))
(declare-fun DefaultModeName_Manager (ModeDeclarationGroup) (Option String))
(declare-fun CategoryAlphabetic (ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun CategoryExplicit (ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun ModeValue (ModeDeclarationGroup String) Int)
(declare-fun OnTransitionValue (ModeDeclarationGroup) Int)
(declare-fun ModeDeclarationMappingApplied (ModeDeclarationGroup ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun CompatibleMDG (ModeDeclarationGroup ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((x ModeDeclarationGroup) (y ModeDeclarationGroup))
    (= (CompatibleMDG x y)
       (or
         (and (= (NumModes x) (NumModes y))
              (= (InitialModeName x) (InitialModeName y))
              (= (UserErrorPolicy x) (UserErrorPolicy y))
              (= (ManagerErrorPolicy x) (ManagerErrorPolicy y))
              (= (DefaultModeName_User x) (DefaultModeName_User y))
              (= (DefaultModeName_Manager x) (DefaultModeName_Manager y))
              (or (and (CategoryAlphabetic x) (CategoryAlphabetic y))
                  (and (CategoryExplicit x) (CategoryExplicit y)
                       (= (OnTransitionValue x) (OnTransitionValue y))
                       (forall ((n String)) (=> (ModeByName x n) (= (ModeValue x n) (ModeValue y n))))))
         )
         (ModeDeclarationMappingApplied x y)))))

; === constr_1076: ArgumentDataPrototypes compatible iff types compatible or mapped, and same direction ===
(declare-sort ArgumentDirection 0)
(declare-fun DirectionOf (ArgumentDataPrototype) ArgumentDirection)
(declare-fun ADPType (ArgumentDataPrototype) AutosarDataType)
(declare-fun ArgumentMapped (ClientServerOperationMapping ArgumentDataPrototype ArgumentDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun CompatibleADP (ArgumentDataPrototype ArgumentDataPrototype) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((a ArgumentDataPrototype) (b ArgumentDataPrototype))
    (= (CompatibleADP a b)
       (and (= (DirectionOf a) (DirectionOf b))
            (or (CompatibleADT (ADPType a) (ADPType b))
                (exists ((m ClientServerOperationMapping)) (ArgumentMapped m a b)))))))

; === constr_1077: ApplicationErrors compatible if same shortName & attributes (esp. errorCode) or mapped ===
(declare-fun AEName (ApplicationError) String)
(declare-fun AECode (ApplicationError) Int)
(declare-fun AEMapped (ClientServerInterfaceMapping ApplicationError ApplicationError) Bool)
(declare-fun CompatibleAE (ApplicationError ApplicationError) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((e1 ApplicationError) (e2 ApplicationError))
    (= (CompatibleAE e1 e2)
       (or (and (= (AEName e1) (AEName e2))
                (= (AECode e1) (AECode e2)))
           (exists ((m ClientServerInterfaceMapping)) (AEMapped m e1 e2))))))

; === constr_1078: ClientServerOperations compatible ===
(declare-fun OpName (ClientServerOperation) String)
(declare-fun ArgCount (ClientServerOperation) Int)
(declare-fun NthArg (ClientServerOperation Int) ArgumentDataPrototype)
(declare-fun PossibleErrors (ClientServerOperation ApplicationError) Bool)
(declare-fun RepresentsEOK (ApplicationError) Bool)
(declare-fun CompatibleCSO (ClientServerOperation ClientServerOperation) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((r ClientServerOperation) (p ClientServerOperation))
    (= (CompatibleCSO r p)
       (and (= (ArgCount r) (ArgCount p))
            (= (OpName r) (OpName p))
            (forall ((i Int))
              (=> (and (<= 0 i) (< i (ArgCount r)))
                  (CompatibleADP (NthArg r i) (NthArg p i))))
            (forall ((e ApplicationError))
              (=> (PossibleErrors p e)
                  (exists ((e2 ApplicationError))
                    (and (CompatibleAE e2 e)
                         (PossibleErrors r e2)))))
            ; E_OK permissive rule
            (=> (exists ((ex ApplicationError)) (and (PossibleErrors p ex) (RepresentsEOK ex)))
                true)))))

; === constr_1079/1080: ClientServerInterfaces compatibility with mapping or per-operation pairing + isService equality ===
(declare-fun CSIOps (ClientServerInterface ClientServerOperation) Bool)
(declare-fun CSIPort (PortPrototype) ClientServerInterface)
(declare-fun IsServiceCSI (ClientServerInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun OperationMappingOf (SwConnector) ClientServerOperationMapping)
(declare-fun CompatibleCSI (ClientServerInterface ClientServerInterface) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((ri ClientServerInterface) (pi ClientServerInterface))
    (= (CompatibleCSI ri pi)
       (and (= (IsServiceCSI ri) (IsServiceCSI pi))
            (or
              (forall ((op ClientServerOperation))
                (=> (CSIOps ri op)
                    (exists ((op2 ClientServerOperation))
                      (and (CSIOps pi op2) (CompatibleCSO op op2)))))
              (exists ((c SwConnector))
                (exists ((m ClientServerOperationMapping))
                  (= m (OperationMappingOf c)))))))))

; === constr_1081/1082/1083: TriggerInterfaces & Triggers compatibility ===
(declare-fun TriggerName (Trigger) String)
(declare-fun TITriggers (DataInterface Trigger) Bool)
(declare-fun CompatibleTrigger (Trigger Trigger) Bool)
(assert (forall ((t1 Trigger) (t2 Trigger))
         (= (CompatibleTrigger t1 t2) (= (TriggerName t1) (TriggerName t2)))))

; === constr_1084: Delegation requirements (abstract uniqueness/coverage) ===
(declare-fun ConnectsDP (DelegationSwConnector DataPrototype DataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun ConnectsMDGP (DelegationSwConnector ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun ConnectsCSO (DelegationSwConnector ClientServerOperation ClientServerOperation) Bool)
(declare-fun ConnectsTrigger (DelegationSwConnector Trigger Trigger) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((o PortPrototype) (i PortPrototype) (c DelegationSwConnector))
    (=> (= o (ProvidedOuterPort c))
        (and
          ; 1) at least one DP pair exists for SR/Nv/Parameter interfaces
          (exists ((d1 DataPrototype) (d2 DataPrototype))
            (and (ContainsDP (PIOf o) d1) (ContainsDP (PIOf i) d2)
                 (CompatibleDP d1 d2)
                 (ConnectsDP c d1 d2)))
          ; 3) exactly one MDGP connection
          (exists ((g1 ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype) (g2 ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype))
            (and (ConnectsMDGP c g1 g2)
                 (forall ((h1 ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype) (h2 ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype))
                   (=> (ConnectsMDGP c h1 h2) (and (= h1 g1) (= h2 g2))))))
          ; 4) exactly one CSO connection
          (exists ((s1 ClientServerOperation) (s2 ClientServerOperation))
            (and (ConnectsCSO c s1 s2)
                 (forall ((t1 ClientServerOperation) (t2 ClientServerOperation))
                   (=> (ConnectsCSO c t1 t2) (and (= t1 s1) (= t2 s2))))))
          ; 5) exactly one Trigger connection
          (exists ((tr1 Trigger) (tr2 Trigger))
            (and (ConnectsTrigger c tr1 tr2)
                 (forall ((u1 Trigger) (u2 Trigger))
                   (=> (ConnectsTrigger c u1 u2) (and (= u1 tr1) (= u2 tr2))))))))))

; === constr_1085: flat ECU extract — at least one compatible DP between R and provided ===
(declare-fun RPortOf (PortPrototype) PortPrototype)
(assert
  (forall ((r PortPrototype) (p PortPrototype))
    (=> true
      (exists ((d DataPrototype) (e DataPrototype))
        (and (ContainsDP (PIOf r) d) (ContainsDP (PIOf p) e)
             (= (ShortNameDP d) (ShortNameDP e))
             (CompatibleDP d e))))))

; === constr_1086: Each pair of PortPrototypes has at most one SwConnector ===
(declare-sort SwConnectorAny 0)
(declare-fun g_defultEndA (SwConnectorAny) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun g_defultEndB (SwConnectorAny) PortPrototype)
(assert
  (forall ((c1 SwConnectorAny) (c2 SwConnectorAny))
    (=> (and (= (g_defultEndA c1) (g_defultEndA c2)) (= (g_defultEndB c1) (g_defultEndB c2)))
        (= c1 c2))))

; === constr_1087: AssemblySwConnector connects components owned by same CompositionSwComponentType ===
(declare-fun Owner (SwComponentPrototype) CompositionSwComponentType)
(declare-fun SourceComp (AssemblySwConnector) SwComponentPrototype)
(declare-fun TargetComp (AssemblySwConnector) SwComponentPrototype)
(assert
  (forall ((c AssemblySwConnector))
    (= (Owner (SourceComp c)) (Owner (TargetComp c)))))

; === constr_1088: DelegationSwConnector connects ports owned by same Composition as delegation port ===
(declare-fun OwnerCompOfPort (PortPrototype) CompositionSwComponentType)
(declare-fun DelegationPortOwner (DelegationSwConnector) CompositionSwComponentType)
(declare-fun InnerPortOf (DelegationSwConnector) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun OuterPortOf (DelegationSwConnector) PortPrototype)
(assert
  (forall ((d DelegationSwConnector))
    (and (= (OwnerCompOfPort (InnerPortOf d)) (DelegationPortOwner d))
         (= (OwnerCompOfPort (OuterPortOf d)) (DelegationPortOwner d)))))

; === constr_1090: RunnableEntity may wait at at most one WaitPoint (if single-scheduled) ===
(declare-fun SingleScheduled (RunnableEntity) Bool)
(declare-fun WaitsAt (RunnableEntity WaitPoint) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((r RunnableEntity))
    (=> (SingleScheduled r)
        (forall ((w1 WaitPoint) (w2 WaitPoint))
          (=> (and (WaitsAt r w1) (WaitsAt r w2)) (= w1 w2))))))

; === constr_1091: Only certain RTEEvents can unblock a WaitPoint ===
(declare-fun TriggerOf (WaitPoint) RTEEvent)
(declare-fun IsDataReceivedEvent (RTEEvent) Bool)
(declare-fun IsDataSendCompletedEvent (RTEEvent) Bool)
(declare-fun IsModeSwitchedAckEvent (RTEEvent) Bool)
(declare-fun IsAsyncServerCallReturnsEvent (RTEEvent) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((w WaitPoint))
    (let ((e (TriggerOf w)))
      (or (IsDataReceivedEvent e)
          (IsDataSendCompletedEvent e)
          (IsModeSwitchedAckEvent e)
          (IsAsyncServerCallReturnsEvent e)))))

; === constr_1092: ParameterSwComponentType owns exclusively PPorts of ParameterInterface and no InternalBehavior ===
(declare-fun IsParameterSwComponentType (SwComponentType) Bool)
(declare-fun AggregatesSwcInternalBehavior (SwComponentType) Bool)
(declare-fun PortOfTypeParameterInterface (PortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun PortsOf (SwComponentType PortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun IsPPort (PortPrototype) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((t SwComponentType))
    (=> (IsParameterSwComponentType t)
        (and (not (AggregatesSwcInternalBehavior t))
             (forall ((p PortPrototype))
               (=> (PortsOf t p)
                   (and (IsPPort p) (PortOfTypeParameterInterface p))))))))

; === constr_1095: nDataSets > 0 ⇒ reliability ≠ errorCorrection ===
(declare-fun NDataSets (NvBlockNeeds) Int)
(declare-sort NvReliability 0)
(declare-fun Reliability (NvBlockNeeds) NvReliability)
(declare-fun ErrorCorrection () NvReliability)
(assert
  (forall ((n NvBlockNeeds))
    (=> (> (NDataSets n) 0)
        (not (= (Reliability n) (ErrorCorrection))))))

; === constr_1096: Runnable with WaitPoint shall not be referenced by a SwcModeSwitchEvent ===
(declare-sort SwcModeSwitchEvent 0)
(declare-fun RunnableOfEvent (SwcModeSwitchEvent) RunnableEntity)
(assert
  (forall ((e SwcModeSwitchEvent) (r RunnableEntity))
    (=> (= r (RunnableOfEvent e))
        (forall ((w WaitPoint))
          (not (WaitsAt r w))))))





; ===== Sorts reused / extended =====

(declare-sort WaitPoint 0)
(declare-sort RTEEvent 0)
(declare-sort SwcModeSwitchEvent 0)
(declare-sort ModeDeclaration 0)
(declare-sort RPortPrototype 0)
(declare-sort PPortPrototype 0)
(declare-sort DataInterface 0)
(declare-sort Trigger 0)
(declare-sort SwConnectorAny 0)
(declare-sort NonqueuedReceiverComSpec 0)
(declare-sort ReceiverComSpec 0)
(declare-sort VariableAccess 0)
(declare-sort VariableDataPrototype 0)
(declare-sort ImplementationDataType 0)
(declare-sort ImplementationDataTypeElement 0)
(declare-sort ApplicationError 0)
(declare-sort SensorActuatorSwComponentType 0)
(declare-sort SwComponentPrototype 0)
(declare-sort ECU 0)
(declare-sort HwElement 0)
(declare-sort HwType 0)
(declare-sort EndToEndDescription 0)

; ===== Core accessors / predicates =====
; (Some predicates were already declared in your previous block; redeclare if needed or assume the same signatures.)

; --- 1097: Runnable with WaitPoint must not be referenced by an RTEEvent that has disabledMode ---
(declare-fun WaitsAt (RunnableEntity WaitPoint) Bool)
(declare-fun StartOnEventRef (RTEEvent) (Option RunnableEntity))
(declare-fun HasDisabledMode (RTEEvent) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((e RTEEvent) (r RunnableEntity) (w WaitPoint))
    (=> (and (HasDisabledMode e)
             (= (StartOnEventRef e) (some r))
             (WaitsAt r w))
        false)))

; --- 1098: SwcModeSwitchEvent must not reference same ModeDeclaration in both roles ---
(declare-fun DisabledModeDecl (RTEEvent) (Option ModeDeclaration))
(declare-fun ModeDeclA (SwcModeSwitchEvent) (Option ModeDeclaration))
(declare-fun ModeDeclB (SwcModeSwitchEvent) (Option ModeDeclaration))
(assert
  (forall ((e SwcModeSwitchEvent))
    (let ((m1 (ModeDeclA e))
          (m2 (ModeDeclB e))
          (dm (DisabledModeDecl (as e RTEEvent))))
      (not (or (and (= m1 dm) (is-some m1) (is-some dm))
               (and (= m2 dm) (is-some m2) (is-some dm)))))))

; --- 1100: Unconnected RPort (typed by DataInterface) must have requiredComSpec.initValue for each DI element ---
(declare-fun TypedByDI (RPortPrototype DataInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun IsConnected (RPortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun DIElements (DataInterface VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun HasRequiredComSpecFor (RPortPrototype VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun NonqueuedInitValuePresent (RPortPrototype VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((rp RPortPrototype) (di DataInterface))
    (=> (and (TypedByDI rp di) (not (IsConnected rp)))
        (forall ((el VariableDataPrototype))
          (=> (DIElements di el)
              (and (HasRequiredComSpecFor rp el)
                   (NonqueuedInitValuePresent rp el)))))))

; --- 1101: RPort typed by ModeSwitchInterface referenced by at most one SwConnector ---
(declare-fun TypedByModeSwitch (RPortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun EndA (SwConnectorAny) RPortPrototype)
(declare-fun EndB (SwConnectorAny) PPortPrototype)
(assert
  (forall ((r RPortPrototype) (c1 SwConnectorAny) (c2 SwConnectorAny))
    (=> (and (TypedByModeSwitch r)
             (= r (EndA c1)) (= r (EndA c2)))
        (= c1 c2))))

; --- 1103: NonqueuedReceiverComSpec.enableUpdate = true ⇒ its dataElement not used as VariableAccess.dataReadAccess ---
(declare-fun EnableUpdate (NonqueuedReceiverComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun DataElementOf (NonqueuedReceiverComSpec) VariableDataPrototype)
(declare-fun DataReadAccesses (VariableAccess VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((cs NonqueuedReceiverComSpec) (va VariableAccess))
    (=> (EnableUpdate cs)
        (not (DataReadAccesses va (DataElementOf cs))))))

; --- 1104: One RPort->multiple PPorts via TriggerInterfaces with same Trigger shortName is forbidden ---
(declare-fun TypedByTriggerInterface (RPortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun PPortTypedByTriggerInterface (PPortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun TriggersOf (PPortPrototype Trigger) Bool)
(declare-fun TriggerName (Trigger) String)
(assert
  (forall ((r RPortPrototype) (c1 SwConnectorAny) (c2 SwConnectorAny)
           (p1 PPortPrototype) (p2 PPortPrototype) (t1 Trigger) (t2 Trigger))
    (=> (and (TypedByTriggerInterface r)
             (= r (EndA c1)) (= r (EndA c2))
             (= p1 (EndB c1)) (= p2 (EndB c2))
             (PPortTypedByTriggerInterface p1) (PPortTypedByTriggerInterface p2)
             (TriggersOf p1 t1) (TriggersOf p2 t2)
             (= (TriggerName t1) (TriggerName t2)))
        (= c1 c2))))

; --- 1105: arraySize > 0 when element (or owning type/element) has category ARRAY ---
(declare-fun CategoryARRAY_Type (ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun CategoryARRAY_Elem (ImplementationDataTypeElement) Bool)
(declare-fun OwnerTypeOf (ImplementationDataTypeElement) (Option ImplementationDataType))
(declare-fun ArraySize (ImplementationDataTypeElement) Int)
(assert
  (forall ((e ImplementationDataTypeElement))
    (=> (or (CategoryARRAY_Elem e)
            (and (is-some (OwnerTypeOf e))
                 (CategoryARRAY_Type (get (OwnerTypeOf e)))))
        (> (ArraySize e) 0))))

; --- 1106: STRUCTURE must have ≥1 subElement (for both type and element-as-structure) ---
(declare-fun CategorySTRUCTURE_Type (ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun CategorySTRUCTURE_Elem (ImplementationDataTypeElement) Bool)
(declare-fun SubElemOfType (ImplementationDataType ImplementationDataTypeElement) Bool)
(declare-fun SubElemOfElem (ImplementationDataTypeElement ImplementationDataTypeElement) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((t ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (CategorySTRUCTURE_Type t)
        (exists ((se ImplementationDataTypeElement)) (SubElemOfType t se)))))
(assert
  (forall ((e ImplementationDataTypeElement))
    (=> (CategorySTRUCTURE_Elem e)
        (exists ((se ImplementationDataTypeElement)) (SubElemOfElem e se)))))

; --- 1107: UNION must have ≥1 subElement (same pattern) ---
(declare-fun CategoryUNION_Type (ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun CategoryUNION_Elem (ImplementationDataTypeElement) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((t ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (CategoryUNION_Type t)
        (exists ((se ImplementationDataTypeElement)) (SubElemOfType t se)))))
(assert
  (forall ((e ImplementationDataTypeElement))
    (=> (CategoryUNION_Elem e)
        (exists ((se ImplementationDataTypeElement)) (SubElemOfElem e se)))))

; --- 1108: ApplicationError.errorCode in 1..63, except 0 allowed only for E_OK ---
(declare-fun AECode (ApplicationError) Int)
(declare-fun RepresentsEOK (ApplicationError) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((ae ApplicationError))
    (or (and (>= (AECode ae) 1) (<= (AECode ae) 63))
        (and (= (AECode ae) 0) (RepresentsEOK ae)))))

; --- 1109: SensorActuator SWC must run on ECU containing required HwElement (I/O HAL access case) ---
(declare-fun TypedBySASWCT (SwComponentPrototype SensorActuatorSwComponentType) Bool)
(declare-fun UsesIoHAL (SwComponentPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun SASWCT_RefHwType (SensorActuatorSwComponentType) HwType)
(declare-fun ECUOf (SwComponentPrototype) ECU)
(declare-fun HwElementOnECU (ECU HwElement) Bool)
(declare-fun HwElementImplementsType (HwElement HwType) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((scp SwComponentPrototype) (sat SensorActuatorSwComponentType))
    (=> (and (TypedBySASWCT scp sat) (UsesIoHAL scp))
        (exists ((ecu ECU) (he HwElement))
          (and (= ecu (ECUOf scp))
               (HwElementOnECU ecu he)
               (HwElementImplementsType he (SASWCT_RefHwType sat)))))))

; --- 1110 handled by SHACL (category enumeration) ---

; --- PROFILE_01 constraints (1111–1117) conditional on category PROFILE_01 ---
(declare-fun IsProfile01 (EndToEndDescription) Bool)
(declare-fun E2E_dataIdCount (EndToEndDescription) Int)
(declare-fun E2E_hasDataId (EndToEndDescription Int) Bool)
(declare-fun E2E_dataIdVal (EndToEndDescription Int) Int)
(declare-fun E2E_dataIdMode (EndToEndDescription) Int)
(declare-fun E2E_dataLength (EndToEndDescription) Int)
(declare-fun E2E_crcOffset (EndToEndDescription) Int)
(declare-fun E2E_counterOffset (EndToEndDescription) Int)
(declare-fun E2E_maxDeltaCounterInit (EndToEndDescription) Int)
(declare-fun Rcv_maxDeltaCounterInit (ReceiverComSpec) Int)
(declare-fun RcvProfileBoundTo (ReceiverComSpec EndToEndDescription) Bool)

; 1111: exactly one dataId in [0..65535]
(assert
  (forall ((d EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (IsProfile01 d)
        (and (= (E2E_dataIdCount d) 1)
             (forall ((i Int))
               (=> (E2E_hasDataId d i)
                   (and (>= (E2E_dataIdVal d i) 0) (<= (E2E_dataIdVal d i) 65535))))))))

; 1112: dataIdMode in [0..3]
(assert
  (forall ((d EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (IsProfile01 d)
        (and (>= (E2E_dataIdMode d) 0) (<= (E2E_dataIdMode d) 3)))))

; 1113: dataLength & dataId must exist (already implied by 1111; enforce dataLength presence)
(assert
  (forall ((d EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (IsProfile01 d)
        (exists ((x Int)) (= x (E2E_dataLength d))))))

; 1114: crcOffset in [0..65535] and multiple of 4
(assert
  (forall ((d EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (IsProfile01 d)
        (and (>= (E2E_crcOffset d) 0) (<= (E2E_crcOffset d) 65535)
             (= (mod (E2E_crcOffset d) 4) 0)))))

; 1115: counterOffset in [0..65535] and multiple of 4
(assert
  (forall ((d EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (IsProfile01 d)
        (and (>= (E2E_counterOffset d) 0) (<= (E2E_counterOffset d) 65535)
             (= (mod (E2E_counterOffset d) 4) 0)))))

; 1116: dataLength in [0..240] and multiple of 8
(assert
  (forall ((d EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (IsProfile01 d)
        (and (>= (E2E_dataLength d) 0) (<= (E2E_dataLength d) 240)
             (= (mod (E2E_dataLength d) 8) 0)))))

; 1117: maxDeltaCounterInit ranges for E2E and ReceiverComSpec: [0..14]
(assert
  (forall ((d EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (IsProfile01 d)
        (and (>= (E2E_maxDeltaCounterInit d) 0) (<= (E2E_maxDeltaCounterInit d) 14)))))
(assert
  (forall ((rc ReceiverComSpec) (d EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (and (IsProfile01 d) (RcvProfileBoundTo rc d))
        (and (>= (Rcv_maxDeltaCounterInit rc) 0) (<= (Rcv_maxDeltaCounterInit rc) 14)))))

; --- PROFILE_02 constraints (1118–1121) conditional on category PROFILE_02 ---
(declare-fun IsProfile02 (EndToEndDescription) Bool)

; 1118: only dataLength and dataId shall exist — model as absence of other profiled fields
(declare-fun E2E_hasOnlyDataLengthAndDataId (EndToEndDescription) Bool)
(assert
  (forall ((d EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (IsProfile02 d) (E2E_hasOnlyDataLengthAndDataId d))))

; 1119: dataLength in [0..65535] and multiple of 8
(assert
  (forall ((d EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (IsProfile02 d)
        (and (>= (E2E_dataLength d) 0) (<= (E2E_dataLength d) 65535)
             (= (mod (E2E_dataLength d) 8) 0)))))

; 1120: exactly 16 ordered dataId elements, each in [0..255]
(declare-fun E2E_dataIdAt (EndToEndDescription Int) Int)
(assert
  (forall ((d EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (IsProfile02 d)
        (and (= (E2E_dataIdCount d) 16)
             (forall ((k Int))
               (=> (and (<= 0 k) (< k 16))
                   (and (E2E_hasDataId d k)
                        (>= (E2E_dataIdAt d k) 0) (<= (E2E_dataIdAt d k) 255))))
             ; "ordered" captured by indexing semantics (0..15)
        )))

; 1121: maxDeltaCounterInit ranges for E2E and ReceiverComSpec: [0..15]
(declare-fun E2E_maxDeltaCounterInit02 (EndToEndDescription) Int)
(assert
  (forall ((d EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (IsProfile02 d)
        (and (>= (E2E_maxDeltaCounterInit02 d) 0) (<= (E2E_maxDeltaCounterInit02 d) 15)))))
(assert
  (forall ((rc ReceiverComSpec) (d EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (and (IsProfile02 d) (RcvProfileBoundTo rc d))
        (and (>= (Rcv_maxDeltaCounterInit rc) 0) (<= (Rcv_maxDeltaCounterInit rc) 15)))))




; -------- Sorts (reuse existing where applicable) --------
(declare-sort EndToEndDescription 0)
