; -------- Accessors / predicates --------
; Profiles / binding
(declare-fun IsProfile01 (EndToEndDescription) Bool)
(declare-fun IsProfile02 (EndToEndDescription) Bool)
(declare-fun RcvProfileBoundTo (ReceiverComSpec EndToEndDescription) Bool)

; 1214 syncCounterInit
(declare-fun E2E_syncCounterInit (EndToEndDescription) Int)
(declare-fun Rcv_syncCounterInit (ReceiverComSpec) Int)
(assert (forall ((d EndToEndDescription))
  (=> (IsProfile02 d)
      (and (>= (E2E_syncCounterInit d) 0) (<= (E2E_syncCounterInit d) 15)))))
(assert (forall ((rc ReceiverComSpec) (d EndToEndDescription))
  (=> (and (IsProfile02 d) (RcvProfileBoundTo rc d))
      (and (>= (Rcv_syncCounterInit rc) 0) (<= (Rcv_syncCounterInit rc) 15)))))

; 1215 / 1217 preference for maxNoNewOrRepeatedData (PROFILE_01/02 variants)
(declare-fun E2E_maxNoNewOrRepeatedData (EndToEndDescription) (Option Int))
(declare-fun Rcv_maxNoNewOrRepeatedData (ReceiverComSpec) (Option Int))
(declare-fun E2EProt_of_RPort (RPortPrototype) (Option EndToEndProtection))
(declare-fun E2EProt_profile (EndToEndProtection) EndToEndDescription)
(declare-fun E2EProtVar_receiverMatchesRcvDataElement (EndToEndProtection VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun RPort_hasReceiverFor (RPortPrototype VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun Rcv_dataElement (ReceiverComSpec) VariableDataPrototype)

; helper: does the correspondence rule hold for this RPort/E2E?
(declare-fun Corresponds (RPortPrototype EndToEndProtection ReceiverComSpec) Bool)
(assert (forall ((rp RPortPrototype) (prot EndToEndProtection) (rc ReceiverComSpec))
  (= (Corresponds rp prot rc)
     (exists ((v VariableDataPrototype))
       (and (RPort_hasReceiverFor rp v)
            (= v (Rcv_dataElement rc))
            (E2EProtVar_receiverMatchesRcvDataElement prot v))))))

; PROFILE_01: prefer Rcv value if correspondence & defined; else require E2E profile value exists
(assert (forall ((rp RPortPrototype) (rc ReceiverComSpec) (prot EndToEndProtection))
  (let ((e (E2EProt_profile prot)))
    (=> (and (IsProfile01 e) (Corresponds rp prot rc))
        (=> (is-some (Rcv_maxNoNewOrRepeatedData rc))
            (= (E2E_maxNoNewOrRepeatedData e) (Rcv_maxNoNewOrRepeatedData rc)))))))

(assert (forall ((rp RPortPrototype) (rc ReceiverComSpec) (prot EndToEndProtection))
  (let ((e (E2EProt_profile prot)))
    (=> (and (IsProfile01 e))
        (or (and (Corresponds rp prot rc) (is-some (Rcv_maxNoNewOrRepeatedData rc)))
            (is-some (E2E_maxNoNewOrRepeatedData e)))))))

; PROFILE_02 identical rule (1217)
(assert (forall ((rp RPortPrototype) (rc ReceiverComSpec) (prot EndToEndProtection))
  (let ((e (E2EProt_profile prot)))
    (=> (and (IsProfile02 e) (Corresponds rp prot rc))
        (=> (is-some (Rcv_maxNoNewOrRepeatedData rc))
            (= (E2E_maxNoNewOrRepeatedData e) (Rcv_maxNoNewOrRepeatedData rc)))))))

(assert (forall ((rp RPortPrototype) (rc ReceiverComSpec) (prot EndToEndProtection))
  (let ((e (E2EProt_profile prot)))
    (=> (IsProfile02 e)
        (or (and (Corresponds rp prot rc) (is-some (Rcv_maxNoNewOrRepeatedData rc)))
            (is-some (E2E_maxNoNewOrRepeatedData e)))))))

; 1216 / 1218 preference for syncCounterInit (PROFILE_01/02)
(declare-fun E2E_syncCounterInit_opt (EndToEndDescription) (Option Int))
(declare-fun Rcv_syncCounterInit_opt (ReceiverComSpec) (Option Int))

; PROFILE_01 syncCounterInit
(assert (forall ((rp RPortPrototype) (rc ReceiverComSpec) (prot EndToEndProtection))
  (let ((e (E2EProt_profile prot)))
    (=> (and (IsProfile01 e) (Corresponds rp prot rc))
        (=> (is-some (Rcv_syncCounterInit_opt rc))
            (= (E2E_syncCounterInit_opt e) (Rcv_syncCounterInit_opt rc)))))))

(assert (forall ((rp RPortPrototype) (rc ReceiverComSpec) (prot EndToEndProtection))
  (let ((e (E2EProt_profile prot)))
    (=> (IsProfile01 e)
        (or (and (Corresponds rp prot rc) (is-some (Rcv_syncCounterInit_opt rc)))
            (is-some (E2E_syncCounterInit_opt e)))))))

; PROFILE_02 syncCounterInit
(assert (forall ((rp RPortPrototype) (rc ReceiverComSpec) (prot EndToEndProtection))
  (let ((e (E2EProt_profile prot)))
    (=> (and (IsProfile02 e) (Corresponds rp prot rc))
        (=> (is-some (Rcv_syncCounterInit_opt rc))
            (= (E2E_syncCounterInit_opt e) (Rcv_syncCounterInit_opt rc)))))))

(assert (forall ((rp RPortPrototype) (rc ReceiverComSpec) (prot EndToEndProtection))
  (let ((e (E2EProt_profile prot)))
    (=> (IsProfile02 e)
        (or (and (Corresponds rp prot rc) (is-some (Rcv_syncCounterInit_opt rc)))
            (is-some (E2E_syncCounterInit_opt e)))))))

; 1219 — invalidation only if swImplPolicy != queued
(declare-fun SwImplPolicyQueued (SwDataDefPropsContent) Bool)
(declare-fun InvalidationAllowedFor (VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun SDPropsOf (VariableDataPrototype) (Option SwDataDefPropsContent))
(assert (forall ((v VariableDataPrototype) (p SwDataDefPropsContent))
  (=> (= (SDPropsOf v) (some p))
      (=> (SwImplPolicyQueued p) (not (InvalidationAllowedFor v))))))

; 1220 — SwBaseType compatibility IFF selected fields equal
(declare-fun SizeKey (BaseTypeDirectDefinition) Int) ; choose baseTypeSize if present, else maxBaseTypeSize
(declare-fun ByteOrder (BaseTypeDirectDefinition) Int)
(declare-fun MemAlignment (BaseTypeDirectDefinition) Int)
(declare-fun BaseTypeEncoding (BaseTypeDirectDefinition) String)
(declare-fun NativeDecl (BaseTypeDirectDefinition) String)
(declare-fun CompatibleSwBaseType (BaseTypeDirectDefinition BaseTypeDirectDefinition) Bool)
(assert (forall ((a BaseTypeDirectDefinition) (b BaseTypeDirectDefinition))
  (= (CompatibleSwBaseType a b)
     (and (= (SizeKey a) (SizeKey b))
          (= (ByteOrder a) (ByteOrder b))
          (= (MemAlignment a) (MemAlignment b))
          (= (BaseTypeEncoding a) (BaseTypeEncoding b))
          (= (NativeDecl a) (NativeDecl b))))))

; 1221 — APDT requires ApplicationValueSpecification; if enum, value matches one of CompuScale texts
(declare-fun DpTypeIsAPDT (DataPrototype ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun APDT_IsEnum (ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun InitValOf (DataPrototype) (Option ValueSpecification))
(declare-fun IsApplicationValueSpec (ValueSpecification) Bool)
(declare-fun MatchesEnumTextValue (ValueSpecification ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) Bool) ; matches vt / shortLabel / symbol
(assert (forall ((dp DataPrototype) (t ApplicationPrimitiveDataType))
  (=> (DpTypeIsAPDT dp t)
      (and (is-some (InitValOf dp))
           (IsApplicationValueSpec (get (InitValOf dp)))
           (=> (APDT_IsEnum t) (MatchesEnumTextValue (get (InitValOf dp)) t))))))

; 1222 — STRING-typed AutosarDataType ⇒ init ApplicationValueSpecification of category STRING
(declare-fun DpTypedByADT (DataPrototype AutosarDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun ADT_CategorySTRING (AutosarDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun AVS_IsSTRING (ApplicationValueSpecification) Bool)
(assert (forall ((dp DataPrototype) (adt AutosarDataType))
  (=> (and (DpTypedByADT dp adt) (ADT_CategorySTRING adt))
      (exists ((v ValueSpecification))
        (and (= (InitValOf dp) (some v))
             (IsApplicationValueSpec v)
             (AVS_IsSTRING (as v ApplicationValueSpecification)))))))

; 1223 — ApplicationRecordDataType ⇒ RecordValueSpecification
(declare-fun DpTypeIsARDT (DataPrototype ApplicationRecordDataType) Bool)
(assert (forall ((dp DataPrototype) (t ApplicationRecordDataType))
  (=> (DpTypeIsARDT dp t)
      (exists ((v ValueSpecification))
        (and (= (InitValOf dp) (some v))
             (= (as v RecordValueSpecification) v))))))

; 1224 — ApplicationArrayDataType ⇒ ArrayValueSpecification or ApplicationRuleBasedValueSpecification
(declare-fun DpTypeIsAADT (DataPrototype ApplicationArrayDataType) Bool)
(assert (forall ((dp DataPrototype) (t ApplicationArrayDataType))
  (=> (DpTypeIsAADT dp t)
      (exists ((v ValueSpecification))
        (and (= (InitValOf dp) (some v))
             (or (= (as v ArrayValueSpecification) v)
                 (= (as v ApplicationRuleBasedValueSpecification) v)))))))

; 1225 — ImplDataType references CompuMethod TEXTTABLE/BITFIELD_TEXTTABLE ⇒ TextValueSpecification with legal text
(declare-fun DpTypeIsIDT (DataPrototype ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun IDT_CompuMethod (ImplementationDataType) (Option CompuMethod))
(declare-fun CM_IsTextTable (CompuMethod) Bool)
(declare-fun CM_IsBitfieldTextTable (CompuMethod) Bool)
(declare-fun MatchesCompuScaleText (TextValueSpecification CompuMethod) Bool)
(assert (forall ((dp DataPrototype) (idt ImplementationDataType) (cm CompuMethod))
  (=> (and (DpTypeIsIDT dp idt) (= (IDT_CompuMethod idt) (some cm))
           (or (CM_IsTextTable cm) (CM_IsBitfieldTextTable cm)))
      (exists ((v ValueSpecification))
        (and (= (InitValOf dp) (some v))
             (= (as v TextValueSpecification) v)
             (MatchesCompuScaleText (as v TextValueSpecification) cm))))))

; 1226 — ExecutableEntityActivationReason.bitPosition ∈ [0..31]
(declare-fun EEAr_bitPosition (ExecutableEntityActivationReason) Int)
(assert (forall ((x ExecutableEntityActivationReason))
  (and (>= (EEAr_bitPosition x) 0) (<= (EEAr_bitPosition x) 31))))

; 1227 — bitPosition and symbol unique within RunnableEntity
(declare-fun ReasonOf (RunnableEntity ExecutableEntityActivationReason) Bool)
(declare-fun EEAr_symbol (ExecutableEntityActivationReason) String)
(assert (forall ((r RunnableEntity) (a ExecutableEntityActivationReason) (b ExecutableEntityActivationReason))
  (=> (and (ReasonOf r a) (ReasonOf r b) (= (EEAr_bitPosition a) (EEAr_bitPosition b))) (= a b))))
(assert (forall ((r RunnableEntity) (a ExecutableEntityActivationReason) (b ExecutableEntityActivationReason))
  (=> (and (ReasonOf r a) (ReasonOf r b) (= (EEAr_symbol a) (EEAr_symbol b))) (= a b))))

; 1228 — If some WaitPoint.trigger references an RTEEvent e, then e must not have activationReasonRepresentation
(declare-fun TriggerRef (WaitPoint) RTEEvent)
(declare-fun HasActivationReasonRepresentation (RTEEvent) Bool)
(assert (forall ((w WaitPoint) (e RTEEvent))
  (=> (= (TriggerRef w) e) (not (HasActivationReasonRepresentation e)))))

; 1231 / 1232 — ConsistencyNeeds instanceRefs must stay within the aggregating component’s context
(declare-fun CN_AggregatedBy (ConsistencyNeeds CompositionSwComponentType) Bool)
(declare-fun CN_AggregatedByAtomic (ConsistencyNeeds AtomicSwComponentType) Bool)
(declare-fun CN_InstanceRefsWithinComposition (ConsistencyNeeds CompositionSwComponentType) Bool)
(declare-fun CN_InstanceRefsWithinAtomic (ConsistencyNeeds AtomicSwComponentType) Bool)
(assert (forall ((cn ConsistencyNeeds) (c CompositionSwComponentType))
  (=> (CN_AggregatedBy cn c) (CN_InstanceRefsWithinComposition cn c))))
(assert (forall ((cn ConsistencyNeeds) (a AtomicSwComponentType))
  (=> (CN_AggregatedByAtomic cn a) (CN_InstanceRefsWithinAtomic cn a))))

; 1233 — InstantiationTimingEventProps.refinedEvent must be a TimingEvent
(declare-fun RefinedEventOf (InstantiationTimingEventProps) RTEEvent)
(declare-fun IsTimingEvent (RTEEvent) Bool)
(assert (forall ((p InstantiationTimingEventProps))
  (IsTimingEvent (RefinedEventOf p))))

; 1234 — NvBlockSwComponentType runnable triggered by OperationInvokedEvent ⇒ symbol in NvM API set
(declare-fun RunnableOwnedByNvBlock (RunnableEntity NvBlockSwComponentType) Bool)
(declare-fun RunnableTriggeredByOpInvoked (RunnableEntity OperationInvokedEvent) Bool)
(declare-fun RunnableSymbol (RunnableEntity) String)
(declare-fun NvMApiName (String) Bool)
(assert (forall ((r RunnableEntity) (n NvBlockSwComponentType) (evt OperationInvokedEvent))
  (=> (and (RunnableOwnedByNvBlock r n) (RunnableTriggeredByOpInvoked r evt))
      (NvMApiName (RunnableSymbol r)))))
; Clarification: otherwise no restriction — modeled implicitly by lack of other constraints.

; 1237 — firstOperation & secondOperation belong to exactly one (different) CSI
(declare-fun FirstOp (ClientServerOperationMapping) ClientServerOperation)
(declare-fun SecondOp (ClientServerOperationMapping) ClientServerOperation)
(declare-fun OwnerCSI (ClientServerOperation) ClientServerInterface)
(assert (forall ((m ClientServerOperationMapping))
  (distinct (OwnerCSI (FirstOp m)) (OwnerCSI (SecondOp m)))))

; 1238 — first/second ApplicationError belong to exactly one (different) CSI
(declare-fun FirstAE (ClientServerApplicationErrorMapping) ApplicationError)
(declare-fun SecondAE (ClientServerApplicationErrorMapping) ApplicationError)
(declare-fun OwnerCSI_AE (ApplicationError) ClientServerInterface)
(assert (forall ((m ClientServerApplicationErrorMapping))
  (distinct (OwnerCSI_AE (FirstAE m)) (OwnerCSI_AE (SecondAE m)))))

; 1240 — argument coverage: every argument of both ops is mapped
(declare-fun ArgsOf (ClientServerOperation ArgumentDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun ArgMappingsOf (ClientServerOperationMapping DataPrototypeMapping) Bool)
(declare-fun MapsFirstDP (DataPrototypeMapping ArgumentDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun MapsSecondDP (DataPrototypeMapping ArgumentDataPrototype) Bool)
(assert (forall ((m ClientServerOperationMapping) (a ArgumentDataPrototype))
  (=> (ArgsOf (FirstOp m) a)
      (exists ((map DataPrototypeMapping))
        (and (ArgMappingsOf m map) (MapsFirstDP map a))))))
(assert (forall ((m ClientServerOperationMapping) (a ArgumentDataPrototype))
  (=> (ArgsOf (SecondOp m) a)
      (exists ((map DataPrototypeMapping))
        (and (ArgMappingsOf m map) (MapsSecondDP map a))))))

; 1241 — APDT category != STRING ⇒ must NOT define invalidValue
(declare-fun APDT_CategorySTRING (ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun APDT_InvalidValueDefined (ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) Bool)
(assert (forall ((t ApplicationPrimitiveDataType))
  (=> (not (APDT_CategorySTRING t)) (not (APDT_InvalidValueDefined t)))))

; 1295 — DATA_REFERENCE limitations for PortInterfaces in ASWC/SensorActuator context

(declare-fun PIUsedByAppOrSensorActuator (PortInterface) Bool)

; 1296 — IRV vs DATA_REFERENCE

(declare-fun VDP_ResolvesToIDT (VariableDataPrototype ImplementationDataType) Bool)

; ---------- Constraints ----------

; 1271 — Record init must match ARDT structure
(assert (forall ((dp DataPrototype) (t ApplicationRecordDataType) (r RecordValueSpecification))
  (=> (and (DpTypeIsARDT dp t)
           (= (InitValOf dp) (some r)))
      (= (CountRecordFields r) (CountRecordElements t)))))

; 1272 — IDT(STRUCTURE) record-init must match subElements count
(assert (forall ((dp DataPrototype) (idt ImplementationDataType) (r RecordValueSpecification))
  (=> (and (DpTypeIsIDT dp idt) (IDT_CategoryStructure idt)
           (= (InitValOf dp) (some r)))
      (= (CountRecordFields r) (IDT_SubElementsCount idt)))))

; 1273 — AADT array-init size equals maxNumberOfElements (arraySizeSemantics ignored)
(assert (forall ((dp DataPrototype) (a ApplicationArrayDataType) (v ArrayValueSpecification))
  (=> (and (DpTypeIsAADT dp a) (= (InitArrValOf dp) (some v)))
      (= (CountArrayElements v) (AADT_ElementMaxNumberOfElements a)))))

; 1274 — IDT(ARRAY) array-init size equals subElement.arraySize (semantics ignored)
(assert (forall ((dp DataPrototype) (idt ImplementationDataType) (v ArrayValueSpecification))
  (=> (and (DpTypeIsIDT dp idt) (IDT_CategoryArray idt)
           (= (InitArrValOf dp) (some v)))
      (= (CountArrayElements v) (IDT_ArraySize idt)))))

; 1277 — swImplPolicy must not be queued for variables accessed by dataReceivePointByValue
(assert (forall ((r RunnableEntity) (va VariableAccess) (v VariableDataPrototype) (p SwDataDefPropsContent))
  (=> (and (DataReceivePointByValue r va)
           (= (AccessedVariable va) v)
           (= (SDPropsOf v) (some p)))
      (not (SwImplPolicyQueued p)))))

; 1278 — DataConstr compatibility depends on units
(assert (forall ((a DataConstr) (b DataConstr))
  (= (CompatibleDataConstr a b)
     (let ((ua (UnitOf a)) (ub (UnitOf b)))
       (or (and (is-none ua) (is-none ub))
           (and (is-some ua) (is-some ub) (CompatibleUnits (get ua) (get ub))))))))

; 1279 — If swImplPolicy=queued then no unmapped receiver-side elements
(assert (forall ((t Trigger))
  (=> (TriggerPolicyQueued t)
      (not (HasUnmappedReceiverElementsUnderQueuedPolicy t)))))

; 1280 — If unmapped elements on receiver side and NonqueuedReceiverComSpec is used, it must have initValue
(assert (forall ((port AbstractRequiredPortPrototype) (n NonqueuedReceiverComSpec))
  (=> (and (HasUnmappedReceiverElementsForPort port)
           (PortHasNonqueuedReceiverComSpec port n))
      (NonqueuedInitExists n))))

; 1281 — If invalidValue is inside CompuMethod scope, it must be an ApplicationValueSpecification
(assert (forall ((t ApplicationPrimitiveDataType))
  (=> (and (APDT_CategoryVALUE t) (InvalidValueInsideCompuScope t))
      (InvalidValueIsApplicationSpec t))))

; 1281_Explanation — That ApplicationValueSpecification must lie within Compu bounds
(assert (forall ((t ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) (avs ApplicationValueSpecification))
  (=> (and (InvalidValueInsideCompuScope t)
           (= (InvalidValueSpecOf t) (some avs)))
      (AVSWithinCompuBounds t avs))))

; 1282 — invalidValue must NOT be RuleBased or ReferenceValueSpecification
(assert (forall ((t ApplicationPrimitiveDataType))
  (and (not (InvalidValueIsRuleBased t))
       (not (InvalidValueIsReference t)))))

; 1283 — If invalidValue is outside CompuMethod scope, it must be NumericalValueSpecification
(assert (forall ((t ApplicationPrimitiveDataType))
  (=> (and (APDT_CategoryVALUE t) (InvalidValueOutsideCompuScope t))
      (InvalidValueIsNumerical t))))

; 1284 — TextValueSpecification allowed only with certain CompuMethod categories
(assert (forall ((dp DataPrototype) (adt AutosarDataType) (tvs TextValueSpecification) (s SwDataDefProps) (c SwDataDefPropsContent) (cm CompuMethod))
  (=> (and (DpTypeIsADT dp adt)
           (InitValueIsTextSpecFor dp tvs)
           (= (ADT_SwDataDefProps adt) (some s))
           (= (SDPropsContentOf s) (some c))
           (= (SDDPC_CompuMethod c) (some cm)))
      (or (CM_IsTextTable cm)
          (CM_IsBitfieldTextTable cm)
          (CM_IsScaleLinearAndText cm)
          (CM_IsScaleRationalAndText cm)))))

; 1285 — NvBlockDataMapping roles vs Port type
(assert (forall ((m NvBlockDataMapping) (p PortPrototype))
  (=> (= (NVMap_Port m) p)
      (and (=> (NVMap_Uses_writtenNvData m) (IsRPort p))
           (=> (NVMap_Uses_writtenReadNvData m) (IsPRPort p))
           (=> (NVMap_Uses_readNvData m) (IsPPort p))))))

; 1286 — serverArgumentImplPolicy != useVoid for IN args typed by primitive C
(assert (forall ((a ArgumentDataPrototype) (t AutosarDataType))
  (=> (and (ArgDirectionIn a) (ArgTypeIsADT a t) (ADT_BoilsDownToPrimitiveC t))
      (not (ServerArgImplPolicy_useVoid a)))))

; 1287 — VDPs in S/R interfaces compatible only if invalidationPolicy equal
(assert (forall ((x VariableDataPrototype) (y VariableDataPrototype))
  (= (CompatibleVdpInSRI x y)
     (= (InvalidationPolicyOf x) (InvalidationPolicyOf y)))))

; 1288 — Allowed SwDataDefProps vs IDT category (Table 5.32)
(assert (forall ((dp DataPrototype) (s SwDataDefProps) (idt ImplementationDataType))
  (=> (and (DpTypeIsIDT dp idt) (DpHasSwDataDefProps dp s))
      (SwDp_AllowedByTable532 s (IDT_Category idt)))))

; 1289 — Allowed SwDataDefProps vs ADT category (Table 5.31)
(assert (forall ((dp DataPrototype) (s SwDataDefProps) (adt ApplicationDataType))
  (=> (and (DpTypeIsADT dp adt) (DpHasSwDataDefProps dp s))
      (SwDp_AllowedByTable531 s (ADT_Category adt)))))

; 1290 — In one PPortPrototype, only one PPortComSpec per referenced dataElement OR operation
(assert (forall ((port PPortPrototype) (c1 PPortComSpec) (c2 PPortComSpec))
  (=> (and (PPortOwnsComSpecRef port c1) (PPortOwnsComSpecRef port c2))
      (and (=> (= (ComSpecRefDataElementKey c1) (ComSpecRefDataElementKey c2)) (= c1 c2))
           (=> (= (ComSpecRefOperationKey c1) (ComSpecRefOperationKey c2)) (= c1 c2))))))

; 1291 — In one RPortPrototype, only one RPortComSpec per referenced dataElement OR operation
(assert (forall ((port RPortPrototype) (c1 RPortComSpec) (c2 RPortComSpec))
  (=> (and (RPortOwnsComSpecRef port c1) (RPortOwnsComSpecRef port c2))
      (and (=> (= (RComSpecRefDataElementKey c1) (RComSpecRefDataElementKey c2)) (= c1 c2))
           (=> (= (RComSpecRefOperationKey c1) (RComSpecRefOperationKey c2)) (= c1 c2))))))

; 1292 — In one PRPortPrototype, only one R and one P ComSpec per referenced element/operation
(assert (forall ((port PRPortPrototype) (c1 PRPortComSpec) (c2 PRPortComSpec))
  (=> (and (PRPortOwnsComSpecRef port c1) (PRPortOwnsComSpecRef port c2))
      (and (=> (= (PR_R_ComSpecRefDataElementKey c1) (PR_R_ComSpecRefDataElementKey c2)) (= c1 c2))
           (=> (= (PR_R_ComSpecRefOperationKey c1) (PR_R_ComSpecRefOperationKey c2)) (= c1 c2))
           (=> (= (PR_P_ComSpecRefDataElementKey c1) (PR_P_ComSpecRefDataElementKey c2)) (= c1 c2))
           (=> (= (PR_P_ComSpecRefOperationKey c1) (PR_P_ComSpecRefOperationKey c2)) (= c1 c2))))))

; 1295 — DATA_REFERENCE allowed only if provider/requester is in allowed component categories
(assert (forall ((dp DataPrototype) (idt ImplementationDataType) (pi PortInterface))
  (=> (and (= (PortInterfaceOf dp) pi)
           (PIUsedByAppOrSensorActuator pi)
           (DP_ResolvesToIDT dp idt)
           (IDT_CategoryDataReference idt))
      (ProviderOrRequesterIsAllowedCat pi))))

; 1296 — IRVs must not be DATA_REFERENCE-typed/mapped
(assert (forall ((beh SwcInternalBehavior) (v VariableDataPrototype) (idt ImplementationDataType))
  (=> (and (VDP_ResolvesToIDT v idt) (IDT_CategoryDataReference idt))
      (and (not (IRV_Explicit beh v))
           (not (IRV_Implicit beh v))))))

; 1297 — serverArgumentImplPolicy=useArrayBaseType only for args that resolve to ARRAY
(assert (forall ((a ArgumentDataPrototype) (idt ImplementationDataType) (adt AutosarDataType))
  (=> (ServerArgImplPolicy_useArrayBaseType a)
      (or (and (ArgTypeIsIDT a idt) (IDT_CategoryArray idt))
          (and (ArgTypeIsADT a adt) (ADT_MapsToIDTArray adt))))))




;; Type declarations
(declare-sort DelegationSwConnector 0)

(declare-sort PortInterface 0)
(declare-sort ComSpec 0)
(declare-sort CompuMethod 0)
(declare-sort CompuScale 0)
(declare-sort ApplicationDataType 0)
(declare-sort ClientServerInterface 0)
(declare-sort EndToEndDescription 0)
(declare-sort RoleBasedPortAssignment 0)

;; Port type predicates
(declare-fun isRPort (PortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun isPPort (PortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun isPRPort (PortPrototype) Bool)

;; Interface type predicates
(declare-fun isSenderReceiverInterface (PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun isNvDataInterface (PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun isClientServerInterface (PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun isModeSwitchInterface (PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun isParameterInterface (PortInterface) Bool)

;; ComSpec type predicates
(declare-fun isClientComSpec (ComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isServerComSpec (ComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isModeSwitchSenderComSpec (ComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isModeSwitchReceiverComSpec (ComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isNvRequireComSpec (ComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isNvProvideComSpec (ComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isParameterProvideComSpec (ComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isParameterRequireComSpec (ComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isReceiverComSpec (ComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isSenderComSpec (ComSpec) Bool)

;; DelegationSwConnector functions
(declare-fun innerPort (DelegationSwConnector) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun outerPort (DelegationSwConnector) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun portInterface (PortPrototype) PortInterface)

;; Table_4_5: DelegationSwConnector connection rules
(assert (forall ((conn DelegationSwConnector))
    (let ((inner (innerPort conn))
          (outer (outerPort conn))
          (iface (portInterface (innerPort conn))))
        (=> (or (isSenderReceiverInterface iface) (isNvDataInterface iface))
            (or
                (and (isRPort inner) (isRPort outer))
                (and (isRPort inner) (isPRPort outer))
                (and (isPPort inner) (isPPort outer))
                (and (isPPort inner) (isPRPort outer))
                (and (isPRPort inner) (isRPort outer))
                (and (isPRPort inner) (isPPort outer))
                (and (isPRPort inner) (isPRPort outer)))))))

;; ComSpec compatibility functions
(declare-fun compatibleComSpec (PortInterface ComSpec) Bool)

;; Table_4_59 series: Interface-ComSpec compatibility
(assert (forall ((iface ClientServerInterface) (cs ComSpec))
    (=> (compatibleComSpec iface cs)
        (or (isClientComSpec cs) (isServerComSpec cs)))))

(assert (forall ((iface PortInterface) (cs ComSpec))
    (=> (and (isModeSwitchInterface iface) (compatibleComSpec iface cs))
        (or (isModeSwitchSenderComSpec cs) (isModeSwitchReceiverComSpec cs)))))

(assert (forall ((iface PortInterface) (cs ComSpec))
    (=> (and (isNvDataInterface iface) (compatibleComSpec iface cs))
        (or (isNvRequireComSpec cs) (isNvProvideComSpec cs)))))

(assert (forall ((iface PortInterface) (cs ComSpec))
    (=> (and (isParameterInterface iface) (compatibleComSpec iface cs))
        (or (isParameterProvideComSpec cs) (isParameterRequireComSpec cs)))))

(assert (forall ((iface PortInterface) (cs ComSpec))
    (=> (and (isSenderReceiverInterface iface) (compatibleComSpec iface cs))
        (or (isReceiverComSpec cs) (isSenderComSpec cs)))))

;; CompuMethod functions
(declare-fun category (CompuMethod) String)
(declare-fun hasCompuInternalToPhys (CompuMethod) Bool)
(declare-fun hasCompuPhysToInternal (CompuMethod) Bool)
(declare-fun hasCompuInverseValue (CompuScale) Bool)
(declare-fun hasCompuConst (CompuScale) Bool)
(declare-fun hasLowerLimit (CompuScale) Bool)
(declare-fun hasUpperLimit (CompuScale) Bool)
(declare-fun belongsTo (CompuScale CompuMethod) Bool)

;; Table_5_74_Annotation_1: LINEAR/SCALE_LINEAR require compuInternalToPhys if not defined by compuPhysToInternal
(assert (forall ((cm CompuMethod))
    (=> (or (= (category cm) "LINEAR") (= (category cm) "SCALE_LINEAR"))
        (=> (not (hasCompuPhysToInternal cm))
            (hasCompuInternalToPhys cm)))))

;; Table_5_74_Annotation_2: RAT_FUNC/SCALE_RAT_FUNC require both directions or inverse value
(assert (forall ((cm CompuMethod))
    (=> (or (= (category cm) "RAT_FUNC") (= (category cm) "SCALE_RAT_FUNC"))
        (or
            (and (hasCompuPhysToInternal cm) (hasCompuInternalToPhys cm))
            (exists ((cs CompuScale)) (and (belongsTo cs cm) (hasCompuInverseValue cs)))))))

;; Table_5_74_Annotation_4: Limit definition requirement
(assert (forall ((cm CompuMethod) (cs CompuScale))
    (=> (and (belongsTo cs cm)
             (or (= (category cm) "RAT_FUNC") (= (category cm) "SCALE_RAT_FUNC")))
        (and (hasLowerLimit cs) (hasUpperLimit cs)))))

;; ApplicationDataType functions
(declare-fun adtCategory (ApplicationDataType) String)
(declare-fun hasSwCalibrationAccess (ApplicationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun hasSwRecordLayout (ApplicationDataType) Bool)

;; Table_5_8_AppDataType_COM_AXIS_swCalibrationAccess_Multiplicity
(assert (forall ((adt ApplicationDataType))
    (=> (= (adtCategory adt) "COM_AXIS")
        (hasSwCalibrationAccess adt))))

;; Table_5_8_AppDataType_VAL_BLK_swRecordLayout_Multiplicity
(assert (forall ((adt ApplicationDataType))
    (=> (= (adtCategory adt) "VAL_BLK")
        (or (hasSwRecordLayout adt) (not (hasSwRecordLayout adt))))))

;; Table_7_64: RoleBasedPortAssignment role matching
(declare-fun roleOfAssignment (RoleBasedPortAssignment) String)
(declare-fun nameOfInterface (ClientServerInterface) String)
(declare-fun usesInterface (RoleBasedPortAssignment ClientServerInterface) Bool)

(assert (forall ((rbpa RoleBasedPortAssignment) (csi ClientServerInterface))
    (=> (usesInterface rbpa csi)
        (= (roleOfAssignment rbpa) (nameOfInterface csi)))))

;; constr_1001: dataId uniqueness within System
(declare-fun dataIdValue (EndToEndDescription Int) Int)
(declare-fun hasDataId (EndToEndDescription Int) Bool)

(assert (forall ((e1 EndToEndDescription) (e2 EndToEndDescription) (i1 Int) (i2 Int))
    (=> (and (hasDataId e1 i1) (hasDataId e2 i2)
             (= (dataIdValue e1 i1) (dataIdValue e2 i2)))
        (and (= e1 e2) (= i1 i2)))))




;; Type declarations

(declare-sort SwComponentPrototype 0)


(declare-sort PortInterface 0)
(declare-sort DataInterface 0)
(declare-sort ClientServerOperation 0)
(declare-sort ApplicationError 0)
(declare-sort ClientServerInterface 0)
(declare-sort AutosarDataPrototype 0)
(declare-sort SwDataDefProps 0)
(declare-sort DataPrototypeMapping 0)

(declare-sort ApplicationPrimitiveDataType 0)
(declare-sort ApplicationRecordDataType 0)
(declare-sort ApplicationArrayDataType 0)
(declare-sort ImplementationDataType 0)
(declare-sort Unit 0)
(declare-sort PhysicalDimension 0)
(declare-sort PhysicalDimensionMapping 0)
(declare-sort ApplicationDataType 0)


;; Functions for composition and typing
(declare-fun containsPrototype (CompositionSwComponentType SwComponentPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun prototypeTypedBy (SwComponentPrototype SwComponentType) Bool)
(declare-fun isCompositionType (SwComponentType) Bool)

;; Reachability for recursive composition check (constr_1035)
(define-fun-rec compositionReachable ((from CompositionSwComponentType) (to SwComponentType)) Bool
    (or
        (exists ((proto SwComponentPrototype))
            (and (containsPrototype from proto)
                 (prototypeTypedBy proto to)))
        (exists ((proto SwComponentPrototype) (intermediate SwComponentType))
            (and (containsPrototype from proto)
                 (prototypeTypedBy proto intermediate)
                 (isCompositionType intermediate)
                 (compositionReachable intermediate to)))))

;; constr_1035: No recursive composition
(assert (forall ((comp CompositionSwComponentType))
    (not (compositionReachable comp comp))))

;; Port and interface functions (constr_1036)
(declare-fun portTypedBy (PortPrototype PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun connected (PortPrototype PortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun interfaceKind (PortInterface) String)
(declare-fun isDataInterfaceSubtype (PortInterface) Bool)

;; constr_1036: Interface kind matching for connections
(assert (forall ((p1 PortPrototype) (p2 PortPrototype) (i1 PortInterface) (i2 PortInterface))
    (=> (and (connected p1 p2) (portTypedBy p1 i1) (portTypedBy p2 i2))
        (or
            (= (interfaceKind i1) (interfaceKind i2))
            (and (isDataInterfaceSubtype i1) (isDataInterfaceSubtype i2))))))

;; Client-server connection functions (constr_1037)
(declare-fun isClientPort (PortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun isServerPort (PortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun handlesSameOperation (PortPrototype PortPrototype) Bool)

;; constr_1037: Client not connected to multiple servers for same operation
(assert (forall ((client PortPrototype) (server1 PortPrototype) (server2 PortPrototype))
    (=> (and (isClientPort client)
             (isServerPort server1) (isServerPort server2)
             (connected client server1) (connected client server2)
             (handlesSameOperation server1 server2))
        (= server1 server2))))

;; Ownership and error reference (constr_1038)
(declare-fun ownedBy (ApplicationError ClientServerInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun operationInInterface (ClientServerOperation ClientServerInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun referencesError (ClientServerOperation ApplicationError) Bool)

;; constr_1038: ApplicationError ownership scope
(assert (forall ((op ClientServerOperation) (err ApplicationError) (iface ClientServerInterface))
    (=> (and (operationInInterface op iface) (referencesError op err))
        (ownedBy err iface))))

;; swImplPolicy consistency (constr_1039)
(declare-fun swImplPolicy (AutosarDataPrototype) String)
(declare-fun mappedTo (AutosarDataPrototype AutosarDataPrototype) Bool)

;; constr_1039: swImplPolicy mapping constraint
(assert (forall ((proto1 AutosarDataPrototype) (proto2 AutosarDataPrototype))
    (=> (mappedTo proto1 proto2)
        (not (and (= (swImplPolicy proto1) "queued")
                  (not (= (swImplPolicy proto2) "queued")))))))

;; Data type compatibility helper functions
(declare-fun category (ApplicationDataType) String)
(declare-fun categoryImpl (ImplementationDataType) String)
(declare-fun swDataDefPropsCompatible (SwDataDefProps SwDataDefProps) Bool)
(declare-fun compatibleDataTypes (ApplicationPrimitiveDataType ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) Bool)

;; constr_1047: ApplicationPrimitiveDataType compatibility
(assert (forall ((adt1 ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) (adt2 ApplicationPrimitiveDataType))
    (=> (compatibleDataTypes adt1 adt2)
        (or
            ;; Condition 1: same category and compatible props
            (exists ((cat String) (props1 SwDataDefProps) (props2 SwDataDefProps))
                (and (= (category adt1) cat) (= (category adt2) cat)
                     (swDataDefPropsCompatible props1 props2)))
            ;; Condition 2 & 3: DataPrototypeMapping exists
            (exists ((mapping DataPrototypeMapping))
                (or (and (mappedTo adt1 adt2))
                    (and (mappedTo adt2 adt1))))))))

;; Record type compatibility (constr_1048)
(declare-fun recordElementAtPosition (ApplicationRecordDataType Int) DataPrototype)
(declare-fun elementTypesCompatible (DataPrototype DataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun compatibleRecordTypes (ApplicationRecordDataType ApplicationRecordDataType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((rec1 ApplicationRecordDataType) (rec2 ApplicationRecordDataType))
    (=> (compatibleRecordTypes rec1 rec2)
        (or
            ;; Condition 1: all elements at same position compatible
            (forall ((pos Int))
                (elementTypesCompatible 
                    (recordElementAtPosition rec1 pos)
                    (recordElementAtPosition rec2 pos)))
            ;; Condition 2: SubElementMapping exists
            (exists ((mapping DataPrototypeMapping))
                (mappedTo rec1 rec2))))))

;; Array type compatibility (constr_1049)
(declare-fun arrayMaxElements (ApplicationArrayDataType) Int)
(declare-fun arraySizeSemantics (ApplicationArrayDataType) String)
(declare-fun arrayElementType (ApplicationArrayDataType) ApplicationDataType)
(declare-fun compatibleArrayTypes (ApplicationArrayDataType ApplicationArrayDataType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((arr1 ApplicationArrayDataType) (arr2 ApplicationArrayDataType))
    (=> (compatibleArrayTypes arr1 arr2)
        (or
            ;; Condition 1: compatible elements and same size/semantics
            (and (compatibleDataTypes (arrayElementType arr1) (arrayElementType arr2))
                 (= (arrayMaxElements arr1) (arrayMaxElements arr2))
                 (= (arraySizeSemantics arr1) (arraySizeSemantics arr2)))
            ;; Condition 2: SubElementMapping exists
            (exists ((mapping DataPrototypeMapping))
                (mappedTo arr1 arr2))))))

;; ImplementationDataType compatibility (constr_1050)
(declare-fun implStructureIdentical (ImplementationDataType ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun implArraySizeIdentical (ImplementationDataType ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun compatibleImplTypes (ImplementationDataType ImplementationDataType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((impl1 ImplementationDataType) (impl2 ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (compatibleImplTypes impl1 impl2)
        (or
            ;; Condition 1: same category, structure, size, props
            (and (= (categoryImpl impl1) (categoryImpl impl2))
                 (implStructureIdentical impl1 impl2)
                 (implArraySizeIdentical impl1 impl2))
            ;; Condition 2 & 3: DataPrototypeMapping exists
            (exists ((mapping DataPrototypeMapping))
                (mappedTo impl1 impl2))))))

;; Unit compatibility (constr_1052)
(declare-fun factorSiToUnit (Unit) Real)
(declare-fun offsetSiToUnit (Unit) Real)
(declare-fun unitPhysicalDimension (Unit) PhysicalDimension)
(declare-fun hasFactor (Unit) Bool)
(declare-fun hasOffset (Unit) Bool)
(declare-fun hasPhysDim (Unit) Bool)
(declare-fun compatibleUnits (Unit Unit) Bool)

(assert (forall ((u1 Unit) (u2 Unit))
    (=> (compatibleUnits u1 u2)
        (and
            ;; Compatible factors and offsets
            (=> (and (hasFactor u1) (hasFactor u2))
                (= (factorSiToUnit u1) (factorSiToUnit u2)))
            (=> (and (hasOffset u1) (hasOffset u2))
                (= (offsetSiToUnit u1) (offsetSiToUnit u2)))
            ;; Identical or both absent PhysicalDimension
            (=> (and (hasPhysDim u1) (hasPhysDim u2))
                (= (unitPhysicalDimension u1) (unitPhysicalDimension u2)))))))

;; PhysicalDimension compatibility (constr_1053)
(declare-fun lengthExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun massExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun timeExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun currentExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun temperatureExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun molarAmountExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun luminousIntensityExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun physDimShortName (PhysicalDimension) String)
(declare-fun physDimMappingExists (PhysicalDimension PhysicalDimension) Bool)
(declare-fun compatiblePhysDims (PhysicalDimension PhysicalDimension) Bool)

(assert (forall ((pd1 PhysicalDimension) (pd2 PhysicalDimension))
    (=> (compatiblePhysDims pd1 pd2)
        (and
            ;; All exponents identical
            (= (lengthExp pd1) (lengthExp pd2))
            (= (massExp pd1) (massExp pd2))
            (= (timeExp pd1) (timeExp pd2))
            (= (currentExp pd1) (currentExp pd2))
            (= (temperatureExp pd1) (temperatureExp pd2))
            (= (molarAmountExp pd1) (molarAmountExp pd2))
            (= (luminousIntensityExp pd1) (luminousIntensityExp pd2))
            ;; Same shortName or mapping exists
            (or (= (physDimShortName pd1) (physDimShortName pd2))
                (physDimMappingExists pd1 pd2))))))

;; Category-based mapping constraints (constr_1055-1066)
(declare-fun baseTypeCompatible (SwBaseType SwBaseType) Bool)
(declare-fun applToImplMapping (ApplicationDataType ImplementationDataType) Bool)

;; constr_1055: VALUE category baseType compatibility
(assert (forall ((impl ImplementationDataType) (base1 SwBaseType) (base2 SwBaseType))
    (=> (and (= (categoryImpl impl) "VALUE")
             (applToImplMapping impl base1)
             (applToImplMapping impl base2))
        (baseTypeCompatible base1 base2))))

;; constr_1059: VALUE category Application to Implementation mapping
(assert (forall ((appl ApplicationDataType) (impl ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (and (= (category appl) "VALUE") (applToImplMapping appl impl))
        (= (categoryImpl impl) "VALUE"))))

;; constr_1060: ARRAY/VAL_BLK mapping to ARRAY
(assert (forall ((appl ApplicationDataType) (impl ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (and (or (= (category appl) "ARRAY") (= (category appl) "VAL_BLK"))
             (applToImplMapping appl impl))
        (= (categoryImpl impl) "ARRAY"))))

;; constr_1061: STRUCTURE mapping
(assert (forall ((appl ApplicationDataType) (impl ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (and (= (category appl) "STRUCTURE") (applToImplMapping appl impl))
        (= (categoryImpl impl) "STRUCTURE"))))

;; constr_1063: BOOLEAN mapping to VALUE
(assert (forall ((appl ApplicationDataType) (impl ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (and (= (category appl) "BOOLEAN") (applToImplMapping appl impl))
        (= (categoryImpl impl) "VALUE"))))

;; constr_1064: COM_AXIS etc. mapping to STRUCTURE or ARRAY
(assert (forall ((appl ApplicationDataType) (impl ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (and (or (= (category appl) "COM_AXIS") (= (category appl) "RES_AXIS")
                 (= (category appl) "CURVE") (= (category appl) "MAP")
                 (= (category appl) "CUBOID") (= (category appl) "CUBE_4")
                 (= (category appl) "CUBE_5"))
             (applToImplMapping appl impl))
        (or (= (categoryImpl impl) "STRUCTURE") (= (categoryImpl impl) "ARRAY")))))

;; constr_1066: Forbidden Implementation categories
(assert (forall ((appl ApplicationDataType) (impl ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (applToImplMapping appl impl)
        (not (or (= (categoryImpl impl) "UNION")
                 (= (categoryImpl impl) "DATA_REFERENCE")
                 (= (categoryImpl impl) "FUNCTION_REFERENCE"))))))

;; DataConstr functions (constr_1126)
(declare-fun providerConstr (DataElement) DataConstr)
(declare-fun requirerConstr (DataElement) DataConstr)
(declare-fun constrLowerLimit (DataConstr) Real)
(declare-fun constrUpperLimit (DataConstr) Real)
(declare-fun hasConstr (DataElement) Bool)
(declare-fun providesTo (DataElement DataElement) Bool)

;; constr_1126: Provider constraints within requirer constraints
(assert (forall ((provider DataElement) (requirer DataElement))
    (=> (and (providesTo provider requirer)
             (hasConstr provider) (hasConstr requirer))
        (let ((pConstr (providerConstr provider))
              (rConstr (requirerConstr requirer)))
            (and (<= (constrLowerLimit rConstr) (constrLowerLimit pConstr))
                 (<= (constrUpperLimit pConstr) (constrUpperLimit rConstr)))))))

;; Queue length functions (constr_1128)
(declare-fun referencesRunnable (OperationInvokedEvent RunnableEntity) Bool)
(declare-fun eventReferencesOperation (OperationInvokedEvent ClientServerOperation) Bool)
(declare-fun comSpecForOperation (ServerComSpec ClientServerOperation) Bool)
(declare-fun queueLength (ServerComSpec) Int)

;; constr_1128: Identical queue length for operations with same runnable
(assert (forall ((event1 OperationInvokedEvent) (event2 OperationInvokedEvent) 
                 (runnable RunnableEntity) (op1 ClientServerOperation) (op2 ClientServerOperation)
                 (spec1 ServerComSpec) (spec2 ServerComSpec))
    (=> (and (referencesRunnable event1 runnable) (referencesRunnable event2 runnable)
             (eventReferencesOperation event1 op1) (eventReferencesOperation event2 op2)
             (comSpecForOperation spec1 op1) (comSpecForOperation spec2 op2)
             (not (= event1 event2)))
        (= (queueLength spec1) (queueLength spec2)))))

;; swImplPolicy functions (constr_1129-1132)
(declare-fun comSpecReferencesDataElement (NonqueuedReceiverComSpec VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun comSpecReferencesDataElementQ (QueuedReceiverComSpec VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun comSpecReferencesDataElementNS (NonqueuedSenderComSpec VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun comSpecReferencesDataElementQS (QueuedSenderComSpec VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun swImplPolicy (VariableDataPrototype) String)

;; constr_1129: NonqueuedReceiverComSpec - swImplPolicy not queued
(assert (forall ((comSpec NonqueuedReceiverComSpec) (dataElem VariableDataPrototype))
    (=> (comSpecReferencesDataElement comSpec dataElem)
        (not (= (swImplPolicy dataElem) "queued")))))

;; constr_1130: QueuedReceiverComSpec - swImplPolicy must be queued
(assert (forall ((comSpec QueuedReceiverComSpec) (dataElem VariableDataPrototype))
    (=> (comSpecReferencesDataElementQ comSpec dataElem)
        (= (swImplPolicy dataElem) "queued"))))

;; constr_1131: NonqueuedSenderComSpec - swImplPolicy not queued
(assert (forall ((comSpec NonqueuedSenderComSpec) (dataElem VariableDataPrototype))
    (=> (comSpecReferencesDataElementNS comSpec dataElem)
        (not (= (swImplPolicy dataElem) "queued")))))

;; constr_1132: QueuedSenderComSpec - swImplPolicy must be queued
(assert (forall ((comSpec QueuedSenderComSpec) (dataElem VariableDataPrototype))
    (=> (comSpecReferencesDataElementQS comSpec dataElem)
        (= (swImplPolicy dataElem) "queued"))))

;; CompuScale symbolic name functions (constr_1133)
(declare-fun symbolicName (CompuScale) String)
(declare-fun scaleLowerLimit (CompuScale) Real)
(declare-fun scaleUpperLimit (CompuScale) Real)
(declare-fun scaleInMethod (CompuScale CompuMethod) Bool)
(declare-fun compuMethodCategory (CompuMethod) String)

;; constr_1133: Identical symbolic names must have same range
(assert (forall ((scale1 CompuScale) (scale2 CompuScale) (method CompuMethod))
    (=> (and (scaleInMethod scale1 method) (scaleInMethod scale2 method)
             (= (symbolicName scale1) (symbolicName scale2))
             (not (= scale1 scale2)))
        (and (= (scaleLowerLimit scale1) (scaleLowerLimit scale2))
             (= (scaleUpperLimit scale1) (scaleUpperLimit scale2))))))

;; BITFIELD_TEXTTABLE vt constraint (constr_1135)
(declare-fun vtValue (CompuScale) String)
(declare-fun containsPipe (String) Bool)

;; constr_1135: vt cannot contain "|" in BITFIELD_TEXTTABLE
(assert (forall ((scale CompuScale) (method CompuMethod))
    (=> (and (scaleInMethod scale method)
             (= (compuMethodCategory method) "BITFIELD_TEXTTABLE"))
        (not (containsPipe (vtValue scale))))))

;; ParameterInterface ownership (constr_1137)
(declare-fun portTypedBy (PPortPrototype PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun isParameterInterface (PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun ownedByComponent (PPortPrototype SwComponentType) Bool)
(declare-fun isParameterSwComponentType (SwComponentType) Bool)

;; constr_1137: ParameterInterface PPort only in ParameterSwComponentType
(assert (forall ((port PPortPrototype) (iface PortInterface) (comp SwComponentType))
    (=> (and (portTypedBy port iface) (isParameterInterface iface)
             (ownedByComponent port comp))
        (isParameterSwComponentType comp))))

;; DiagEventDebounceMonitorInternal functions (constr_1138-1139)
(declare-fun hasDebounceAlgorithm (DiagnosticEventNeeds DiagEventDebounceAlgorithm) Bool)
(declare-fun hasAssignedPort (DiagnosticEventNeeds RoleBasedPortAssignment) Bool)
(declare-fun isMonitorInternal (DiagEventDebounceAlgorithm) Bool)
(declare-fun assignedPortRole (RoleBasedPortAssignment) String)
(declare-fun portAssignmentReferencesRPort (RoleBasedPortAssignment) Bool)

;; constr_1138: assignedPort only with DiagEventDebounceMonitorInternal
(assert (forall ((needs DiagnosticEventNeeds) (algo DiagEventDebounceAlgorithm) (port RoleBasedPortAssignment))
    (=> (and (hasDebounceAlgorithm needs algo) (hasAssignedPort needs port))
        (isMonitorInternal algo))))

;; constr_1139: assignedPort refers to RPortPrototype with specific role
(assert (forall ((needs DiagnosticEventNeeds) (algo DiagEventDebounceAlgorithm) (port RoleBasedPortAssignment))
    (=> (and (hasDebounceAlgorithm needs algo) (isMonitorInternal algo)
             (hasAssignedPort needs port))
        (and (portAssignmentReferencesRPort port)
             (= (assignedPortRole port) "DiagFaultDetectionCounterPort")))))

;; invalidValue and handleInvalid (constr_1140)
(declare-fun handleInvalid (InvalidationPolicy) String)
(declare-fun initValue (NonqueuedReceiverComSpec) Real)
(declare-fun invalidValue (VariableDataPrototype) Real)
(declare-fun policyForInterface (InvalidationPolicy PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun comSpecForDataElement (NonqueuedReceiverComSpec VariableDataPrototype) Bool)

;; constr_1140: handleInvalid=replace and initValue=invalidValue not supported
(assert (forall ((policy InvalidationPolicy) (comSpec NonqueuedReceiverComSpec) 
                 (dataElem VariableDataPrototype))
    (=> (comSpecForDataElement comSpec dataElem)
        (not (and (= (handleInvalid policy) "replace")
                  (= (initValue comSpec) (invalidValue dataElem)))))))

;; CompuScale symbol applicability (constr_1146)
(declare-fun hasSymbol (CompuScale) Bool)

;; constr_1146: symbol only for specific categories
(assert (forall ((scale CompuScale) (method CompuMethod))
    (=> (and (scaleInMethod scale method) (hasSymbol scale))
        (or (= (compuMethodCategory method) "SCALE_LINEAR_AND_TEXTTABLE")
            (= (compuMethodCategory method) "SCALE_RATIONAL_AND_TEXTTABLE")
            (= (compuMethodCategory method) "TEXTTABLE")
            (= (compuMethodCategory method) "BITFIELD_TEXTTABLE")))))

;; NvBlockSwComponentType isService constraint (constr_1148)
(declare-fun portInterfaceForNvBlock (PortInterface NvBlockSwComponentType) Bool)
(declare-fun isService (PortInterface) Bool)

;; constr_1148: isService must be false for NvBlock interfaces
(assert (forall ((iface PortInterface) (nvBlock NvBlockSwComponentType))
    (=> (portInterfaceForNvBlock iface nvBlock)
        (not (isService iface)))))

;; PortDefinedArgumentValue valueType (constr_1150)
(declare-fun valueTypeCategory (PortDefinedArgumentValue) String)
(declare-fun valueTypeTargetCategory (PortDefinedArgumentValue) String)

;; constr_1150: valueType category must be VALUE or TYPE_REFERENCE (with target VALUE)
(assert (forall ((pdav PortDefinedArgumentValue))
    (or (= (valueTypeCategory pdav) "VALUE")
        (and (= (valueTypeCategory pdav) "TYPE_REFERENCE")
             (= (valueTypeTargetCategory pdav) "VALUE")))))

;; PortInterfaceMapping applicability (constr_1151)
(declare-sort SwConnector 0)
(declare-sort PortInterfaceMapping 0)
(declare-fun connectorPort1 (SwConnector) PPortPrototype)
(declare-fun connectorPort2 (SwConnector) RPortPrototype)
(declare-fun mappingInterface1 (PortInterfaceMapping) PortInterface)
(declare-fun mappingInterface2 (PortInterfaceMapping) PortInterface)
(declare-fun mappingAppliedToConnector (PortInterfaceMapping SwConnector) Bool)

;; constr_1151: PortInterfaceMapping validity
(assert (forall ((mapping PortInterfaceMapping) (conn SwConnector) 
                 (iface1 PortInterface) (iface2 PortInterface))
    (=> (and (mappingAppliedToConnector mapping conn)
             (= (mappingInterface1 mapping) iface1)
             (= (mappingInterface2 mapping) iface2))
        (and (portTypedBy (connectorPort1 conn) iface1)
             (or (portTypedBy (connectorPort1 conn) iface2)
                 (exists ((port2 RPortPrototype))
                     (and (= (connectorPort2 conn) port2)
                          (or (portTypedBy (connectorPort1 conn) iface1)
                              (portTypedBy (connectorPort1 conn) iface2)))))))))

;; ApplicationArrayElement category sync (constr_1152)
(declare-fun arrayElementCategory (ApplicationArrayElement) String)
(declare-fun arrayElementTypeCategory (ApplicationArrayElement) String)

;; constr_1152: ApplicationArrayElement category matches type category
(assert (forall ((elem ApplicationArrayElement))
    (= (arrayElementCategory elem) (arrayElementTypeCategory elem))))

;; CompuScale compatibility for sender-receiver (constr_1154)
(declare-fun providerCompuScales (PPortPrototype) (Set CompuScale))
(declare-fun requirerCompuScales (RPortPrototype) (Set CompuScale))
(declare-fun connectedPorts (PPortPrototype RPortPrototype) Bool)

;; constr_1154: Provider CompuScales subset of requirer CompuScales
(assert (forall ((pPort PPortPrototype) (rPort RPortPrototype))
    (=> (connectedPorts pPort rPort)
        (forall ((scale CompuScale))
            (=> (set.member scale (providerCompuScales pPort))
                (set.member scale (requirerCompuScales rPort)))))))

;; CompuScale compatibility for client-server (constr_1155)
(declare-fun argumentDirection (ArgumentDataPrototype) String)
(declare-fun clientCompuScales (RPortPrototype ArgumentDataPrototype) (Set CompuScale))
(declare-fun serverCompuScales (PPortPrototype ArgumentDataPrototype) (Set CompuScale))
(declare-fun argumentInOperation (ArgumentDataPrototype ClientServerOperation) Bool)
(declare-fun operationConnected (ClientServerOperation PPortPrototype RPortPrototype) Bool)

;; constr_1155: CompuScale compatibility based on argument direction
(assert (forall ((arg ArgumentDataPrototype) (op ClientServerOperation) 
                 (pPort PPortPrototype) (rPort RPortPrototype))
    (=> (and (argumentInOperation arg op) (operationConnected op pPort rPort))
        (let ((dir (argumentDirection arg))
              (cScales (clientCompuScales rPort arg))
              (sScales (serverCompuScales pPort arg)))
            (or
                ;; IN: client subset of server
                (and (= dir "IN")
                     (forall ((scale CompuScale))
                         (=> (set.member scale cScales)
                             (set.member scale sScales))))
                ;; OUT: server subset of client
                (and (= dir "OUT")
                     (forall ((scale CompuScale))
                         (=> (set.member scale sScales)
                             (set.member scale cScales))))
                ;; INOUT: identical
                (and (= dir "INOUT")
                     (forall ((scale CompuScale))
                         (and (=> (set.member scale cScales) (set.member scale sScales))
                              (=> (set.member scale sScales) (set.member scale cScales))))))))))

;; EndToEndProtection sender consistency (constr_1183)
(declare-fun aggregatedBy (EndToEndProtectionVariablePrototype EndToEndProtection) Bool)
(declare-fun senderOf (EndToEndProtectionVariablePrototype) Sender)

(assert (forall ((proto1 EndToEndProtectionVariablePrototype) 
                 (proto2 EndToEndProtectionVariablePrototype)
                 (protection EndToEndProtection))
    (=> (and (aggregatedBy proto1 protection) (aggregatedBy proto2 protection))
        (= (senderOf proto1) (senderOf proto2)))))

;; Data type consistency (constr_1185)
(declare-fun rootDataPrototypeType (ApplicationCompositeElementInPortInterfaceInstanceRef) AutosarDataType)
(declare-fun contextEnclosedIn (DataPrototype AutosarDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun targetEnclosedIn (DataPrototype AutosarDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun contextDataPrototypeRef (ApplicationCompositeElementInPortInterfaceInstanceRef Int) DataPrototype)
(declare-fun targetDataPrototypeRef (ApplicationCompositeElementInPortInterfaceInstanceRef) DataPrototype)
(declare-fun numContextProtos (ApplicationCompositeElementInPortInterfaceInstanceRef) Int)

(assert (forall ((iref ApplicationCompositeElementInPortInterfaceInstanceRef))
    (let ((rootType (rootDataPrototypeType iref)))
        (and
            (forall ((i Int))
                (=> (and (>= i 0) (< i (numContextProtos iref)))
                    (contextEnclosedIn (contextDataPrototypeRef iref i) rootType)))
            (targetEnclosedIn (targetDataPrototypeRef iref) rootType)))))

;; Data type consistency for Implementation (constr_1186)
(declare-fun rootVariableDataPrototypeType (ArVariableInImplementationDataInstanceRef) ImplementationDataType)
(declare-fun implContextEnclosedIn (DataPrototype ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun implTargetEnclosedIn (DataPrototype ImplementationDataType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((iref ArVariableInImplementationDataInstanceRef))
    (let ((rootType (rootVariableDataPrototypeType iref)))
        (and
            (forall ((i Int))
                (=> (and (>= i 0) (< i (numContextProtos iref)))
                    (implContextEnclosedIn (contextDataPrototypeRef iref i) rootType)))
            (implTargetEnclosedIn (targetDataPrototypeRef iref) rootType)))))

;; Composite data type compatibility (constr_1187)
(declare-fun compositeDataTypesIdentical (ApplicationCompositeDataType ApplicationCompositeDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun sameNumberOfElements (ApplicationCompositeDataType ApplicationCompositeDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun elementsCompatibleInOrder (ApplicationCompositeDataType ApplicationCompositeDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun attributesMatchExceptShortName (ApplicationCompositeDataType ApplicationCompositeDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun subElementMappingExists (DataPrototype DataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun compatibleCompositeDataPrototypes (DataPrototype DataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun dataPrototypeTypedBy (DataPrototype ApplicationCompositeDataType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((proto1 DataPrototype) (proto2 DataPrototype) 
                 (type1 ApplicationCompositeDataType) (type2 ApplicationCompositeDataType))
    (=> (and (dataPrototypeTypedBy proto1 type1) (dataPrototypeTypedBy proto2 type2))
        (=> (compatibleCompositeDataPrototypes proto1 proto2)
            (or
                ;; Condition 1: Identical types
                (compositeDataTypesIdentical type1 type2)
                ;; Condition 2: Same structure
                (and (sameNumberOfElements type1 type2)
                     (elementsCompatibleInOrder type1 type2)
                     (attributesMatchExceptShortName type1 type2))
                ;; Condition 3 & 4: SubElementMapping exists
                (subElementMappingExists proto1 proto2))))))

;; ReceiverComSpec.replaceWith existence (constr_1188)
(declare-fun handleOutOfRange (ReceiverComSpec) String)
(declare-fun hasReplaceWith (ReceiverComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun comSpecForInterface (ReceiverComSpec SenderReceiverInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun invalidationPolicyHandleInvalid (InvalidationPolicy) String)
(declare-fun interfaceHasPolicy (SenderReceiverInterface InvalidationPolicy) Bool)

(assert (forall ((comSpec ReceiverComSpec) (iface SenderReceiverInterface))
    (=> (comSpecForInterface comSpec iface)
        (= (hasReplaceWith comSpec)
           (or (= (handleOutOfRange comSpec) "externalReplacement")
               (exists ((policy InvalidationPolicy))
                   (and (interfaceHasPolicy iface policy)
                        (= (invalidationPolicyHandleInvalid policy) "externalReplacement"))))))))

;; CompuMethod compatibility (constr_1192)
(declare-fun compuMethodCategory (CompuMethod) String)
(declare-fun coefficientN0 (CompuScale) Real)
(declare-fun coefficientN1 (CompuScale) Real)
(declare-fun coefficientD0 (CompuScale) Real)
(declare-fun coefficientD1 (CompuScale) Real)
(declare-fun compuScaleCompatibleIdenticalToRatFunc (CompuScale CompuScale) Bool)
(declare-fun scaleInCompuMethod (CompuScale CompuMethod) Bool)

(assert (forall ((scale1 CompuScale) (scale2 CompuScale) 
                 (method1 CompuMethod) (method2 CompuMethod))
    (=> (and (scaleInCompuMethod scale1 method1) (scaleInCompuMethod scale2 method2)
             (= (compuMethodCategory method1) "IDENTICAL")
             (or (= (compuMethodCategory method2) "RAT_FUNC") 
                 (= (compuMethodCategory method2) "LINEAR")))
        (=> (compuScaleCompatibleIdenticalToRatFunc scale1 scale2)
            (and (< (abs (coefficientN0 scale1)) 0.001)
                 (< (abs (- (coefficientD0 scale1) 1.0)) 0.001)
                 (< (abs (- (coefficientN1 scale1) 1.0)) 0.001)
                 (< (abs (coefficientD1 scale1)) 0.001))))))

;; ModeDeclaration referenced by ModeTransition (constr_1193)
(declare-fun modeInGroup (ModeDeclaration ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun transitionInGroup (ModeTransition ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun transitionEntersMode (ModeTransition ModeDeclaration) Bool)
(declare-fun isInitialMode (ModeDeclaration ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun groupHasTransitions (ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)

(assert (forall ((mode ModeDeclaration) (group ModeDeclarationGroup))
    (=> (and (modeInGroup mode group) 
             (groupHasTransitions group)
             (not (isInitialMode mode group)))
        (exists ((trans ModeTransition))
            (and (transitionInGroup trans group)
                 (transitionEntersMode trans mode))))))

;; Identical ModeTransitions (constr_1194)
(declare-fun transitionShortName (ModeTransition) String)
(declare-fun modeShortName (ModeDeclaration) String)
(declare-fun transitionExitsMode (ModeTransition ModeDeclaration) Bool)
(declare-fun identicalTransitions (ModeDeclarationGroup ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)

(assert (forall ((group1 ModeDeclarationGroup) (group2 ModeDeclarationGroup))
    (=> (identicalTransitions group1 group2)
        (and
            ;; Condition 1: Same shortNames exist
            (forall ((trans1 ModeTransition))
                (=> (transitionInGroup trans1 group1)
                    (exists ((trans2 ModeTransition))
                        (and (transitionInGroup trans2 group2)
                             (= (transitionShortName trans1) (transitionShortName trans2))))))
            ;; Condition 2: Matching transitions have same targets
            (forall ((trans1 ModeTransition) (trans2 ModeTransition))
                (=> (and (transitionInGroup trans1 group1) (transitionInGroup trans2 group2)
                         (= (transitionShortName trans1) (transitionShortName trans2)))
                    (exists ((entered1 ModeDeclaration) (entered2 ModeDeclaration)
                            (exited1 ModeDeclaration) (exited2 ModeDeclaration))
                        (and (transitionEntersMode trans1 entered1)
                             (transitionEntersMode trans2 entered2)
                             (transitionExitsMode trans1 exited1)
                             (transitionExitsMode trans2 exited2)
                             (= (modeShortName entered1) (modeShortName entered2))
                             (= (modeShortName exited1) (modeShortName exited2))))))))))

;; SwcModeSwitchEvent and ModeTransition (constr_1195)
(declare-fun eventActivation (SwcModeSwitchEvent) String)
(declare-fun eventReferencesModePair (SwcModeSwitchEvent ModeDeclaration ModeDeclaration) Bool)
(declare-fun transitionExists (ModeDeclarationGroup ModeDeclaration ModeDeclaration) Bool)

(assert (forall ((event SwcModeSwitchEvent) (group ModeDeclarationGroup)
                 (exitedMode ModeDeclaration) (enteredMode ModeDeclaration))
    (=> (and (= (eventActivation event) "onTransition")
             (eventReferencesModePair event exitedMode enteredMode)
             (groupHasTransitions group))
        (transitionExists group exitedMode enteredMode))))

;; Network representation mutual exclusion (constr_1196)
(declare-fun hasNetworkRepresentation (ReceiverComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun hasCompositeNetworkRepresentation (ReceiverComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun hasNetworkRepresentationS (SenderComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun hasCompositeNetworkRepresentationS (SenderComSpec) Bool)

(assert (forall ((comSpec ReceiverComSpec))
    (not (and (hasNetworkRepresentation comSpec) 
              (hasCompositeNetworkRepresentation comSpec)))))

(assert (forall ((comSpec SenderComSpec))
    (not (and (hasNetworkRepresentationS comSpec) 
              (hasCompositeNetworkRepresentationS comSpec)))))

;; Comprehensive compositeNetworkRepresentation (constr_1197)
(declare-fun leafElementOf (DataPrototype ApplicationCompositeDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun compositeNetworkRepForElement (ReceiverComSpec DataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun hasAnyCompositeNetworkRep (ReceiverComSpec ApplicationCompositeDataType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((comSpec ReceiverComSpec) (compType ApplicationCompositeDataType))
    (=> (hasAnyCompositeNetworkRep comSpec compType)
        (forall ((elem DataPrototype))
            (=> (leafElementOf elem compType)
                (compositeNetworkRepForElement comSpec elem))))))

;; PRPortPrototype queued constraint (constr_1200)
(declare-fun dataElementOwnedBy (VariableDataPrototype PRPortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun dataElementSwImplPolicy (VariableDataPrototype) String)

(assert (forall ((dataElem VariableDataPrototype) (prPort PRPortPrototype))
    (=> (dataElementOwnedBy dataElem prPort)
        (not (= (dataElementSwImplPolicy dataElem) "queued")))))

;; initValue in RPortPrototype (constr_1201)
(declare-fun nonqueuedComSpecInRPort (NonqueuedReceiverComSpec RPortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun hasInitValue (NonqueuedReceiverComSpec) Bool)

(assert (forall ((comSpec NonqueuedReceiverComSpec) (rPort RPortPrototype))
    (=> (nonqueuedComSpecInRPort comSpec rPort)
        (hasInitValue comSpec))))

;; Supported connector connections (constr_1202-1205)
(declare-fun connectorProviderPort (AssemblySwConnector) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun connectorRequesterPort (AssemblySwConnector) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun portTypedByInterface (PortPrototype PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun isSenderReceiverInterface (PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun isNvDataInterface (PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun connectionAllowedBySRTable (PortPrototype PortPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((conn AssemblySwConnector) (iface PortInterface))
    (=> (and (portTypedByInterface (connectorProviderPort conn) iface)
             (or (isSenderReceiverInterface iface) (isNvDataInterface iface)))
        (connectionAllowedBySRTable 
            (connectorProviderPort conn) 
            (connectorRequesterPort conn)))))

;; ModeDeclaration mapping constraints (constr_1209)
(declare-sort ModeDeclarationMapping 0)
(declare-fun mappingUserMode (ModeDeclarationMapping) ModeDeclaration)
(declare-fun mappingManagerMode (ModeDeclarationMapping) ModeDeclaration)
(declare-fun isModeUser (ModeDeclaration) Bool)
(declare-fun isModeManager (ModeDeclaration) Bool)

(assert (forall ((map1 ModeDeclarationMapping) (map2 ModeDeclarationMapping))
    (=> (and (isModeUser (mappingUserMode map1))
             (isModeUser (mappingUserMode map2))
             (isModeManager (mappingManagerMode map1))
             (isModeManager (mappingManagerMode map2)))
        (=> (= (mappingManagerMode map1) (mappingManagerMode map2))
            (= map1 map2)))))

;; Complete mode mapping (constr_1210)
(declare-fun managerModeInGroup (ModeDeclaration ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun mappingExistsForMode (ModeDeclaration) Bool)

(assert (forall ((managerGroup ModeDeclarationGroup))
    (=> (exists ((mode ModeDeclaration) (mapping ModeDeclarationMapping))
            (and (managerModeInGroup mode managerGroup)
                 (= (mappingManagerMode mapping) mode)))
        (forall ((mode ModeDeclaration))
            (=> (managerModeInGroup mode managerGroup)
                (mappingExistsForMode mode))))))

;; Profile-specific value ranges (constr_1211-1213)
(declare-fun e2eCategory (EndToEndDescription) String)
(declare-fun maxNoNewOrRepeatedDataE2E (EndToEndDescription) Int)
(declare-fun maxNoNewOrRepeatedDataRCS (ReceiverComSpec) Int)
(declare-fun syncCounterInitE2E (EndToEndDescription) Int)
(declare-fun syncCounterInitRCS (ReceiverComSpec) Int)

;; constr_1211: PROFILE_01 maxNoNewOrRepeatedData range [0..14]
(assert (forall ((desc EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (= (e2eCategory desc) "PROFILE_01")
        (and (>= (maxNoNewOrRepeatedDataE2E desc) 0)
             (<= (maxNoNewOrRepeatedDataE2E desc) 14)))))

(assert (forall ((comSpec ReceiverComSpec) (desc EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (= (e2eCategory desc) "PROFILE_01")
        (and (>= (maxNoNewOrRepeatedDataRCS comSpec) 0)
             (<= (maxNoNewOrRepeatedDataRCS comSpec) 14)))))

;; constr_1212: PROFILE_01 syncCounterInit range [0..14]
(assert (forall ((desc EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (= (e2eCategory desc) "PROFILE_01")
        (and (>= (syncCounterInitE2E desc) 0)
             (<= (syncCounterInitE2E desc) 14)))))

;; constr_1213: PROFILE_02 maxNoNewOrRepeatedData range [0..15]
(assert (forall ((desc EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (= (e2eCategory desc) "PROFILE_02")
        (and (>= (maxNoNewOrRepeatedDataE2E desc) 0)
             (<= (maxNoNewOrRepeatedDataE2E desc) 15)))))




;; Type declarations
(declare-sort NumericalOrText 0)

(declare-sort ModeDeclarationMappingSet 0)
(declare-sort ModeDeclarationMapping 0)
(declare-sort ModeDeclaration 0)

(declare-sort ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype 0)
(declare-sort ModeDeclarationGroupPrototypeMapping 0)

(declare-sort DataInterface 0)
(declare-sort PortInterface 0)
(declare-sort VariableDataPrototype 0)
(declare-sort ParameterDataPrototype 0)
(declare-sort ModeSwitchInterface 0)
(declare-sort ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype 0)
(declare-sort ClientServerInterface 0)
(declare-sort ClientServerOperation 0)
(declare-sort TriggerInterface 0)
(declare-sort Trigger 0)
(declare-sort PassThroughSwConnector 0)
(declare-sort ApplicationPrimitiveDataType 0)
(declare-sort Unit 0)
(declare-sort PhysicalDimension 0)

(declare-sort PPortPrototype 0)
(declare-sort SenderComSpec 0)
