;; NumericalOrText vf/vt exclusion (constr_1243)
(declare-fun hasVf (NumericalOrText) Bool)
(declare-fun hasVt (NumericalOrText) Bool)

(assert (forall ((not NumericalOrText))
    (or (and (hasVf not) (not (hasVt not)))
        (and (not (hasVf not)) (hasVt not)))))

;; DataPrototype C enum restriction (constr_1244)
(declare-fun prototypeInComponent (DataPrototype AtomicSwComponentType) Bool)
(declare-fun additionalNativeTypeQualifier (DataPrototype) String)

(assert (forall ((proto DataPrototype) (comp AtomicSwComponentType))
    (=> (prototypeInComponent proto comp)
        (not (= (additionalNativeTypeQualifier proto) "enum")))))

;; ModeDeclarationMappingSet consistency (constr_1246)
(declare-fun mappingInSet (ModeDeclarationMapping ModeDeclarationMappingSet) Bool)
(declare-fun firstModeOfMapping (ModeDeclarationMapping Int) ModeDeclaration)
(declare-fun secondModeOfMapping (ModeDeclarationMapping) ModeDeclaration)
(declare-fun modeInGroup (ModeDeclaration ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun numFirstModes (ModeDeclarationMapping) Int)

(assert (forall ((set ModeDeclarationMappingSet))
    (exists ((group1 ModeDeclarationGroup) (group2 ModeDeclarationGroup))
        (and
            ;; All firstModes in one group
            (forall ((map ModeDeclarationMapping))
                (=> (mappingInSet map set)
                    (forall ((i Int))
                        (=> (and (>= i 0) (< i (numFirstModes map)))
                            (modeInGroup (firstModeOfMapping map i) group1)))))
            ;; All secondModes in another group
            (forall ((map ModeDeclarationMapping))
                (=> (mappingInSet map set)
                    (modeInGroup (secondModeOfMapping map) group2)))
            ;; Groups are different
            (not (= group1 group2))))))

;; ModeDeclarationGroupPrototypeMapping consistency (constr_1247)
(declare-fun mappingSetRef (ModeDeclarationGroupPrototypeMapping) ModeDeclarationMappingSet)
(declare-fun firstModeGroupType (ModeDeclarationGroupPrototypeMapping) ModeDeclarationGroup)
(declare-fun secondModeGroupType (ModeDeclarationGroupPrototypeMapping) ModeDeclarationGroup)
(declare-fun hasMappingSet (ModeDeclarationGroupPrototypeMapping) Bool)
(declare-fun setFirstGroup (ModeDeclarationMappingSet) ModeDeclarationGroup)
(declare-fun setSecondGroup (ModeDeclarationMappingSet) ModeDeclarationGroup)

(assert (forall ((protoMap ModeDeclarationGroupPrototypeMapping))
    (=> (hasMappingSet protoMap)
        (let ((set (mappingSetRef protoMap)))
            (and (= (firstModeGroupType protoMap) (setFirstGroup set))
                 (= (secondModeGroupType protoMap) (setSecondGroup set)))))))

;; PassThroughSwConnector compatibility for DataInterfaces (constr_1248)
(declare-fun requiredOuterPort (PassThroughSwConnector) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun providedOuterPort (PassThroughSwConnector) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun portTypedByInterface (PortPrototype PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun isService (PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun dataProtoInInterface (VariableDataPrototype DataInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun dataProtoCompatible (VariableDataPrototype VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun protoShortName (VariableDataPrototype) String)
(declare-fun portInterfaceMappingExists (VariableDataPrototype VariableDataPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((conn PassThroughSwConnector) 
                 (reqIface DataInterface) (provIface DataInterface))
    (=> (and (portTypedByInterface (requiredOuterPort conn) reqIface)
             (portTypedByInterface (providedOuterPort conn) provIface))
        (and
            ;; Condition 1: At least one compatible pair exists
            (exists ((reqProto VariableDataPrototype) (provProto VariableDataPrototype))
                (and (dataProtoInInterface reqProto reqIface)
                     (dataProtoInInterface provProto provIface)
                     (dataProtoCompatible reqProto provProto)
                     (or (= (protoShortName reqProto) (protoShortName provProto))
                         (portInterfaceMappingExists reqProto provProto))))
            ;; Condition 2: isService attributes identical
            (= (isService reqIface) (isService provIface))))))

;; PassThroughSwConnector compatibility for ModeSwitchInterfaces (constr_1249)
(declare-fun modeGroupProtoInInterface (ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype ModeSwitchInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun modeGroupProtoCompatible (ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun modeGroupShortName (ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype) String)
(declare-fun modeInterfaceMappingExists (ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((conn PassThroughSwConnector)
                 (reqIface ModeSwitchInterface) (provIface ModeSwitchInterface))
    (=> (and (portTypedByInterface (requiredOuterPort conn) reqIface)
             (portTypedByInterface (providedOuterPort conn) provIface))
        (and
            (exists ((reqProto ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype) (provProto ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype))
                (and (modeGroupProtoInInterface reqProto reqIface)
                     (modeGroupProtoInInterface provProto provIface)
                     (modeGroupProtoCompatible reqProto provProto)
                     (or (= (modeGroupShortName reqProto) (modeGroupShortName provProto))
                         (modeInterfaceMappingExists reqProto provProto))))
            (= (isService reqIface) (isService provIface))))))

;; PassThroughSwConnector compatibility for ClientServerInterfaces (constr_1250)
(declare-fun operationInInterface (ClientServerOperation ClientServerInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun operationsCompatible (ClientServerOperation ClientServerOperation) Bool)
(declare-fun operationShortName (ClientServerOperation) String)
(declare-fun csInterfaceMappingExists (ClientServerOperation ClientServerOperation) Bool)

(assert (forall ((conn PassThroughSwConnector)
                 (reqIface ClientServerInterface) (provIface ClientServerInterface))
    (=> (and (portTypedByInterface (requiredOuterPort conn) reqIface)
             (portTypedByInterface (providedOuterPort conn) provIface))
        (and
            (exists ((reqOp ClientServerOperation) (provOp ClientServerOperation))
                (and (operationInInterface reqOp reqIface)
                     (operationInInterface provOp provIface)
                     (operationsCompatible reqOp provOp)
                     (or (= (operationShortName reqOp) (operationShortName provOp))
                         (csInterfaceMappingExists reqOp provOp))))
            (= (isService reqIface) (isService provIface))))))

;; PassThroughSwConnector compatibility for TriggerInterfaces (constr_1251)
(declare-fun triggerInInterface (Trigger TriggerInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun triggersCompatible (Trigger Trigger) Bool)
(declare-fun triggerShortName (Trigger) String)

(assert (forall ((conn PassThroughSwConnector)
                 (reqIface TriggerInterface) (provIface TriggerInterface))
    (=> (and (portTypedByInterface (requiredOuterPort conn) reqIface)
             (portTypedByInterface (providedOuterPort conn) provIface))
        (and
            (exists ((reqTrig Trigger) (provTrig Trigger))
                (and (triggerInInterface reqTrig reqIface)
                     (triggerInInterface provTrig provIface)
                     (triggersCompatible reqTrig provTrig)
                     (= (triggerShortName reqTrig) (triggerShortName provTrig))))
            (= (isService reqIface) (isService provIface))))))

;; PassThroughSwConnector loop prevention (constr_1252)
(declare-sort SwConnector 0)
(declare-fun connectorChainReaches (PortPrototype PortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun atomicSwComponentInChain (PortPrototype PortPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((conn PassThroughSwConnector))
    (=> (connectorChainReaches (requiredOuterPort conn) (providedOuterPort conn))
        (atomicSwComponentInChain (requiredOuterPort conn) (providedOuterPort conn)))))

;; Unit restriction for BOOLEAN and STRING (constr_1255)
(declare-fun adtCategory (ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) String)
(declare-fun unitReferencedBy (Unit ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun unitPhysicalDimension (Unit) PhysicalDimension)
(declare-fun lengthExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun massExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun timeExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun currentExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun temperatureExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun molarAmountExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun luminousIntensityExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)

(assert (forall ((adt ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) (unit Unit) (physDim PhysicalDimension))
    (=> (and (or (= (adtCategory adt) "BOOLEAN") (= (adtCategory adt) "STRING"))
             (unitReferencedBy unit adt)
             (= (unitPhysicalDimension unit) physDim))
        (and (= (lengthExp physDim) 0)
             (= (massExp physDim) 0)
             (= (timeExp physDim) 0)
             (= (currentExp physDim) 0)
             (= (temperatureExp physDim) 0)
             (= (molarAmountExp physDim) 0)
             (= (luminousIntensityExp physDim) 0)))))

;; Acknowledgement feedback in n:1 writer case (constr_1256)
(declare-fun runnableInBehavior (RunnableEntity SwcInternalBehavior) Bool)
(declare-fun dataSendPointOf (VariableAccess RunnableEntity) Bool)
(declare-fun dataWriteAccessOf (VariableAccess RunnableEntity) Bool)
(declare-fun accessedVariableTarget (VariableAccess) VariableDataPrototype)
(declare-fun accessedVariablePort (VariableAccess) PPortPrototype)
(declare-fun hasTransmissionAcknowledge (SenderComSpec VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun comSpecForPort (SenderComSpec PPortPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((behavior SwcInternalBehavior))
    (forall ((run1 RunnableEntity) (run2 RunnableEntity) 
             (access1 VariableAccess) (access2 VariableAccess))
        (=> (and (runnableInBehavior run1 behavior) (runnableInBehavior run2 behavior)
                 (not (= run1 run2))
                 (or (dataSendPointOf access1 run1) (dataWriteAccessOf access1 run1))
                 (or (dataSendPointOf access2 run2) (dataWriteAccessOf access2 run2))
                 (= (accessedVariableTarget access1) (accessedVariableTarget access2))
                 (exists ((comSpec SenderComSpec))
                     (and (comSpecForPort comSpec (accessedVariablePort access1))
                          (hasTransmissionAcknowledge comSpec (accessedVariableTarget access1)))))
            false))))

;; InitEvent constraints (constr_1257-1260)
(declare-fun eventStartsRunnable (InitEvent RunnableEntity) Bool)
(declare-fun runnableHasWaitPoint (RunnableEntity WaitPoint) Bool)
(declare-fun runnableHasAsyncCallPoint (RunnableEntity AsynchronousServerCallPoint) Bool)
(declare-fun runnableHasAsyncResultPoint (RunnableEntity AsynchronousServerCallResultPoint) Bool)
(declare-fun eventHasDisabledMode (InitEvent ModeDeclaration) Bool)
(declare-fun runnableMinimumStartInterval (RunnableEntity) Real)

;; constr_1257: No WaitPoints
(assert (forall ((event InitEvent) (runnable RunnableEntity) (wp WaitPoint))
    (=> (eventStartsRunnable event runnable)
        (not (runnableHasWaitPoint runnable wp)))))

;; constr_1258: minimumStartInterval = 0
(assert (forall ((event InitEvent) (runnable RunnableEntity))
    (=> (eventStartsRunnable event runnable)
        (= (runnableMinimumStartInterval runnable) 0.0))))

;; constr_1259: No AsynchronousServerCallResultPoint
(assert (forall ((event InitEvent) (runnable RunnableEntity) (resultPoint AsynchronousServerCallResultPoint))
    (=> (eventStartsRunnable event runnable)
        (not (runnableHasAsyncResultPoint runnable resultPoint)))))

;; constr_1260: No disabledMode
(assert (forall ((event InitEvent) (mode ModeDeclaration))
    (not (eventHasDisabledMode event mode))))

;; EndToEndDescription constraints (constr_1261)
(declare-fun e2eCategory (EndToEndDescription) String)
(declare-fun dataIdMode (EndToEndDescription) Int)
(declare-fun hasDataIdNibbleOffset (EndToEndDescription) Bool)

(assert (forall ((desc EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (hasDataIdNibbleOffset desc)
        (and (= (dataIdMode desc) 3)
             (= (e2eCategory desc) "PROFILE_01")))))

;; ModeErrorBehavior defaultMode existence (constr_1263)
(declare-fun errorReactionPolicy (ModeErrorBehavior) String)
(declare-fun hasDefaultMode (ModeErrorBehavior) Bool)

(assert (forall ((meb ModeErrorBehavior))
    (=> (= (errorReactionPolicy meb) "defaultMode")
        (hasDefaultMode meb))))

;; SwRecordLayoutV index constraint (constr_1264)
(declare-fun swRecordLayoutVCategory (SwRecordLayoutV) String)
(declare-fun swRecordLayoutVIndex (SwRecordLayoutV) Int)

(assert (forall ((layout SwRecordLayoutV))
    (=> (and (not (= (swRecordLayoutVCategory layout) "VALUE"))
             (not (= (swRecordLayoutVCategory layout) "VAL_BLK")))
        (not (= (swRecordLayoutVIndex layout) 0)))))

;; ClientServerOperationMapping constraints (constr_1268-1270)
(declare-fun firstOperationOf (ClientServerOperationMapping) ClientServerOperation)
(declare-fun secondOperationOf (ClientServerOperationMapping) ClientServerOperation)
(declare-fun argumentDirection (ArgumentDataPrototype) String)
(declare-fun argumentsInOperation (ClientServerOperation) Int)
(declare-fun mappingHasArgumentMapping (ClientServerOperationMapping DataPrototypeMapping) Bool)
(declare-fun mappingFirstProto (DataPrototypeMapping) ArgumentDataPrototype)
(declare-fun mappingSecondProto (DataPrototypeMapping) ArgumentDataPrototype)

;; constr_1268: Direction preservation
(assert (forall ((opMap ClientServerOperationMapping) (argMap DataPrototypeMapping))
    (=> (mappingHasArgumentMapping opMap argMap)
        (= (argumentDirection (mappingFirstProto argMap))
           (argumentDirection (mappingSecondProto argMap))))))

;; constr_1269: Same number of arguments
(assert (forall ((opMap ClientServerOperationMapping))
    (= (argumentsInOperation (firstOperationOf opMap))
       (argumentsInOperation (secondOperationOf opMap)))))

;; constr_1270: Each argument mapped once
(assert (forall ((opMap ClientServerOperationMapping) (arg ArgumentDataPrototype))
    (let ((count (ite (exists ((map DataPrototypeMapping))
                          (and (mappingHasArgumentMapping opMap map)
                               (or (= (mappingFirstProto map) arg)
                                   (= (mappingSecondProto map) arg))))
                      1 0)))
        (<= count 1))))

(declare-sort AutosarVariableRef 0)

;; Dynamic array size profile (constr_1322)
(declare-fun idtCategory (ImplementationDataType) String)
(declare-fun idtHasDynamicArraySizeProfile (ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun adtHasDynamicArraySizeProfile (ApplicationArrayDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun idtMappedToAdt (ImplementationDataType ApplicationArrayDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun representsSizeIndicatorArray (ImplementationDataType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((idt ImplementationDataType) (adt ApplicationArrayDataType))
    (=> (and (not (idtHasDynamicArraySizeProfile idt))
             (idtMappedToAdt idt adt)
             (adtHasDynamicArraySizeProfile adt))
        (and (= (idtCategory idt) "STRUCTURE")
             (representsSizeIndicatorArray idt)))))

;; ReceiverComSpec usesEndToEndProtection (constr_1323)
(declare-fun usesEndToEndProtection (ReceiverComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun hasTransformationComSpecProps (ReceiverComSpec) Bool)

(assert (forall ((comSpec ReceiverComSpec))
    (=> (hasTransformationComSpecProps comSpec)
        (not (usesEndToEndProtection comSpec)))))

;; DiagnosticValueNeeds attributes (constr_1363)
(declare-fun diagValueNeedsInServiceDep (DiagnosticValueNeeds SwcServiceDependency) Bool)
(declare-fun hasDataLength (DiagnosticValueNeeds) Bool)
(declare-fun hasDiagnosticValueAccess (DiagnosticValueNeeds) Bool)

(assert (forall ((needs DiagnosticValueNeeds) (dep SwcServiceDependency))
    (=> (diagValueNeedsInServiceDep needs dep)
        (and (not (hasDataLength needs))
             (not (hasDiagnosticValueAccess needs))))))

;; DiagnosticIoControlNeeds attributes (constr_1364)
(declare-fun diagIoControlNeedsInServiceDep (DiagnosticIoControlNeeds SwcServiceDependency) Bool)
(declare-fun hasFreezeCurrentStateSupported (DiagnosticIoControlNeeds) Bool)
(declare-fun hasShortTermAdjustmentSupported (DiagnosticIoControlNeeds) Bool)

(assert (forall ((needs DiagnosticIoControlNeeds) (dep SwcServiceDependency))
    (=> (diagIoControlNeedsInServiceDep needs dep)
        (and (not (hasFreezeCurrentStateSupported needs))
             (not (hasShortTermAdjustmentSupported needs))))))

;; MemorySection/SwAddrMethod option cardinality (constr_1381)
(declare-fun memorySectionOptions (MemorySection) (Set String))
(declare-fun swAddrMethodOptions (SwAddrMethod) (Set String))

(assert (forall ((ms MemorySection))
    (let ((opts (memorySectionOptions ms)))
        (let ((hasCoreGlobal (set.member "coreGlobal" opts))
              (hasCoreLocal (set.member "coreLocal" opts)))
            (not (and hasCoreGlobal hasCoreLocal))))))

(assert (forall ((sam SwAddrMethod))
    (let ((opts (swAddrMethodOptions sam)))
        (let ((hasCoreGlobal (set.member "coreGlobal" opts))
              (hasCoreLocal (set.member "coreLocal" opts)))
            (not (and hasCoreGlobal hasCoreLocal))))))

;; SwVariableRefProxy mutual exclusion (constr_1382)
(declare-fun hasAutosarVariable (SwVariableRefProxy) Bool)
(declare-fun hasMcDataInstanceVar (SwVariableRefProxy) Bool)

(assert (forall ((proxy SwVariableRefProxy))
    (not (and (hasAutosarVariable proxy) (hasMcDataInstanceVar proxy)))))

;; TYPE_REFERENCE CompuMethod/DataConstr existence (constr_1383)
(declare-fun hasCompuMethod (ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun hasDataConstr (ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun resolvedCategory (ImplementationDataType) String)

(assert (forall ((idt ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (and (= (idtCategory idt) "TYPE_REFERENCE")
             (or (hasCompuMethod idt) (hasDataConstr idt)))
        (= (resolvedCategory idt) "VALUE"))))

;; invalidValue restriction for certain categories (constr_1384)
(declare-fun protoTypedByAdt (DataPrototype ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun adtCategory (ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) String)
(declare-fun protoHasInvalidValue (DataPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((proto DataPrototype) (adt ApplicationPrimitiveDataType))
    (=> (and (protoTypedByAdt proto adt)
             (or (= (adtCategory adt) "CURVE")
                 (= (adtCategory adt) "MAP")
                 (= (adtCategory adt) "CUBOID")
                 (= (adtCategory adt) "CUBE_4")
                 (= (adtCategory adt) "CUBE_5")
                 (= (adtCategory adt) "COM_AXIS")
                 (= (adtCategory adt) "RES_AXIS")
                 (= (adtCategory adt) "VAL_BLK")))
        (not (protoHasInvalidValue proto)))))

;; ImplementationDataType initValue restriction (constr_1385)
(declare-fun protoTypedByIdt (DataPrototype ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun initValueIsApplicationValueSpec (DataPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((proto DataPrototype) (idt ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (protoTypedByIdt proto idt)
        (not (initValueIsApplicationValueSpec proto)))))

;; PortDefinedArgumentValue restriction (constr_1386)
(declare-fun portAPIOptionHasArgValue (PortAPIOption PortDefinedArgumentValue) Bool)
(declare-fun portAPIOptionPort (PortAPIOption) AbstractProvidedPortPrototype)
(declare-fun portTypedByCSInterface (AbstractProvidedPortPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((option PortAPIOption) (argValue PortDefinedArgumentValue))
    (=> (portAPIOptionHasArgValue option argValue)
        (portTypedByCSInterface (portAPIOptionPort option)))))

;; VariationPointProxy category VALUE restriction (constr_1388)
(declare-fun vppCategory (VariationPointProxy) String)
(declare-fun vppMixesPrePostBuild (VariationPointProxy) Bool)

(assert (forall ((vpp VariationPointProxy))
    (=> (= (vppCategory vpp) "VALUE")
        (not (vppMixesPrePostBuild vpp)))))

;; VariationPointProxy implementationDataType category restriction (constr_1389)
(declare-fun vppImplementationDataType (VariationPointProxy) ImplementationDataType)
(declare-fun hasVppImplementationDataType (VariationPointProxy) Bool)

(assert (forall ((vpp VariationPointProxy))
    (=> (hasVppImplementationDataType vpp)
        (let ((idt (vppImplementationDataType vpp)))
            (let ((cat (idtCategory idt)))
                (and (not (= cat "STRUCTURE"))
                     (not (= cat "ARRAY"))
                     (not (= cat "UNION"))
                     (not (= cat "FUNCTION_REFERENCE"))
                     (not (= cat "DATA_REFERENCE"))
                     (or (= cat "VALUE")
                         (and (= cat "TYPE_REFERENCE")
                              (= (resolvedCategory idt) "VALUE")))))))))

;; invalidValue vs CompuMethod interval (constr_1390)
(declare-fun handleInvalidValue (InvalidationPolicy) String)
(declare-fun dataElementInvalidValue (VariableDataPrototype) Real)
(declare-fun dataElementCompuMethod (VariableDataPrototype) CompuMethod)
(declare-fun valueInCompuMethodInterval (Real CompuMethod) Bool)
(declare-fun hasInvalidValueDef (VariableDataPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((policy InvalidationPolicy) (dataElem VariableDataPrototype))
    (=> (and (not (= (handleInvalidValue policy) "dontInvalidate"))
             (hasInvalidValueDef dataElem))
        (not (valueInCompuMethodInterval 
              (dataElementInvalidValue dataElem)
              (dataElementCompuMethod dataElem))))))

;; Unit compatibility for ApplicationValueSpecification (constr_1391)
(declare-fun avSpecSwValueContUnit (ApplicationValueSpecification) Unit)
(declare-fun protoDataTypeUnit (AutosarDataPrototype) Unit)
(declare-fun unitsCompatible (Unit Unit) Bool)
(declare-fun assignedToProto (ApplicationValueSpecification AutosarDataPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((avSpec ApplicationValueSpecification) (proto AutosarDataPrototype))
    (=> (assignedToProto avSpec proto)
        (unitsCompatible 
            (avSpecSwValueContUnit avSpec)
            (protoDataTypeUnit proto)))))

;; Unit compatibility for ApplicationRuleBasedValueSpecification (constr_1392)
(declare-fun arbvSpecSwValueContUnit (ApplicationRuleBasedValueSpecification) Unit)
(declare-fun assignedToProtoRuleBased (ApplicationRuleBasedValueSpecification AutosarDataPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((arbvSpec ApplicationRuleBasedValueSpecification) (proto AutosarDataPrototype))
    (=> (assignedToProtoRuleBased arbvSpec proto)
        (unitsCompatible 
            (arbvSpecSwValueContUnit arbvSpec)
            (protoDataTypeUnit proto)))))

;; RuleBasedValueCont.unit existence (constr_1393)
(declare-fun ruleBasedValueContHasUnit (RuleBasedValueCont) Bool)

(assert (forall ((rbvc RuleBasedValueCont))
    (ruleBasedValueContHasUnit rbvc)))

;; NvBlockDataMapping completeness (constr_1395)
(declare-fun nvDataInInterface (VariableDataPrototype NvDataInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun allSubElementsMapped (NvDataInterface) Bool)

(assert (forall ((iface NvDataInterface))
    (=> (exists ((vdp VariableDataPrototype))
            (nvDataInInterface vdp iface))
        (allSubElementsMapped iface))))

;; Variable-Size Array category restriction (constr_1396)
(declare-fun isNonTerminatingElement (ImplementationDataTypeElement) Bool)
(declare-fun modelsVariableSizeArray (ImplementationDataTypeElement) Bool)
(declare-fun elementCategory (ImplementationDataTypeElement) String)

(assert (forall ((elem ImplementationDataTypeElement))
    (=> (and (isNonTerminatingElement elem)
             (modelsVariableSizeArray elem))
        (not (= (elementCategory elem) "TYPE_REFERENCE")))))

;; TransformerHardErrorEvent mutual exclusion (constr_1397)
(declare-fun hasOperation (TransformerHardErrorEvent) Bool)
(declare-fun hasTrigger (TransformerHardErrorEvent) Bool)

(assert (forall ((event TransformerHardErrorEvent))
    (and (or (hasOperation event) (hasTrigger event))
         (not (and (hasOperation event) (hasTrigger event))))))

;; BaseTypeDirectDefinition byteOrder for UTF-16 (constr_1398)
(declare-fun baseTypeEncoding (BaseTypeDirectDefinition) String)
(declare-fun hasByteOrder (BaseTypeDirectDefinition) Bool)
(declare-fun byteOrderValue (BaseTypeDirectDefinition) String)

(assert (forall ((btdd BaseTypeDirectDefinition))
    (=> (= (baseTypeEncoding btdd) "UTF-16")
        (and (hasByteOrder btdd)
             (or (= (byteOrderValue btdd) "mostSignificantByteFirst")
                 (= (byteOrderValue btdd) "mostSignificantByteLast"))))))

;; ModeDeclarationGroup.category standardized values (constr_1399)
(declare-fun modeDeclarationGroupCategory (ModeDeclarationGroup) String)
(declare-fun hasCategoryMDG (ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)

(assert (forall ((mdg ModeDeclarationGroup))
    (=> (hasCategoryMDG mdg)
        (or (= (modeDeclarationGroupCategory mdg) "EXPLICIT_ORDER")
            (= (modeDeclarationGroupCategory mdg) "ALPHABETIC_ORDER")))))

;; DataTransformation reference uniqueness (constr_1400)
(declare-fun dtReferencedByDataProtoMapping (DataTransformation DataPrototypeMapping) Bool)
(declare-fun dtReferencedByISignal (DataTransformation ISignal) Bool)
(declare-fun dtReferencedByISignalGroup (DataTransformation ISignalGroup) Bool)

(assert (forall ((dt DataTransformation))
    (let ((inProtoMapping (exists ((dpm DataPrototypeMapping)) (dtReferencedByDataProtoMapping dt dpm)))
          (inISignal (exists ((isig ISignal)) (dtReferencedByISignal dt isig)))
          (inISignalGroup (exists ((isg ISignalGroup)) (dtReferencedByISignalGroup dt isg))))
        ;; At most one of these can be true
        (or (and inProtoMapping (not inISignal) (not inISignalGroup))
            (and (not inProtoMapping) inISignal (not inISignalGroup))
            (and (not inProtoMapping) (not inISignal) inISignalGroup)
            (and (not inProtoMapping) (not inISignal) (not inISignalGroup))))))

;; DataPrototypeMapping and DataTransformation restrictions (constr_1401)
(declare-fun vdpInPortProto (VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun dataProtoMappingRefsVdp (DataPrototypeMapping VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun dataProtoMappingRefsDataTransformation (DataPrototypeMapping DataTransformation) Bool)
(declare-fun dataMappingPointsToVdp (DataMapping VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun dataMappingRefsISignalWithDT (DataMapping ISignal DataTransformation) Bool)

(assert (forall ((vdp VariableDataPrototype) (dpm DataPrototypeMapping) 
                 (dt1 DataTransformation) (dm DataMapping) 
                 (isig ISignal) (dt2 DataTransformation))
    (=> (and (vdpInPortProto vdp)
             (dataProtoMappingRefsVdp dpm vdp)
             (dataProtoMappingRefsDataTransformation dpm dt1)
             (dataMappingPointsToVdp dm vdp)
             (dataMappingRefsISignalWithDT dm isig dt2))
        false)))

; ########## 约束定义 ##########

; constr_1157: Applicability of constraints of CompuScales
; (Expression: "The constraints [...] shall only apply in the absence of a TextTableMapping...")
; 策略: 这是一个关于约束优先级的元逻辑 (SMT)。我们定义存在性检查。
(declare-const AllTextTableMappings (Set TextTableMapping))
(define-fun existsTextTableMapping () Bool
  (not (= AllTextTableMappings (as set.empty (Set TextTableMapping))))
)
; (无法断言元逻辑，因为它依赖于 [1154, 1155, 1156] 的断言，这会是臆测)

; constr_1160: Size of Compound Primitive Data Type is variant
; (Expression: "size ... initValues shall match the range ... SwSystemconst")
; 策略: 算术比较 (SMT)。但
; (由于缺少属性路径，无法定义断言)

; constr_1161: Applicability of the index attribute of Ref
; (Expression: "index attribute ... is limited to a given set if use cases...")
; 策略: 上下文检查 (SMT)。
(declare-fun isIndexAttributeUsed (AnyInstanceRef) Bool)
(declare-fun isUsedInMcDataInstance (AnyInstanceRef) Bool)
(declare-fun isUsedInAutosarVariableRef (AnyInstanceRef) Bool)
(declare-fun isUsedInAutosarParameterRef (AnyInstanceRef) Bool)
(declare-fun isUsedInFlatInstanceDescriptor (AnyInstanceRef) Bool)
(assert (forall ((r AnyInstanceRef))
  (=> (isIndexAttributeUsed r)
      (or (isUsedInMcDataInstance r)
          (isUsedInAutosarVariableRef r)
          (isUsedInAutosarParameterRef r)
          (isUsedInFlatInstanceDescriptor r)
      )
  )
))

; constr_1162: Compatibility of SwRecordLayouts
; (Expression: "Two SwRecordLayout definitions are compatible if and only if all attributes except [...] are identical.")
; 策略: 跨对象一致性 (SMT)。
(declare-fun areCompatible_1162 (SwRecordLayout SwRecordLayout) Bool)
; (无法定义函数体，因为未提供必须相同的属性列表)

; constr_1163: Compatibility of CompuMethods
; (Expression: "Two CompuMethod definitions are compatible if ... attributes ... are identical and the compuScales and units are compatible.")
; 策略: 跨对象一致性 (SMT)。
(declare-fun areCompuScalesCompatible (CompuScale CompuScale) Bool)
(declare-fun areUnitsCompatible (Unit Unit) Bool)
(declare-fun areCompatible_1163 (CompuMethod CompuMethod) Bool)
; (无法定义函数体，因为未提供必须相同的属性列表)

; constr_1164: Number of arguments owned by a RunnableEntity
; (Expression: "number of ... arguments ... shall be identical to the number of ... portArgValues ... plus the number of ArgumentDataPrototypes...")
; 策略: 算术/计数 (SMT)。
; (规则禁止 set.card，无法对集合进行计数和加法。仅声明相关类型。)

; constr_1165: Applicability of RunnableEntityArgument
; (Expression: "existence of a RunnableEntityArgument is limited to RunnableEntitys triggered by a ClientServerOperation.")
; 策略: 条件存在性 (SMT)。
(declare-fun getArguments_1165 (RunnableEntity) (Set RunnableEntityArgument))
(declare-fun isTriggeredByCSO (RunnableEntity) Bool)
(define-fun hasRunnableEntityArgument_1165 ((re RunnableEntity)) Bool
  (not (= (getArguments_1165 re) (as set.empty (Set RunnableEntityArgument))))
)
(assert (forall ((re RunnableEntity))
  (=> (hasRunnableEntityArgument_1165 re)
      (isTriggeredByCSO re)
  )
))

; constr_1166: Restrictions of ModeRequestTypeMap
; (Expression: "For every ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype... a ModeRequestTypeMap shall exist that points to...")
; 策略: 复杂路径和存在性检查 (SMT)。
; (路径逻辑过于复杂，无法在不臆测的情况下建模。仅声明相关类型。)

; constr_1168: Compatibility of ImplementationDataTypes...
; (Expression: "Both ImplementationDataTypes shall fulfill [constr_1167]. In addition ... range ... shall match...")
; 策略: 引用元约束和范围匹配 (SMT)。
(declare-fun fulfills_constr_1167 (ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun rangeMatches_constr_1075 (ImplementationDataType ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun areCompatible_1168 (ImplementationDataType ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(assert (forall ((idt1 ImplementationDataType) (idt2 ImplementationDataType))
  (= (areCompatible_1168 idt1 idt2)
     (and (fulfills_constr_1167 idt1)
          (fulfills_constr_1167 idt2)
          (rangeMatches_constr_1075 idt1 idt2)
     )
  )
))

; constr_1170: Interpretation of attribute maxDeltaCounterInit owned by EndToEndDescription
; (Expression: "If A... is identical to B... and B... is defined then ... [precedence logic]...")
; 策略: 复杂条件逻辑和优先级 (SMT)。
; (路径和访问器逻辑过于复杂，无法在不臆测的情况下建模。)

; constr_1171: Interpretation of attribute maxDeltaCounterInit of EndToEndDescription
; (Expression: (Similar to 1170, but "PROFILE_02"))
; 策略: 复杂条件逻辑和优先级 (SMT)。
; (路径和访问器逻辑过于复杂，无法在不臆测的情况下建模。)

; constr_1173: Applicability of AutosarParameterRef referencing a VariableDataPrototype
; (Expression: "A reference ... is only applicable if ... used in the context of SwAxisGrouped.")
; 策略: 上下文检查 (SMT)。
(declare-fun refsVariableDataPrototype_1173 (AutosarParameterRef) Bool)
(declare-fun isUsedInSwAxisGrouped (AutosarParameterRef) Bool)
(assert (forall ((apr AutosarParameterRef))
  (=> (refsVariableDataPrototype_1173 apr)
      (isUsedInSwAxisGrouped apr)
  )
))

; constr_1174: PortInterfaces used in the context of CompositionSwComponentTypes cannot refer to AUTOSAR services
; (Expression: "CompositionSwComponentTypes shall not own PortPrototypes typed by PortInterfaces where ... isService is set to true.")
; 策略: 跨对象路径检查 (SMT)。
(declare-fun getOwnedPortPrototypes (CompositionSwComponentType) (Set PortPrototype))
(declare-fun getPortInterface (PortPrototype) PortInterface)
(declare-fun getIsService (PortInterface) Bool)
(assert (forall ((c CompositionSwComponentType) (p PortPrototype))
  (=> (set.member p (getOwnedPortPrototypes c))
      (not (getIsService (getPortInterface p)))
  )
))

; constr_1175: Depending on its category, CompuMethod shall refer to a unit
; (Expression: "...shall refer to a unit unless the CompuMethod's category is one of TEXTTABLE, BITFIELD_TEXTTABLE, or IDENTICAL.")
; 策略: 条件存在性 (SMT)。
(declare-fun getCategory_1175 (CompuMethod) String)
(declare-fun hasUnit (CompuMethod) Bool)
(assert (forall ((cm CompuMethod))
  (=>
    (not (or (= (getCategory_1175 cm) "TEXTTABLE")
             (= (getCategory_1175 cm) "BITFIELD_TEXTTABLE")
             (= (getCategory_1175 cm) "IDENTICAL")
         )
    )
    (hasUnit cm)
  )
))

; constr_1A76: Compatibility of CompuScales of category LINEAR and RAT_FUNC
; (Expression: "compatible if ... `n₀+n₁*phys` / `d₀+d₁*phys` is compatible to `N₀+N₁*phys` / `D₀` if `n₀ ~ N₀ ... d₁ ~ 0`.")
; 策略: 算术/等式 (SMT)。(假设 ~ 为 =，类型为 Real)
(declare-fun getCategory_1176 (CompuScale) String)
(declare-fun getN0 (CompuScale) Real)
(declare-fun getN1 (CompuScale) Real)
(declare-fun getD0 (CompuScale) Real)
(declare-fun getD1 (CompuScale) Real)
(declare-fun getLN0 (CompuScale) Real) ; (Linear N0)
(declare-fun getLN1 (CompuScale) Real) ; (Linear N1)
(declare-fun getLD0 (CompuScale) Real) ; (Linear D0)
(define-fun areCompatible_1176 ((rf CompuScale) (lin CompuScale)) Bool
  (if (and (= (getCategory_1176 rf) "RAT_FUNC")
           (= (getCategory_1176 lin) "LINEAR")
      )
      (and (= (getN0 rf) (getLN0 lin))
           (= (getN1 rf) (getLN1 lin))
           (= (getD0 rf) (getLD0 lin))
           (= (getD1 rf) 0.0) ; (Assuming d1 ~ 0 means d1 = 0.0)
      )
      false ; (Not the types specified)
  )
)

; constr_1177: Allowed targetCategory for SwPointerTargetProps
; (Expression: "...can only be one of TYPE_REFERENCE or FUNCTION_REFERENCE. The only exception ... if ... void, in this case ... VALUE is also permitted.")
; 策略: 条件值限制 (SMT)。
(declare-fun getTargetCategory (SwPointerTargetProps) String)
(declare-fun getSwBaseType (SwPointerTargetProps) SwBaseType)
(declare-fun getNativeDeclaration (SwBaseType) String)
(assert (forall ((sptp SwPointerTargetProps))
  (let ((category (getTargetCategory sptp)))
    (if (= (getNativeDeclaration (getSwBaseType sptp)) "void")
        (or (= category "TYPE_REFERENCE")
            (= category "FUNCTION_REFERENCE")
            (= category "VALUE")
        )
        (or (= category "TYPE_REFERENCE")
            (= category "FUNCTION_REFERENCE")
        )
    )
  )
))

; constr_1178: Existence of attributes of SwDataDefProps in the context of ImplementationDataType
; (Expression: "...can only have one of • baseType • swPointerTargetProps • implementationDataType")
; 策略: 恰选其一 (SMT)。
(declare-fun hasBaseType (SwDataDefPropsContent) Bool)
(declare-fun hasSwPointerTargetProps (SwDataDefPropsContent) Bool)
(declare-fun hasImplementationDataType (SwDataDefPropsContent) Bool)
(assert (forall ((sdp SwDataDefPropsContent))
  (= (+ (ite (hasBaseType sdp) 1 0)
        (ite (hasSwPointerTargetProps sdp) 1 0)
        (ite (hasImplementationDataType sdp) 1 0)
     )
     1
  )
))

; constr_1181: Numerical values used in ModeDeclaration.value and ModeDeclarationGroup.onTransitionValue
; (Expression: "...values ... shall not overlap.")
; 策略: 集合不相交 (SMT)。
(declare-fun getModeDeclarations (ModeDeclarationGroup) (Set ModeDeclaration))
(declare-fun getValue (ModeDeclaration) Int)
(declare-fun getOnTransitionValue (ModeDeclarationGroup) Int)
(declare-fun hasOnTransitionValue (ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)
(assert (forall ((mdg ModeDeclarationGroup) (md ModeDeclaration))
  (=> (and (hasOnTransitionValue mdg)
           (set.member md (getModeDeclarations mdg))
      )
      (not (= (getOnTransitionValue mdg) (getValue md)))
  )
))

(declare-sort PortInterface 0)
(declare-sort ParameterInterface 0)

; ########## 声明所有需要的 Functions (避免重复) ##########
(declare-fun getCategory_1298 (ModeDeclarationGroup) String)
(declare-fun hasOnTransitionValue (ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun getModeDeclarations (ModeDeclarationGroup) (Set ModeDeclaration))
(declare-fun hasValue (ModeDeclaration) Bool)
(declare-fun getProviderClientProto (DataPrototypeMapping) AutosarDataPrototype)
(declare-fun getSubMappings (DataPrototypeMapping) (Set SubElementMapping))
(declare-fun isProviderPrimitiveValue (AutosarDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun isRequesterCompositeElement (SubElementMapping) Bool)
(declare-fun getRole_Type (RoleBasedDataTypeAssignment) String)
(declare-fun getRole_Data (RoleBasedDataAssignment) String)
(declare-fun getRB_TypeAssignments (SwcServiceDependency) (Set RoleBasedDataTypeAssignment))
(declare-fun getRB_DataAssignments (SwcServiceDependency) (Set RoleBasedDataAssignment))
(declare-fun isInvalidationUsed (VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun isCase1 (VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun isCase2 (VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun isCase3 (VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun getTextTableMappings (DataPrototypeMapping) (Set TextTableMapping))
(declare-fun getFirstCompuMethod (DataPrototypeMapping) CompuMethod)
(declare-fun getSecondCompuMethod (DataPrototypeMapping) CompuMethod)

(declare-fun getSecondValue_1307 (TextTableValuePair) (_ BitVec 32))

(declare-fun hasStoreCyclic (NvBlockNeeds) Bool)
(declare-fun getStoreCyclic (NvBlockNeeds) Bool)

; ########## 约束断言 ##########

; constr_1298: Existence of attributes if category ... EXPLICIT_ORDER
(assert (forall ((mdg ModeDeclarationGroup))
  (=> (= (getCategory_1298 mdg) "EXPLICIT_ORDER")
      (and (hasOnTransitionValue mdg)
           (forall ((md ModeDeclaration))
             (=> (set.member md (getModeDeclarations mdg))
                 (hasValue md)
             )
           )
      )
  )
))

; constr_1299: Existence of attributes if category ... other than EXPLICIT_ORDER
(assert (forall ((mdg ModeDeclarationGroup))
  (=> (not (= (getCategory_1298 mdg) "EXPLICIT_ORDER"))
      (and (not (hasOnTransitionValue mdg))
           (forall ((md ModeDeclaration))
             (=> (set.member md (getModeDeclarations mdg))
                 (not (hasValue md))
             )
           )
      )
  )
))

; constr_1300: Primitive DataPrototype... shall not be mapped to element of a composite...
(assert (forall ((dpm DataPrototypeMapping) (sem SubElementMapping))
  (=> (and (isProviderPrimitiveValue (getProviderClientProto dpm))
           (set.member sem (getSubMappings dpm))
           (isRequesterCompositeElement sem)
      )
      false ; (Configuration is not supported)
  )
))

; constr_1301: Existence of RoleBasedDataTypeAssignment.role vs. RoleBasedDataAssignment.role
(define-fun hasDefault_1301 ((ssd SwcServiceDependency)) Bool
  (exists ((rda RoleBasedDataAssignment))
    (and (set.member rda (getRB_DataAssignments ssd))
         (= (getRole_Data rda) "defaultValue")
    )
  )
)
(assert (forall ((ssd SwcServiceDependency) (rdta RoleBasedDataTypeAssignment))
  (=> (and (set.member rdta (getRB_TypeAssignments ssd))
           (= (getRole_Type rdta) "temporaryRamBlock")
      )
      (not (hasDefault_1301 ssd))
  )
))

; constr_1302: Restriction of data invalidation
(assert (forall ((vdp VariableDataPrototype))
  (=> (isInvalidationUsed vdp)
      (or (isCase1 vdp) (isCase2 vdp) (isCase3 vdp))
  )
))

; constr_1303: Applicability of TextTableMapping depending on... CompuMethod.category
(define-fun hasTextTableMapping_1303 ((dpm DataPrototypeMapping)) Bool
  (not (= (getTextTableMappings dpm) (as set.empty (Set TextTableMapping))))
)
(assert (forall ((dpm DataPrototypeMapping))
  (=> (hasTextTableMapping_1303 dpm)
      (let ((cat1 (getCategory_1303 (getFirstCompuMethod dpm)))
            (cat2 (getCategory_1303 (getSecondCompuMethod dpm))))
        (or (and (= cat1 "TEXTTABLE") (= cat2 "TEXTTABLE"))
            (and (= cat1 "SCALE_LINEAR_AND_TEXTTABLE") (= cat2 "TEXTTABLE"))
            (and (= cat1 "TEXTTABLE") (= cat2 "SCALE_LINEAR_AND_TEXTTABLE"))
            (and (= cat1 "BITFIELD_TEXTTABLE") (= cat2 "TEXTTABLE"))
            (and (= cat1 "TEXTTABLE") (= cat2 "BITFIELD_TEXTTABLE"))
            (and (= cat1 "BITFIELD_TEXTTABLE") (= cat2 "BITFIELD_TEXTTABLE"))
        )
      )
  )
))

; constr_1304: Existence of attribute bitfieldTextTableMaskFirst
(assert (forall ((ttm TextTableMapping))
  (= (hasMaskFirst ttm)
     (= (getCategory_1303 (getFirstCompuMethod (getOwnerDPM ttm))) "BITFIELD_TEXTTABLE")
  )
))

; constr_1305: Existence of attribute bitfieldTextTableMaskSecond
(assert (forall ((ttm TextTableMapping))
  (= (hasMaskSecond ttm)
     (= (getCategory_1303 (getSecondCompuMethod (getOwnerDPM ttm))) "BITFIELD_TEXTTABLE")
  )
))

; constr_1306: Limitation of TextTableMapping for... BITFIELD_TEXTTABLE
(define-fun hasValuePair_1306 ((ttm TextTableMapping)) Bool
  (not (= (getValuePairs ttm) (as set.empty (Set TextTableValuePair))))
)
(assert (forall ((ttm TextTableMapping))
  (let ((dpm (getOwnerDPM ttm)))
    (=> (and (= (getCategory_1303 (getFirstCompuMethod dpm)) "BITFIELD_TEXTTABLE")
             (= (getCategory_1303 (getSecondCompuMethod dpm)) "BITFIELD_TEXTTABLE")
             (hasValuePair_1306 ttm)
        )
        (not (getIdenticalMapping ttm)) ; (identicalMapping shall be set to false)
    )
  )
))

; constr_1307: Consistency of values and masks in TextTableMapping
; (Assuming values must be consistent with masks if masks are present)
(assert (forall ((ttm TextTableMapping) (vp TextTableValuePair))
  (=> (set.member vp (getValuePairs ttm))
      (and
        (=> (hasMaskFirst ttm) (= (getFirstValue_1307 vp) (bvand (getFirstValue_1307 vp) (getMaskFirst_1307 ttm))))
        (=> (hasMaskSecond ttm) (= (getSecondValue_1307 vp) (bvand (getSecondValue_1307 vp) (getMaskSecond_1307 ttm))))
      )
  )
))

; constr_1308: Existence of NvBlockNeeds.cyclicWritingPeriod
(assert (forall ((nbn NvBlockNeeds))
  (= (hasCyclicWritingPeriod nbn)
     (and (hasStoreCyclic nbn) (getStoreCyclic nbn))
  )
))

; constr_1309: Existence of NvBlockDescriptor.timingEvent
(assert (forall ((nbd NvBlockDescriptor))
  (let ((nbn (getNvBlockNeeds nbd)))
    (= (hasTimingEvent nbd)
       (and (hasStoreCyclic nbn) (getStoreCyclic nbn))
    )
  )
))

; constr_1310: Existence of attributes of meta-class NvBlockNeeds
(define-fun hasNvDataPort_1310 ((ssd SwcServiceDependency)) Bool
  (exists ((rbpa RoleBasedPortAssignment))
    (and (set.member rbpa (getAssignedPorts ssd))
         (= (getRole_1310 rbpa) "NvDataPort")
    )
  )
)
(assert (forall ((app ApplicationSwComponentType) (ssd SwcServiceDependency))
  (=> (set.member ssd (getServiceDependencies app))
      (let ((nbn (getServiceNeeds ssd)))
        (=> (or (hasStoreCyclic nbn) (hasCyclicWritingPeriod nbn) (hasStoreEmergency nbn) (hasStoreImmediate nbn))
            (hasNvDataPort_1310 ssd)
        )
      )
  )
))

; constr_1311: Appearance of safety-related possible values... at most include a single value
(define-fun checkAtMostOneSafety ((options (Set String))) Bool
  (<= (+ (ite (set.member "safetyQM" options) 1 0)
         (ite (set.member "safetyAsilA" options) 1 0)
         (ite (set.member "safetyAsilB" options) 1 0)
         (ite (set.member "safetyAsilC" options) 1 0)
         (ite (set.member "safetyAsilD" options) 1 0))
      1)
)
(assert (forall ((ms MemorySection)) (checkAtMostOneSafety (getOptions_Mem ms))))
(assert (forall ((sam SwAddrMethod)) (checkAtMostOneSafety (getOptions_Sw sam))))

; constr_1312: PortPrototypes typed by a ParameterInterface
(assert (forall ((prp PRPortPrototype))
  (not (isParameterInterface (getPortInterface_1312 prp)))
))

; constr_1313: Completeness of TextTableMapping...
; (Requires bitmask value enumeration and coverage check, which is too complex/speculative based on input)
; (仅声明了相关 types/functions)

; constr_1314: Profile VSA_LINEAR for ApplicationArrayDataType
(assert (forall ((aadt ApplicationArrayDataType))
  (=> (= (getDynamicProfile_VSA aadt) "VSA_LINEAR")
      (let ((elem (getElement_VSA aadt)))
        (and (= (getArraySizeSemantics_VSA elem) "variableSize")
             (hasMaxElements_VSA elem)
             (= (getArraySizeHandling_VSA elem) "allIndicesSameArraySize")
             (not (hasDynamicProfile_VSA (getElementType_VSA elem)))
        )
      )
  )
))

; constr_1315: Profile VSA_SQUARE for ApplicationArrayDataType
; (Requires recursive path check, which is complex. Defining minimal assertions.)
(assert (forall ((aadt ApplicationArrayDataType))
  (=> (= (getDynamicProfile_VSA aadt) "VSA_SQUARE")
      (let ((elem (getElement_VSA aadt)))
        (and (= (getArraySizeSemantics_VSA elem) "variableSize")
             (not (hasMaxElements_VSA elem))
             (= (getArraySizeHandling_VSA elem) "inheritedFromArrayElementTypeSize")
             (isArray_VSA elem) ; (Placeholder for "is typed by ApplicationArrayDataType")
             ; (Further recursive checks constr_1315 are not fully modeled)
        )
      )
  )
))

; constr_1316: Profile VSA_RECTANGULAR for ApplicationArrayDataType
(assert (forall ((aadt ApplicationArrayDataType))
  (=> (= (getDynamicProfile_VSA aadt) "VSA_RECTANGULAR")
      (let ((elem (getElement_VSA aadt)))
        (and (= (getArraySizeSemantics_VSA elem) "variableSize")
             (hasMaxElements_VSA elem)
             (= (getArraySizeHandling_VSA elem) "allIndicesSameArraySize")
             (isArray_VSA elem) ; (Placeholder for "is typed by ApplicationArrayDataType")
             ; (Further recursive checks constr_1316 are not fully modeled)
        )
      )
  )
))

; constr_1317: Profile VSA_FULLY_FLEXIBLE for ApplicationArrayDataType
(assert (forall ((aadt ApplicationArrayDataType))
  (=> (= (getDynamicProfile_VSA aadt) "VSA_FULLY_FLEXIBLE")
      (let ((elem (getElement_VSA aadt)))
        (and (= (getArraySizeSemantics_VSA elem) "variableSize")
             (hasMaxElements_VSA elem)
             (= (getArraySizeHandling_VSA elem) "allIndicesDifferentArraySize")
             (isArray_VSA elem) ; (Placeholder for "is typed by ApplicationArrayDataType")
             ; (Further recursive checks constr_1317 are not fully modeled)
        )
      )
  )
))

; constr_1318: Profile VSA_LINEAR for ImplementationDataType
; (Defines constraints on the *first* and *second* element in the chain)
(assert (forall ((idt ImplementationDataType))
  (=> (= (getDynamicProfile_IDT_VSA idt) "VSA_LINEAR")
      (exists ((payload ImplementationDataTypeElement) (second ImplementationDataTypeElement))
        (and 
             (set.member payload (getSubElements_VSA idt))
             ; (Payload checks)
             (not (has (field arraySizeSemantics) payload)) ; (Using pseudo-code as function wasn't declared)
             (= (getCategory_1303 payload) "ARRAY")
             (not (has (field arraySize) payload))
             (not (has (field arraySizeHandling) payload))
             ; (Second element checks)
             (set.member second (getSubElements_VSA payload)) ; (Assuming sub-elements of elements)
             (= (getArraySizeSemantics_VSA second) "variableSize")
             (has (field arraySize) second)
             (= (getArraySizeHandling_VSA second) "allIndicesSameArraySize")
        )
      )
  )
))

; constr_1319: Profile VSA_SQUARE for ImplementationDataType
; (Requires recursive path check, not fully modeled)
(assert (forall ((idt ImplementationDataType))
  (=> (= (getDynamicProfile_IDT_VSA idt) "VSA_SQUARE")
      ; (Recursive logic omitted due to complexity/missing inputs)
      true
  )
))

; constr_1320: Profile VSA_RECTANGULAR for ImplementationDataType
; (Requires recursive path check, not fully modeled)
(assert (forall ((idt ImplementationDataType))
  (=> (= (getDynamicProfile_IDT_VSA idt) "VSA_RECTANGULAR")
      ; (Recursive logic omitted due to complexity/missing inputs)
      true
  )
))

; constr_1321: Profile VSA_FULLY_FLEXIBLE for ImplementationDataType
; (Requires recursive path check, not fully modeled)
(assert (forall ((idt ImplementationDataType))
  (=> (= (getDynamicProfile_IDT_VSA idt) "VSA_FULLY_FLEXIBLE")
      ; (Recursive logic omitted due to complexity/missing inputs)
      true
  )
))



;; Type declarations
(declare-sort DelegationSwConnector 0)

(declare-sort PortInterface 0)
(declare-sort ComSpec 0)
(declare-sort CompuMethod 0)
(declare-sort CompuScale 0)
(declare-sort ApplicationDataType 0)
(declare-sort ClientServerInterface 0)
(declare-sort EndToEndDescription 0)
(declare-sort RoleBasedPortAssignment 0)

;; Port type predicates
(declare-fun isRPort (PortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun isPPort (PortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun isPRPort (PortPrototype) Bool)

;; Interface type predicates
(declare-fun isSenderReceiverInterface (PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun isNvDataInterface (PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun isClientServerInterface (PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun isModeSwitchInterface (PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun isParameterInterface (PortInterface) Bool)

;; ComSpec type predicates
(declare-fun isClientComSpec (ComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isServerComSpec (ComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isModeSwitchSenderComSpec (ComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isModeSwitchReceiverComSpec (ComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isNvRequireComSpec (ComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isNvProvideComSpec (ComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isParameterProvideComSpec (ComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isParameterRequireComSpec (ComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isReceiverComSpec (ComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun isSenderComSpec (ComSpec) Bool)

;; DelegationSwConnector functions
(declare-fun innerPort (DelegationSwConnector) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun outerPort (DelegationSwConnector) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun portInterface (PortPrototype) PortInterface)

;; Table_4_5: DelegationSwConnector connection rules
(assert (forall ((conn DelegationSwConnector))
    (let ((inner (innerPort conn))
          (outer (outerPort conn))
          (iface (portInterface (innerPort conn))))
        (=> (or (isSenderReceiverInterface iface) (isNvDataInterface iface))
            (or
                (and (isRPort inner) (isRPort outer))
                (and (isRPort inner) (isPRPort outer))
                (and (isPPort inner) (isPPort outer))
                (and (isPPort inner) (isPRPort outer))
                (and (isPRPort inner) (isRPort outer))
                (and (isPRPort inner) (isPPort outer))
                (and (isPRPort inner) (isPRPort outer)))))))

;; ComSpec compatibility functions
(declare-fun compatibleComSpec (PortInterface ComSpec) Bool)

;; Table_4_59 series: Interface-ComSpec compatibility
(assert (forall ((iface ClientServerInterface) (cs ComSpec))
    (=> (compatibleComSpec iface cs)
        (or (isClientComSpec cs) (isServerComSpec cs)))))

(assert (forall ((iface PortInterface) (cs ComSpec))
    (=> (and (isModeSwitchInterface iface) (compatibleComSpec iface cs))
        (or (isModeSwitchSenderComSpec cs) (isModeSwitchReceiverComSpec cs)))))

(assert (forall ((iface PortInterface) (cs ComSpec))
    (=> (and (isNvDataInterface iface) (compatibleComSpec iface cs))
        (or (isNvRequireComSpec cs) (isNvProvideComSpec cs)))))

(assert (forall ((iface PortInterface) (cs ComSpec))
    (=> (and (isParameterInterface iface) (compatibleComSpec iface cs))
        (or (isParameterProvideComSpec cs) (isParameterRequireComSpec cs)))))

(assert (forall ((iface PortInterface) (cs ComSpec))
    (=> (and (isSenderReceiverInterface iface) (compatibleComSpec iface cs))
        (or (isReceiverComSpec cs) (isSenderComSpec cs)))))

;; CompuMethod functions
(declare-fun category (CompuMethod) String)
(declare-fun hasCompuInternalToPhys (CompuMethod) Bool)
(declare-fun hasCompuPhysToInternal (CompuMethod) Bool)
(declare-fun hasCompuInverseValue (CompuScale) Bool)
(declare-fun hasCompuConst (CompuScale) Bool)
(declare-fun hasLowerLimit (CompuScale) Bool)
(declare-fun hasUpperLimit (CompuScale) Bool)
(declare-fun belongsTo (CompuScale CompuMethod) Bool)

;; Table_5_74_Annotation_1: LINEAR/SCALE_LINEAR require compuInternalToPhys if not defined by compuPhysToInternal
(assert (forall ((cm CompuMethod))
    (=> (or (= (category cm) "LINEAR") (= (category cm) "SCALE_LINEAR"))
        (=> (not (hasCompuPhysToInternal cm))
            (hasCompuInternalToPhys cm)))))

;; Table_5_74_Annotation_2: RAT_FUNC/SCALE_RAT_FUNC require both directions or inverse value
(assert (forall ((cm CompuMethod))
    (=> (or (= (category cm) "RAT_FUNC") (= (category cm) "SCALE_RAT_FUNC"))
        (or
            (and (hasCompuPhysToInternal cm) (hasCompuInternalToPhys cm))
            (exists ((cs CompuScale)) (and (belongsTo cs cm) (hasCompuInverseValue cs)))))))

;; Table_5_74_Annotation_4: Limit definition requirement
(assert (forall ((cm CompuMethod) (cs CompuScale))
    (=> (and (belongsTo cs cm)
             (or (= (category cm) "RAT_FUNC") (= (category cm) "SCALE_RAT_FUNC")))
        (and (hasLowerLimit cs) (hasUpperLimit cs)))))

;; ApplicationDataType functions
(declare-fun adtCategory (ApplicationDataType) String)
(declare-fun hasSwCalibrationAccess (ApplicationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun hasSwRecordLayout (ApplicationDataType) Bool)

;; Table_5_8_AppDataType_COM_AXIS_swCalibrationAccess_Multiplicity
(assert (forall ((adt ApplicationDataType))
    (=> (= (adtCategory adt) "COM_AXIS")
        (hasSwCalibrationAccess adt))))

;; Table_5_8_AppDataType_VAL_BLK_swRecordLayout_Multiplicity
(assert (forall ((adt ApplicationDataType))
    (=> (= (adtCategory adt) "VAL_BLK")
        (or (hasSwRecordLayout adt) (not (hasSwRecordLayout adt))))))

;; Table_7_64: RoleBasedPortAssignment role matching
(declare-fun roleOfAssignment (RoleBasedPortAssignment) String)
(declare-fun nameOfInterface (ClientServerInterface) String)
(declare-fun usesInterface (RoleBasedPortAssignment ClientServerInterface) Bool)

(assert (forall ((rbpa RoleBasedPortAssignment) (csi ClientServerInterface))
    (=> (usesInterface rbpa csi)
        (= (roleOfAssignment rbpa) (nameOfInterface csi)))))

;; constr_1001: dataId uniqueness within System
(declare-fun dataIdValue (EndToEndDescription Int) Int)
(declare-fun hasDataId (EndToEndDescription Int) Bool)

(assert (forall ((e1 EndToEndDescription) (e2 EndToEndDescription) (i1 Int) (i2 Int))
    (=> (and (hasDataId e1 i1) (hasDataId e2 i2)
             (= (dataIdValue e1 i1) (dataIdValue e2 i2)))
        (and (= e1 e2) (= i1 i2)))))



;; Type declarations

(declare-sort SwComponentPrototype 0)


(declare-sort PortInterface 0)
(declare-sort DataInterface 0)
(declare-sort ClientServerOperation 0)
(declare-sort ApplicationError 0)
(declare-sort ClientServerInterface 0)
(declare-sort AutosarDataPrototype 0)
(declare-sort SwDataDefProps 0)
(declare-sort DataPrototypeMapping 0)

(declare-sort ApplicationPrimitiveDataType 0)
(declare-sort ApplicationRecordDataType 0)
(declare-sort ApplicationArrayDataType 0)
(declare-sort ImplementationDataType 0)
(declare-sort Unit 0)
(declare-sort PhysicalDimension 0)
(declare-sort PhysicalDimensionMapping 0)
(declare-sort ApplicationDataType 0)


;; Functions for composition and typing
(declare-fun containsPrototype (CompositionSwComponentType SwComponentPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun prototypeTypedBy (SwComponentPrototype SwComponentType) Bool)
(declare-fun isCompositionType (SwComponentType) Bool)

;; Reachability for recursive composition check (constr_1035)
(define-fun-rec compositionReachable ((from CompositionSwComponentType) (to SwComponentType)) Bool
    (or
        (exists ((proto SwComponentPrototype))
            (and (containsPrototype from proto)
                 (prototypeTypedBy proto to)))
        (exists ((proto SwComponentPrototype) (intermediate SwComponentType))
            (and (containsPrototype from proto)
                 (prototypeTypedBy proto intermediate)
                 (isCompositionType intermediate)
                 (compositionReachable intermediate to)))))

;; constr_1035: No recursive composition
(assert (forall ((comp CompositionSwComponentType))
    (not (compositionReachable comp comp))))

;; Port and interface functions (constr_1036)
(declare-fun portTypedBy (PortPrototype PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun connected (PortPrototype PortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun interfaceKind (PortInterface) String)
(declare-fun isDataInterfaceSubtype (PortInterface) Bool)

;; constr_1036: Interface kind matching for connections
(assert (forall ((p1 PortPrototype) (p2 PortPrototype) (i1 PortInterface) (i2 PortInterface))
    (=> (and (connected p1 p2) (portTypedBy p1 i1) (portTypedBy p2 i2))
        (or
            (= (interfaceKind i1) (interfaceKind i2))
            (and (isDataInterfaceSubtype i1) (isDataInterfaceSubtype i2))))))

;; Client-server connection functions (constr_1037)
(declare-fun isClientPort (PortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun isServerPort (PortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun handlesSameOperation (PortPrototype PortPrototype) Bool)

;; constr_1037: Client not connected to multiple servers for same operation
(assert (forall ((client PortPrototype) (server1 PortPrototype) (server2 PortPrototype))
    (=> (and (isClientPort client)
             (isServerPort server1) (isServerPort server2)
             (connected client server1) (connected client server2)
             (handlesSameOperation server1 server2))
        (= server1 server2))))

;; Ownership and error reference (constr_1038)
(declare-fun ownedBy (ApplicationError ClientServerInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun operationInInterface (ClientServerOperation ClientServerInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun referencesError (ClientServerOperation ApplicationError) Bool)

;; constr_1038: ApplicationError ownership scope
(assert (forall ((op ClientServerOperation) (err ApplicationError) (iface ClientServerInterface))
    (=> (and (operationInInterface op iface) (referencesError op err))
        (ownedBy err iface))))

;; swImplPolicy consistency (constr_1039)
(declare-fun swImplPolicy (AutosarDataPrototype) String)
(declare-fun mappedTo (AutosarDataPrototype AutosarDataPrototype) Bool)

;; constr_1039: swImplPolicy mapping constraint
(assert (forall ((proto1 AutosarDataPrototype) (proto2 AutosarDataPrototype))
    (=> (mappedTo proto1 proto2)
        (not (and (= (swImplPolicy proto1) "queued")
                  (not (= (swImplPolicy proto2) "queued")))))))

;; Data type compatibility helper functions
(declare-fun category (ApplicationDataType) String)
(declare-fun categoryImpl (ImplementationDataType) String)
(declare-fun swDataDefPropsCompatible (SwDataDefProps SwDataDefProps) Bool)
(declare-fun compatibleDataTypes (ApplicationPrimitiveDataType ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) Bool)

;; constr_1047: ApplicationPrimitiveDataType compatibility
(assert (forall ((adt1 ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) (adt2 ApplicationPrimitiveDataType))
    (=> (compatibleDataTypes adt1 adt2)
        (or
            ;; Condition 1: same category and compatible props
            (exists ((cat String) (props1 SwDataDefProps) (props2 SwDataDefProps))
                (and (= (category adt1) cat) (= (category adt2) cat)
                     (swDataDefPropsCompatible props1 props2)))
            ;; Condition 2 & 3: DataPrototypeMapping exists
            (exists ((mapping DataPrototypeMapping))
                (or (and (mappedTo adt1 adt2))
                    (and (mappedTo adt2 adt1))))))))

;; Record type compatibility (constr_1048)
(declare-fun recordElementAtPosition (ApplicationRecordDataType Int) DataPrototype)
(declare-fun elementTypesCompatible (DataPrototype DataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun compatibleRecordTypes (ApplicationRecordDataType ApplicationRecordDataType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((rec1 ApplicationRecordDataType) (rec2 ApplicationRecordDataType))
    (=> (compatibleRecordTypes rec1 rec2)
        (or
            ;; Condition 1: all elements at same position compatible
            (forall ((pos Int))
                (elementTypesCompatible 
                    (recordElementAtPosition rec1 pos)
                    (recordElementAtPosition rec2 pos)))
            ;; Condition 2: SubElementMapping exists
            (exists ((mapping DataPrototypeMapping))
                (mappedTo rec1 rec2))))))

;; Array type compatibility (constr_1049)
(declare-fun arrayMaxElements (ApplicationArrayDataType) Int)
(declare-fun arraySizeSemantics (ApplicationArrayDataType) String)
(declare-fun arrayElementType (ApplicationArrayDataType) ApplicationDataType)
(declare-fun compatibleArrayTypes (ApplicationArrayDataType ApplicationArrayDataType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((arr1 ApplicationArrayDataType) (arr2 ApplicationArrayDataType))
    (=> (compatibleArrayTypes arr1 arr2)
        (or
            ;; Condition 1: compatible elements and same size/semantics
            (and (compatibleDataTypes (arrayElementType arr1) (arrayElementType arr2))
                 (= (arrayMaxElements arr1) (arrayMaxElements arr2))
                 (= (arraySizeSemantics arr1) (arraySizeSemantics arr2)))
            ;; Condition 2: SubElementMapping exists
            (exists ((mapping DataPrototypeMapping))
                (mappedTo arr1 arr2))))))

;; ImplementationDataType compatibility (constr_1050)
(declare-fun implStructureIdentical (ImplementationDataType ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun implArraySizeIdentical (ImplementationDataType ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun compatibleImplTypes (ImplementationDataType ImplementationDataType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((impl1 ImplementationDataType) (impl2 ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (compatibleImplTypes impl1 impl2)
        (or
            ;; Condition 1: same category, structure, size, props
            (and (= (categoryImpl impl1) (categoryImpl impl2))
                 (implStructureIdentical impl1 impl2)
                 (implArraySizeIdentical impl1 impl2))
            ;; Condition 2 & 3: DataPrototypeMapping exists
            (exists ((mapping DataPrototypeMapping))
                (mappedTo impl1 impl2))))))

;; Unit compatibility (constr_1052)
(declare-fun factorSiToUnit (Unit) Real)
(declare-fun offsetSiToUnit (Unit) Real)
(declare-fun unitPhysicalDimension (Unit) PhysicalDimension)
(declare-fun hasFactor (Unit) Bool)
(declare-fun hasOffset (Unit) Bool)
(declare-fun hasPhysDim (Unit) Bool)
(declare-fun compatibleUnits (Unit Unit) Bool)

(assert (forall ((u1 Unit) (u2 Unit))
    (=> (compatibleUnits u1 u2)
        (and
            ;; Compatible factors and offsets
            (=> (and (hasFactor u1) (hasFactor u2))
                (= (factorSiToUnit u1) (factorSiToUnit u2)))
            (=> (and (hasOffset u1) (hasOffset u2))
                (= (offsetSiToUnit u1) (offsetSiToUnit u2)))
            ;; Identical or both absent PhysicalDimension
            (=> (and (hasPhysDim u1) (hasPhysDim u2))
                (= (unitPhysicalDimension u1) (unitPhysicalDimension u2)))))))

;; PhysicalDimension compatibility (constr_1053)
(declare-fun lengthExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun massExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun timeExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun currentExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun temperatureExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun molarAmountExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun luminousIntensityExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun physDimShortName (PhysicalDimension) String)
(declare-fun physDimMappingExists (PhysicalDimension PhysicalDimension) Bool)
(declare-fun compatiblePhysDims (PhysicalDimension PhysicalDimension) Bool)

(assert (forall ((pd1 PhysicalDimension) (pd2 PhysicalDimension))
    (=> (compatiblePhysDims pd1 pd2)
        (and
            ;; All exponents identical
            (= (lengthExp pd1) (lengthExp pd2))
            (= (massExp pd1) (massExp pd2))
            (= (timeExp pd1) (timeExp pd2))
            (= (currentExp pd1) (currentExp pd2))
            (= (temperatureExp pd1) (temperatureExp pd2))
            (= (molarAmountExp pd1) (molarAmountExp pd2))
            (= (luminousIntensityExp pd1) (luminousIntensityExp pd2))
            ;; Same shortName or mapping exists
            (or (= (physDimShortName pd1) (physDimShortName pd2))
                (physDimMappingExists pd1 pd2))))))

;; Category-based mapping constraints (constr_1055-1066)
(declare-fun baseTypeCompatible (SwBaseType SwBaseType) Bool)
(declare-fun applToImplMapping (ApplicationDataType ImplementationDataType) Bool)

;; constr_1055: VALUE category baseType compatibility
(assert (forall ((impl ImplementationDataType) (base1 SwBaseType) (base2 SwBaseType))
    (=> (and (= (categoryImpl impl) "VALUE")
             (applToImplMapping impl base1)
             (applToImplMapping impl base2))
        (baseTypeCompatible base1 base2))))

;; constr_1059: VALUE category Application to Implementation mapping
(assert (forall ((appl ApplicationDataType) (impl ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (and (= (category appl) "VALUE") (applToImplMapping appl impl))
        (= (categoryImpl impl) "VALUE"))))

;; constr_1060: ARRAY/VAL_BLK mapping to ARRAY
(assert (forall ((appl ApplicationDataType) (impl ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (and (or (= (category appl) "ARRAY") (= (category appl) "VAL_BLK"))
             (applToImplMapping appl impl))
        (= (categoryImpl impl) "ARRAY"))))

;; constr_1061: STRUCTURE mapping
(assert (forall ((appl ApplicationDataType) (impl ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (and (= (category appl) "STRUCTURE") (applToImplMapping appl impl))
        (= (categoryImpl impl) "STRUCTURE"))))

;; constr_1063: BOOLEAN mapping to VALUE
(assert (forall ((appl ApplicationDataType) (impl ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (and (= (category appl) "BOOLEAN") (applToImplMapping appl impl))
        (= (categoryImpl impl) "VALUE"))))

;; constr_1064: COM_AXIS etc. mapping to STRUCTURE or ARRAY
(assert (forall ((appl ApplicationDataType) (impl ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (and (or (= (category appl) "COM_AXIS") (= (category appl) "RES_AXIS")
                 (= (category appl) "CURVE") (= (category appl) "MAP")
                 (= (category appl) "CUBOID") (= (category appl) "CUBE_4")
                 (= (category appl) "CUBE_5"))
             (applToImplMapping appl impl))
        (or (= (categoryImpl impl) "STRUCTURE") (= (categoryImpl impl) "ARRAY")))))

;; constr_1066: Forbidden Implementation categories
(assert (forall ((appl ApplicationDataType) (impl ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (applToImplMapping appl impl)
        (not (or (= (categoryImpl impl) "UNION")
                 (= (categoryImpl impl) "DATA_REFERENCE")
                 (= (categoryImpl impl) "FUNCTION_REFERENCE"))))))

;; DataConstr functions (constr_1126)
(declare-fun providerConstr (DataElement) DataConstr)
(declare-fun requirerConstr (DataElement) DataConstr)
(declare-fun constrLowerLimit (DataConstr) Real)
(declare-fun constrUpperLimit (DataConstr) Real)
(declare-fun hasConstr (DataElement) Bool)
(declare-fun providesTo (DataElement DataElement) Bool)

;; constr_1126: Provider constraints within requirer constraints
(assert (forall ((provider DataElement) (requirer DataElement))
    (=> (and (providesTo provider requirer)
             (hasConstr provider) (hasConstr requirer))
        (let ((pConstr (providerConstr provider))
              (rConstr (requirerConstr requirer)))
            (and (<= (constrLowerLimit rConstr) (constrLowerLimit pConstr))
                 (<= (constrUpperLimit pConstr) (constrUpperLimit rConstr)))))))

;; Queue length functions (constr_1128)
(declare-fun referencesRunnable (OperationInvokedEvent RunnableEntity) Bool)
(declare-fun eventReferencesOperation (OperationInvokedEvent ClientServerOperation) Bool)
(declare-fun comSpecForOperation (ServerComSpec ClientServerOperation) Bool)
(declare-fun queueLength (ServerComSpec) Int)

;; constr_1128: Identical queue length for operations with same runnable
(assert (forall ((event1 OperationInvokedEvent) (event2 OperationInvokedEvent) 
                 (runnable RunnableEntity) (op1 ClientServerOperation) (op2 ClientServerOperation)
                 (spec1 ServerComSpec) (spec2 ServerComSpec))
    (=> (and (referencesRunnable event1 runnable) (referencesRunnable event2 runnable)
             (eventReferencesOperation event1 op1) (eventReferencesOperation event2 op2)
             (comSpecForOperation spec1 op1) (comSpecForOperation spec2 op2)
             (not (= event1 event2)))
        (= (queueLength spec1) (queueLength spec2)))))

;; swImplPolicy functions (constr_1129-1132)
(declare-fun comSpecReferencesDataElement (NonqueuedReceiverComSpec VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun comSpecReferencesDataElementQ (QueuedReceiverComSpec VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun comSpecReferencesDataElementNS (NonqueuedSenderComSpec VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun comSpecReferencesDataElementQS (QueuedSenderComSpec VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun swImplPolicy (VariableDataPrototype) String)

;; constr_1129: NonqueuedReceiverComSpec - swImplPolicy not queued
(assert (forall ((comSpec NonqueuedReceiverComSpec) (dataElem VariableDataPrototype))
    (=> (comSpecReferencesDataElement comSpec dataElem)
        (not (= (swImplPolicy dataElem) "queued")))))

;; constr_1130: QueuedReceiverComSpec - swImplPolicy must be queued
(assert (forall ((comSpec QueuedReceiverComSpec) (dataElem VariableDataPrototype))
    (=> (comSpecReferencesDataElementQ comSpec dataElem)
        (= (swImplPolicy dataElem) "queued"))))

;; constr_1131: NonqueuedSenderComSpec - swImplPolicy not queued
(assert (forall ((comSpec NonqueuedSenderComSpec) (dataElem VariableDataPrototype))
    (=> (comSpecReferencesDataElementNS comSpec dataElem)
        (not (= (swImplPolicy dataElem) "queued")))))

;; constr_1132: QueuedSenderComSpec - swImplPolicy must be queued
(assert (forall ((comSpec QueuedSenderComSpec) (dataElem VariableDataPrototype))
    (=> (comSpecReferencesDataElementQS comSpec dataElem)
        (= (swImplPolicy dataElem) "queued"))))

;; CompuScale symbolic name functions (constr_1133)
(declare-fun symbolicName (CompuScale) String)
(declare-fun scaleLowerLimit (CompuScale) Real)
(declare-fun scaleUpperLimit (CompuScale) Real)
(declare-fun scaleInMethod (CompuScale CompuMethod) Bool)
(declare-fun compuMethodCategory (CompuMethod) String)

;; constr_1133: Identical symbolic names must have same range
(assert (forall ((scale1 CompuScale) (scale2 CompuScale) (method CompuMethod))
    (=> (and (scaleInMethod scale1 method) (scaleInMethod scale2 method)
             (= (symbolicName scale1) (symbolicName scale2))
             (not (= scale1 scale2)))
        (and (= (scaleLowerLimit scale1) (scaleLowerLimit scale2))
             (= (scaleUpperLimit scale1) (scaleUpperLimit scale2))))))

;; BITFIELD_TEXTTABLE vt constraint (constr_1135)
(declare-fun vtValue (CompuScale) String)
(declare-fun containsPipe (String) Bool)

;; constr_1135: vt cannot contain "|" in BITFIELD_TEXTTABLE
(assert (forall ((scale CompuScale) (method CompuMethod))
    (=> (and (scaleInMethod scale method)
             (= (compuMethodCategory method) "BITFIELD_TEXTTABLE"))
        (not (containsPipe (vtValue scale))))))

;; ParameterInterface ownership (constr_1137)
(declare-fun portTypedBy (PPortPrototype PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun isParameterInterface (PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun ownedByComponent (PPortPrototype SwComponentType) Bool)
(declare-fun isParameterSwComponentType (SwComponentType) Bool)

;; constr_1137: ParameterInterface PPort only in ParameterSwComponentType
(assert (forall ((port PPortPrototype) (iface PortInterface) (comp SwComponentType))
    (=> (and (portTypedBy port iface) (isParameterInterface iface)
             (ownedByComponent port comp))
        (isParameterSwComponentType comp))))

;; DiagEventDebounceMonitorInternal functions (constr_1138-1139)
(declare-fun hasDebounceAlgorithm (DiagnosticEventNeeds DiagEventDebounceAlgorithm) Bool)
(declare-fun hasAssignedPort (DiagnosticEventNeeds RoleBasedPortAssignment) Bool)
(declare-fun isMonitorInternal (DiagEventDebounceAlgorithm) Bool)
(declare-fun assignedPortRole (RoleBasedPortAssignment) String)
(declare-fun portAssignmentReferencesRPort (RoleBasedPortAssignment) Bool)

;; constr_1138: assignedPort only with DiagEventDebounceMonitorInternal
(assert (forall ((needs DiagnosticEventNeeds) (algo DiagEventDebounceAlgorithm) (port RoleBasedPortAssignment))
    (=> (and (hasDebounceAlgorithm needs algo) (hasAssignedPort needs port))
        (isMonitorInternal algo))))

;; constr_1139: assignedPort refers to RPortPrototype with specific role
(assert (forall ((needs DiagnosticEventNeeds) (algo DiagEventDebounceAlgorithm) (port RoleBasedPortAssignment))
    (=> (and (hasDebounceAlgorithm needs algo) (isMonitorInternal algo)
             (hasAssignedPort needs port))
        (and (portAssignmentReferencesRPort port)
             (= (assignedPortRole port) "DiagFaultDetectionCounterPort")))))

;; invalidValue and handleInvalid (constr_1140)
(declare-fun handleInvalid (InvalidationPolicy) String)
(declare-fun initValue (NonqueuedReceiverComSpec) Real)
(declare-fun invalidValue (VariableDataPrototype) Real)
(declare-fun policyForInterface (InvalidationPolicy PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun comSpecForDataElement (NonqueuedReceiverComSpec VariableDataPrototype) Bool)

;; constr_1140: handleInvalid=replace and initValue=invalidValue not supported
(assert (forall ((policy InvalidationPolicy) (comSpec NonqueuedReceiverComSpec) 
                 (dataElem VariableDataPrototype))
    (=> (comSpecForDataElement comSpec dataElem)
        (not (and (= (handleInvalid policy) "replace")
                  (= (initValue comSpec) (invalidValue dataElem)))))))

;; CompuScale symbol applicability (constr_1146)
(declare-fun hasSymbol (CompuScale) Bool)

;; constr_1146: symbol only for specific categories
(assert (forall ((scale CompuScale) (method CompuMethod))
    (=> (and (scaleInMethod scale method) (hasSymbol scale))
        (or (= (compuMethodCategory method) "SCALE_LINEAR_AND_TEXTTABLE")
            (= (compuMethodCategory method) "SCALE_RATIONAL_AND_TEXTTABLE")
            (= (compuMethodCategory method) "TEXTTABLE")
            (= (compuMethodCategory method) "BITFIELD_TEXTTABLE")))))

;; NvBlockSwComponentType isService constraint (constr_1148)
(declare-fun portInterfaceForNvBlock (PortInterface NvBlockSwComponentType) Bool)
(declare-fun isService (PortInterface) Bool)

;; constr_1148: isService must be false for NvBlock interfaces
(assert (forall ((iface PortInterface) (nvBlock NvBlockSwComponentType))
    (=> (portInterfaceForNvBlock iface nvBlock)
        (not (isService iface)))))

;; PortDefinedArgumentValue valueType (constr_1150)
(declare-fun valueTypeCategory (PortDefinedArgumentValue) String)
(declare-fun valueTypeTargetCategory (PortDefinedArgumentValue) String)

;; constr_1150: valueType category must be VALUE or TYPE_REFERENCE (with target VALUE)
(assert (forall ((pdav PortDefinedArgumentValue))
    (or (= (valueTypeCategory pdav) "VALUE")
        (and (= (valueTypeCategory pdav) "TYPE_REFERENCE")
             (= (valueTypeTargetCategory pdav) "VALUE")))))

;; PortInterfaceMapping applicability (constr_1151)
(declare-sort SwConnector 0)
(declare-sort PortInterfaceMapping 0)
(declare-fun connectorPort1 (SwConnector) PPortPrototype)
(declare-fun connectorPort2 (SwConnector) RPortPrototype)
(declare-fun mappingInterface1 (PortInterfaceMapping) PortInterface)
(declare-fun mappingInterface2 (PortInterfaceMapping) PortInterface)
(declare-fun mappingAppliedToConnector (PortInterfaceMapping SwConnector) Bool)

;; constr_1151: PortInterfaceMapping validity
(assert (forall ((mapping PortInterfaceMapping) (conn SwConnector) 
                 (iface1 PortInterface) (iface2 PortInterface))
    (=> (and (mappingAppliedToConnector mapping conn)
             (= (mappingInterface1 mapping) iface1)
             (= (mappingInterface2 mapping) iface2))
        (and (portTypedBy (connectorPort1 conn) iface1)
             (or (portTypedBy (connectorPort1 conn) iface2)
                 (exists ((port2 RPortPrototype))
                     (and (= (connectorPort2 conn) port2)
                          (or (portTypedBy (connectorPort1 conn) iface1)
                              (portTypedBy (connectorPort1 conn) iface2)))))))))

;; ApplicationArrayElement category sync (constr_1152)
(declare-fun arrayElementCategory (ApplicationArrayElement) String)
(declare-fun arrayElementTypeCategory (ApplicationArrayElement) String)

;; constr_1152: ApplicationArrayElement category matches type category
(assert (forall ((elem ApplicationArrayElement))
    (= (arrayElementCategory elem) (arrayElementTypeCategory elem))))

;; CompuScale compatibility for sender-receiver (constr_1154)
(declare-fun providerCompuScales (PPortPrototype) (Set CompuScale))
(declare-fun requirerCompuScales (RPortPrototype) (Set CompuScale))
(declare-fun connectedPorts (PPortPrototype RPortPrototype) Bool)

;; constr_1154: Provider CompuScales subset of requirer CompuScales
(assert (forall ((pPort PPortPrototype) (rPort RPortPrototype))
    (=> (connectedPorts pPort rPort)
        (forall ((scale CompuScale))
            (=> (set.member scale (providerCompuScales pPort))
                (set.member scale (requirerCompuScales rPort)))))))

;; CompuScale compatibility for client-server (constr_1155)
(declare-fun argumentDirection (ArgumentDataPrototype) String)
(declare-fun clientCompuScales (RPortPrototype ArgumentDataPrototype) (Set CompuScale))
(declare-fun serverCompuScales (PPortPrototype ArgumentDataPrototype) (Set CompuScale))
(declare-fun argumentInOperation (ArgumentDataPrototype ClientServerOperation) Bool)
(declare-fun operationConnected (ClientServerOperation PPortPrototype RPortPrototype) Bool)

;; constr_1155: CompuScale compatibility based on argument direction
(assert (forall ((arg ArgumentDataPrototype) (op ClientServerOperation) 
                 (pPort PPortPrototype) (rPort RPortPrototype))
    (=> (and (argumentInOperation arg op) (operationConnected op pPort rPort))
        (let ((dir (argumentDirection arg))
              (cScales (clientCompuScales rPort arg))
              (sScales (serverCompuScales pPort arg)))
            (or
                ;; IN: client subset of server
                (and (= dir "IN")
                     (forall ((scale CompuScale))
                         (=> (set.member scale cScales)
                             (set.member scale sScales))))
                ;; OUT: server subset of client
                (and (= dir "OUT")
                     (forall ((scale CompuScale))
                         (=> (set.member scale sScales)
                             (set.member scale cScales))))
                ;; INOUT: identical
                (and (= dir "INOUT")
                     (forall ((scale CompuScale))
                         (and (=> (set.member scale cScales) (set.member scale sScales))
                              (=> (set.member scale sScales) (set.member scale cScales))))))))))



;; Type declarations
(declare-sort EndToEndProtection 0)
(declare-sort EndToEndProtectionVariablePrototype 0)
(declare-sort Sender 0)

(declare-sort ArVariableInImplementationDataInstanceRef 0)

(declare-sort AutosarDataType 0)
