;; EndToEndProtection sender consistency (constr_1183)
(declare-fun aggregatedBy (EndToEndProtectionVariablePrototype EndToEndProtection) Bool)
(declare-fun senderOf (EndToEndProtectionVariablePrototype) Sender)

(assert (forall ((proto1 EndToEndProtectionVariablePrototype) 
                 (proto2 EndToEndProtectionVariablePrototype)
                 (protection EndToEndProtection))
    (=> (and (aggregatedBy proto1 protection) (aggregatedBy proto2 protection))
        (= (senderOf proto1) (senderOf proto2)))))

;; Data type consistency (constr_1185)
(declare-fun rootDataPrototypeType (ApplicationCompositeElementInPortInterfaceInstanceRef) AutosarDataType)
(declare-fun contextEnclosedIn (DataPrototype AutosarDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun targetEnclosedIn (DataPrototype AutosarDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun contextDataPrototypeRef (ApplicationCompositeElementInPortInterfaceInstanceRef Int) DataPrototype)
(declare-fun targetDataPrototypeRef (ApplicationCompositeElementInPortInterfaceInstanceRef) DataPrototype)
(declare-fun numContextProtos (ApplicationCompositeElementInPortInterfaceInstanceRef) Int)

(assert (forall ((iref ApplicationCompositeElementInPortInterfaceInstanceRef))
    (let ((rootType (rootDataPrototypeType iref)))
        (and
            (forall ((i Int))
                (=> (and (>= i 0) (< i (numContextProtos iref)))
                    (contextEnclosedIn (contextDataPrototypeRef iref i) rootType)))
            (targetEnclosedIn (targetDataPrototypeRef iref) rootType)))))

;; Data type consistency for Implementation (constr_1186)
(declare-fun rootVariableDataPrototypeType (ArVariableInImplementationDataInstanceRef) ImplementationDataType)
(declare-fun implContextEnclosedIn (DataPrototype ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun implTargetEnclosedIn (DataPrototype ImplementationDataType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((iref ArVariableInImplementationDataInstanceRef))
    (let ((rootType (rootVariableDataPrototypeType iref)))
        (and
            (forall ((i Int))
                (=> (and (>= i 0) (< i (numContextProtos iref)))
                    (implContextEnclosedIn (contextDataPrototypeRef iref i) rootType)))
            (implTargetEnclosedIn (targetDataPrototypeRef iref) rootType)))))

;; Composite data type compatibility (constr_1187)
(declare-fun compositeDataTypesIdentical (ApplicationCompositeDataType ApplicationCompositeDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun sameNumberOfElements (ApplicationCompositeDataType ApplicationCompositeDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun elementsCompatibleInOrder (ApplicationCompositeDataType ApplicationCompositeDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun attributesMatchExceptShortName (ApplicationCompositeDataType ApplicationCompositeDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun subElementMappingExists (DataPrototype DataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun compatibleCompositeDataPrototypes (DataPrototype DataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun dataPrototypeTypedBy (DataPrototype ApplicationCompositeDataType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((proto1 DataPrototype) (proto2 DataPrototype) 
                 (type1 ApplicationCompositeDataType) (type2 ApplicationCompositeDataType))
    (=> (and (dataPrototypeTypedBy proto1 type1) (dataPrototypeTypedBy proto2 type2))
        (=> (compatibleCompositeDataPrototypes proto1 proto2)
            (or
                ;; Condition 1: Identical types
                (compositeDataTypesIdentical type1 type2)
                ;; Condition 2: Same structure
                (and (sameNumberOfElements type1 type2)
                     (elementsCompatibleInOrder type1 type2)
                     (attributesMatchExceptShortName type1 type2))
                ;; Condition 3 & 4: SubElementMapping exists
                (subElementMappingExists proto1 proto2))))))

;; ReceiverComSpec.replaceWith existence (constr_1188)
(declare-fun handleOutOfRange (ReceiverComSpec) String)
(declare-fun hasReplaceWith (ReceiverComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun comSpecForInterface (ReceiverComSpec SenderReceiverInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun invalidationPolicyHandleInvalid (InvalidationPolicy) String)
(declare-fun interfaceHasPolicy (SenderReceiverInterface InvalidationPolicy) Bool)

(assert (forall ((comSpec ReceiverComSpec) (iface SenderReceiverInterface))
    (=> (comSpecForInterface comSpec iface)
        (= (hasReplaceWith comSpec)
           (or (= (handleOutOfRange comSpec) "externalReplacement")
               (exists ((policy InvalidationPolicy))
                   (and (interfaceHasPolicy iface policy)
                        (= (invalidationPolicyHandleInvalid policy) "externalReplacement"))))))))

;; CompuMethod compatibility (constr_1192)
(declare-fun compuMethodCategory (CompuMethod) String)
(declare-fun coefficientN0 (CompuScale) Real)
(declare-fun coefficientN1 (CompuScale) Real)
(declare-fun coefficientD0 (CompuScale) Real)
(declare-fun coefficientD1 (CompuScale) Real)
(declare-fun compuScaleCompatibleIdenticalToRatFunc (CompuScale CompuScale) Bool)
(declare-fun scaleInCompuMethod (CompuScale CompuMethod) Bool)

(assert (forall ((scale1 CompuScale) (scale2 CompuScale) 
                 (method1 CompuMethod) (method2 CompuMethod))
    (=> (and (scaleInCompuMethod scale1 method1) (scaleInCompuMethod scale2 method2)
             (= (compuMethodCategory method1) "IDENTICAL")
             (or (= (compuMethodCategory method2) "RAT_FUNC") 
                 (= (compuMethodCategory method2) "LINEAR")))
        (=> (compuScaleCompatibleIdenticalToRatFunc scale1 scale2)
            (and (< (abs (coefficientN0 scale1)) 0.001)
                 (< (abs (- (coefficientD0 scale1) 1.0)) 0.001)
                 (< (abs (- (coefficientN1 scale1) 1.0)) 0.001)
                 (< (abs (coefficientD1 scale1)) 0.001))))))

;; ModeDeclaration referenced by ModeTransition (constr_1193)
(declare-fun modeInGroup (ModeDeclaration ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun transitionInGroup (ModeTransition ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun transitionEntersMode (ModeTransition ModeDeclaration) Bool)
(declare-fun isInitialMode (ModeDeclaration ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun groupHasTransitions (ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)

(assert (forall ((mode ModeDeclaration) (group ModeDeclarationGroup))
    (=> (and (modeInGroup mode group) 
             (groupHasTransitions group)
             (not (isInitialMode mode group)))
        (exists ((trans ModeTransition))
            (and (transitionInGroup trans group)
                 (transitionEntersMode trans mode))))))

;; Identical ModeTransitions (constr_1194)
(declare-fun transitionShortName (ModeTransition) String)
(declare-fun modeShortName (ModeDeclaration) String)
(declare-fun transitionExitsMode (ModeTransition ModeDeclaration) Bool)
(declare-fun identicalTransitions (ModeDeclarationGroup ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)

(assert (forall ((group1 ModeDeclarationGroup) (group2 ModeDeclarationGroup))
    (=> (identicalTransitions group1 group2)
        (and
            ;; Condition 1: Same shortNames exist
            (forall ((trans1 ModeTransition))
                (=> (transitionInGroup trans1 group1)
                    (exists ((trans2 ModeTransition))
                        (and (transitionInGroup trans2 group2)
                             (= (transitionShortName trans1) (transitionShortName trans2))))))
            ;; Condition 2: Matching transitions have same targets
            (forall ((trans1 ModeTransition) (trans2 ModeTransition))
                (=> (and (transitionInGroup trans1 group1) (transitionInGroup trans2 group2)
                         (= (transitionShortName trans1) (transitionShortName trans2)))
                    (exists ((entered1 ModeDeclaration) (entered2 ModeDeclaration)
                            (exited1 ModeDeclaration) (exited2 ModeDeclaration))
                        (and (transitionEntersMode trans1 entered1)
                             (transitionEntersMode trans2 entered2)
                             (transitionExitsMode trans1 exited1)
                             (transitionExitsMode trans2 exited2)
                             (= (modeShortName entered1) (modeShortName entered2))
                             (= (modeShortName exited1) (modeShortName exited2))))))))))

;; SwcModeSwitchEvent and ModeTransition (constr_1195)
(declare-fun eventActivation (SwcModeSwitchEvent) String)
(declare-fun eventReferencesModePair (SwcModeSwitchEvent ModeDeclaration ModeDeclaration) Bool)
(declare-fun transitionExists (ModeDeclarationGroup ModeDeclaration ModeDeclaration) Bool)

(assert (forall ((event SwcModeSwitchEvent) (group ModeDeclarationGroup)
                 (exitedMode ModeDeclaration) (enteredMode ModeDeclaration))
    (=> (and (= (eventActivation event) "onTransition")
             (eventReferencesModePair event exitedMode enteredMode)
             (groupHasTransitions group))
        (transitionExists group exitedMode enteredMode))))

;; Network representation mutual exclusion (constr_1196)
(declare-fun hasNetworkRepresentation (ReceiverComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun hasCompositeNetworkRepresentation (ReceiverComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun hasNetworkRepresentationS (SenderComSpec) Bool)
(declare-fun hasCompositeNetworkRepresentationS (SenderComSpec) Bool)

(assert (forall ((comSpec ReceiverComSpec))
    (not (and (hasNetworkRepresentation comSpec) 
              (hasCompositeNetworkRepresentation comSpec)))))

(assert (forall ((comSpec SenderComSpec))
    (not (and (hasNetworkRepresentationS comSpec) 
              (hasCompositeNetworkRepresentationS comSpec)))))

;; Comprehensive compositeNetworkRepresentation (constr_1197)
(declare-fun leafElementOf (DataPrototype ApplicationCompositeDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun compositeNetworkRepForElement (ReceiverComSpec DataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun hasAnyCompositeNetworkRep (ReceiverComSpec ApplicationCompositeDataType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((comSpec ReceiverComSpec) (compType ApplicationCompositeDataType))
    (=> (hasAnyCompositeNetworkRep comSpec compType)
        (forall ((elem DataPrototype))
            (=> (leafElementOf elem compType)
                (compositeNetworkRepForElement comSpec elem))))))

;; PRPortPrototype queued constraint (constr_1200)
(declare-fun dataElementOwnedBy (VariableDataPrototype PRPortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun dataElementSwImplPolicy (VariableDataPrototype) String)

(assert (forall ((dataElem VariableDataPrototype) (prPort PRPortPrototype))
    (=> (dataElementOwnedBy dataElem prPort)
        (not (= (dataElementSwImplPolicy dataElem) "queued")))))

;; initValue in RPortPrototype (constr_1201)
(declare-fun nonqueuedComSpecInRPort (NonqueuedReceiverComSpec RPortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun hasInitValue (NonqueuedReceiverComSpec) Bool)

(assert (forall ((comSpec NonqueuedReceiverComSpec) (rPort RPortPrototype))
    (=> (nonqueuedComSpecInRPort comSpec rPort)
        (hasInitValue comSpec))))

;; Supported connector connections (constr_1202-1205)
(declare-fun connectorProviderPort (AssemblySwConnector) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun connectorRequesterPort (AssemblySwConnector) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun portTypedByInterface (PortPrototype PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun isSenderReceiverInterface (PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun isNvDataInterface (PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun connectionAllowedBySRTable (PortPrototype PortPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((conn AssemblySwConnector) (iface PortInterface))
    (=> (and (portTypedByInterface (connectorProviderPort conn) iface)
             (or (isSenderReceiverInterface iface) (isNvDataInterface iface)))
        (connectionAllowedBySRTable 
            (connectorProviderPort conn) 
            (connectorRequesterPort conn)))))

;; ModeDeclaration mapping constraints (constr_1209)
(declare-sort ModeDeclarationMapping 0)
(declare-fun mappingUserMode (ModeDeclarationMapping) ModeDeclaration)
(declare-fun mappingManagerMode (ModeDeclarationMapping) ModeDeclaration)
(declare-fun isModeUser (ModeDeclaration) Bool)
(declare-fun isModeManager (ModeDeclaration) Bool)

(assert (forall ((map1 ModeDeclarationMapping) (map2 ModeDeclarationMapping))
    (=> (and (isModeUser (mappingUserMode map1))
             (isModeUser (mappingUserMode map2))
             (isModeManager (mappingManagerMode map1))
             (isModeManager (mappingManagerMode map2)))
        (=> (= (mappingManagerMode map1) (mappingManagerMode map2))
            (= map1 map2)))))

;; Complete mode mapping (constr_1210)
(declare-fun managerModeInGroup (ModeDeclaration ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun mappingExistsForMode (ModeDeclaration) Bool)

(assert (forall ((managerGroup ModeDeclarationGroup))
    (=> (exists ((mode ModeDeclaration) (mapping ModeDeclarationMapping))
            (and (managerModeInGroup mode managerGroup)
                 (= (mappingManagerMode mapping) mode)))
        (forall ((mode ModeDeclaration))
            (=> (managerModeInGroup mode managerGroup)
                (mappingExistsForMode mode))))))

;; Profile-specific value ranges (constr_1211-1213)
(declare-fun e2eCategory (EndToEndDescription) String)
(declare-fun maxNoNewOrRepeatedDataE2E (EndToEndDescription) Int)
(declare-fun maxNoNewOrRepeatedDataRCS (ReceiverComSpec) Int)
(declare-fun syncCounterInitE2E (EndToEndDescription) Int)
(declare-fun syncCounterInitRCS (ReceiverComSpec) Int)

;; constr_1211: PROFILE_01 maxNoNewOrRepeatedData range [0..14]
(assert (forall ((desc EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (= (e2eCategory desc) "PROFILE_01")
        (and (>= (maxNoNewOrRepeatedDataE2E desc) 0)
             (<= (maxNoNewOrRepeatedDataE2E desc) 14)))))

(assert (forall ((comSpec ReceiverComSpec) (desc EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (= (e2eCategory desc) "PROFILE_01")
        (and (>= (maxNoNewOrRepeatedDataRCS comSpec) 0)
             (<= (maxNoNewOrRepeatedDataRCS comSpec) 14)))))

;; constr_1212: PROFILE_01 syncCounterInit range [0..14]
(assert (forall ((desc EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (= (e2eCategory desc) "PROFILE_01")
        (and (>= (syncCounterInitE2E desc) 0)
             (<= (syncCounterInitE2E desc) 14)))))

;; constr_1213: PROFILE_02 maxNoNewOrRepeatedData range [0..15]
(assert (forall ((desc EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (= (e2eCategory desc) "PROFILE_02")
        (and (>= (maxNoNewOrRepeatedDataE2E desc) 0)
             (<= (maxNoNewOrRepeatedDataE2E desc) 15)))))



;; Type declarations
(declare-sort NumericalOrText 0)

(declare-sort ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype 0)
(declare-sort ModeDeclarationGroupPrototypeMapping 0)

(declare-sort DataInterface 0)
(declare-sort PortInterface 0)
(declare-sort VariableDataPrototype 0)
(declare-sort ParameterDataPrototype 0)
(declare-sort ModeSwitchInterface 0)
(declare-sort ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype 0)
(declare-sort ClientServerInterface 0)
(declare-sort ClientServerOperation 0)
(declare-sort TriggerInterface 0)
(declare-sort Trigger 0)
(declare-sort PassThroughSwConnector 0)

(declare-sort PhysicalDimension 0)

;; NumericalOrText vf/vt exclusion (constr_1243)
(declare-fun hasVf (NumericalOrText) Bool)
(declare-fun hasVt (NumericalOrText) Bool)

(assert (forall ((not NumericalOrText))
    (or (and (hasVf not) (not (hasVt not)))
        (and (not (hasVf not)) (hasVt not)))))

;; DataPrototype C enum restriction (constr_1244)
(declare-fun prototypeInComponent (DataPrototype AtomicSwComponentType) Bool)
(declare-fun additionalNativeTypeQualifier (DataPrototype) String)

(assert (forall ((proto DataPrototype) (comp AtomicSwComponentType))
    (=> (prototypeInComponent proto comp)
        (not (= (additionalNativeTypeQualifier proto) "enum")))))

;; ModeDeclarationMappingSet consistency (constr_1246)
(declare-fun mappingInSet (ModeDeclarationMapping ModeDeclarationMappingSet) Bool)
(declare-fun firstModeOfMapping (ModeDeclarationMapping Int) ModeDeclaration)
(declare-fun secondModeOfMapping (ModeDeclarationMapping) ModeDeclaration)
(declare-fun modeInGroup (ModeDeclaration ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun numFirstModes (ModeDeclarationMapping) Int)

(assert (forall ((set ModeDeclarationMappingSet))
    (exists ((group1 ModeDeclarationGroup) (group2 ModeDeclarationGroup))
        (and
            ;; All firstModes in one group
            (forall ((map ModeDeclarationMapping))
                (=> (mappingInSet map set)
                    (forall ((i Int))
                        (=> (and (>= i 0) (< i (numFirstModes map)))
                            (modeInGroup (firstModeOfMapping map i) group1)))))
            ;; All secondModes in another group
            (forall ((map ModeDeclarationMapping))
                (=> (mappingInSet map set)
                    (modeInGroup (secondModeOfMapping map) group2)))
            ;; Groups are different
            (not (= group1 group2))))))

;; ModeDeclarationGroupPrototypeMapping consistency (constr_1247)
(declare-fun mappingSetRef (ModeDeclarationGroupPrototypeMapping) ModeDeclarationMappingSet)
(declare-fun firstModeGroupType (ModeDeclarationGroupPrototypeMapping) ModeDeclarationGroup)
(declare-fun secondModeGroupType (ModeDeclarationGroupPrototypeMapping) ModeDeclarationGroup)
(declare-fun hasMappingSet (ModeDeclarationGroupPrototypeMapping) Bool)
(declare-fun setFirstGroup (ModeDeclarationMappingSet) ModeDeclarationGroup)
(declare-fun setSecondGroup (ModeDeclarationMappingSet) ModeDeclarationGroup)

(assert (forall ((protoMap ModeDeclarationGroupPrototypeMapping))
    (=> (hasMappingSet protoMap)
        (let ((set (mappingSetRef protoMap)))
            (and (= (firstModeGroupType protoMap) (setFirstGroup set))
                 (= (secondModeGroupType protoMap) (setSecondGroup set)))))))

;; PassThroughSwConnector compatibility for DataInterfaces (constr_1248)
(declare-fun requiredOuterPort (PassThroughSwConnector) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun providedOuterPort (PassThroughSwConnector) PortPrototype)
(declare-fun portTypedByInterface (PortPrototype PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun isService (PortInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun dataProtoInInterface (VariableDataPrototype DataInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun dataProtoCompatible (VariableDataPrototype VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun protoShortName (VariableDataPrototype) String)
(declare-fun portInterfaceMappingExists (VariableDataPrototype VariableDataPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((conn PassThroughSwConnector) 
                 (reqIface DataInterface) (provIface DataInterface))
    (=> (and (portTypedByInterface (requiredOuterPort conn) reqIface)
             (portTypedByInterface (providedOuterPort conn) provIface))
        (and
            ;; Condition 1: At least one compatible pair exists
            (exists ((reqProto VariableDataPrototype) (provProto VariableDataPrototype))
                (and (dataProtoInInterface reqProto reqIface)
                     (dataProtoInInterface provProto provIface)
                     (dataProtoCompatible reqProto provProto)
                     (or (= (protoShortName reqProto) (protoShortName provProto))
                         (portInterfaceMappingExists reqProto provProto))))
            ;; Condition 2: isService attributes identical
            (= (isService reqIface) (isService provIface))))))

;; PassThroughSwConnector compatibility for ModeSwitchInterfaces (constr_1249)
(declare-fun modeGroupProtoInInterface (ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype ModeSwitchInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun modeGroupProtoCompatible (ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun modeGroupShortName (ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype) String)
(declare-fun modeInterfaceMappingExists (ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((conn PassThroughSwConnector)
                 (reqIface ModeSwitchInterface) (provIface ModeSwitchInterface))
    (=> (and (portTypedByInterface (requiredOuterPort conn) reqIface)
             (portTypedByInterface (providedOuterPort conn) provIface))
        (and
            (exists ((reqProto ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype) (provProto ModeDeclarationGroupPrototype))
                (and (modeGroupProtoInInterface reqProto reqIface)
                     (modeGroupProtoInInterface provProto provIface)
                     (modeGroupProtoCompatible reqProto provProto)
                     (or (= (modeGroupShortName reqProto) (modeGroupShortName provProto))
                         (modeInterfaceMappingExists reqProto provProto))))
            (= (isService reqIface) (isService provIface))))))

;; PassThroughSwConnector compatibility for ClientServerInterfaces (constr_1250)
(declare-fun operationInInterface (ClientServerOperation ClientServerInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun operationsCompatible (ClientServerOperation ClientServerOperation) Bool)
(declare-fun operationShortName (ClientServerOperation) String)
(declare-fun csInterfaceMappingExists (ClientServerOperation ClientServerOperation) Bool)

(assert (forall ((conn PassThroughSwConnector)
                 (reqIface ClientServerInterface) (provIface ClientServerInterface))
    (=> (and (portTypedByInterface (requiredOuterPort conn) reqIface)
             (portTypedByInterface (providedOuterPort conn) provIface))
        (and
            (exists ((reqOp ClientServerOperation) (provOp ClientServerOperation))
                (and (operationInInterface reqOp reqIface)
                     (operationInInterface provOp provIface)
                     (operationsCompatible reqOp provOp)
                     (or (= (operationShortName reqOp) (operationShortName provOp))
                         (csInterfaceMappingExists reqOp provOp))))
            (= (isService reqIface) (isService provIface))))))

;; PassThroughSwConnector compatibility for TriggerInterfaces (constr_1251)
(declare-fun triggerInInterface (Trigger TriggerInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun triggersCompatible (Trigger Trigger) Bool)
(declare-fun triggerShortName (Trigger) String)

(assert (forall ((conn PassThroughSwConnector)
                 (reqIface TriggerInterface) (provIface TriggerInterface))
    (=> (and (portTypedByInterface (requiredOuterPort conn) reqIface)
             (portTypedByInterface (providedOuterPort conn) provIface))
        (and
            (exists ((reqTrig Trigger) (provTrig Trigger))
                (and (triggerInInterface reqTrig reqIface)
                     (triggerInInterface provTrig provIface)
                     (triggersCompatible reqTrig provTrig)
                     (= (triggerShortName reqTrig) (triggerShortName provTrig))))
            (= (isService reqIface) (isService provIface))))))

;; PassThroughSwConnector loop prevention (constr_1252)
(declare-sort SwConnector 0)
(declare-fun connectorChainReaches (PortPrototype PortPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun atomicSwComponentInChain (PortPrototype PortPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((conn PassThroughSwConnector))
    (=> (connectorChainReaches (requiredOuterPort conn) (providedOuterPort conn))
        (atomicSwComponentInChain (requiredOuterPort conn) (providedOuterPort conn)))))

;; Unit restriction for BOOLEAN and STRING (constr_1255)
(declare-fun adtCategory (ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) String)
(declare-fun unitReferencedBy (Unit ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun unitPhysicalDimension (Unit) PhysicalDimension)
(declare-fun lengthExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun massExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun timeExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun currentExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun temperatureExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun molarAmountExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)
(declare-fun luminousIntensityExp (PhysicalDimension) Int)

(assert (forall ((adt ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) (unit Unit) (physDim PhysicalDimension))
    (=> (and (or (= (adtCategory adt) "BOOLEAN") (= (adtCategory adt) "STRING"))
             (unitReferencedBy unit adt)
             (= (unitPhysicalDimension unit) physDim))
        (and (= (lengthExp physDim) 0)
             (= (massExp physDim) 0)
             (= (timeExp physDim) 0)
             (= (currentExp physDim) 0)
             (= (temperatureExp physDim) 0)
             (= (molarAmountExp physDim) 0)
             (= (luminousIntensityExp physDim) 0)))))

;; Acknowledgement feedback in n:1 writer case (constr_1256)
(declare-fun runnableInBehavior (RunnableEntity SwcInternalBehavior) Bool)
(declare-fun dataSendPointOf (VariableAccess RunnableEntity) Bool)
(declare-fun dataWriteAccessOf (VariableAccess RunnableEntity) Bool)
(declare-fun accessedVariableTarget (VariableAccess) VariableDataPrototype)
(declare-fun accessedVariablePort (VariableAccess) PPortPrototype)
(declare-fun hasTransmissionAcknowledge (SenderComSpec VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun comSpecForPort (SenderComSpec PPortPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((behavior SwcInternalBehavior))
    (forall ((run1 RunnableEntity) (run2 RunnableEntity) 
             (access1 VariableAccess) (access2 VariableAccess))
        (=> (and (runnableInBehavior run1 behavior) (runnableInBehavior run2 behavior)
                 (not (= run1 run2))
                 (or (dataSendPointOf access1 run1) (dataWriteAccessOf access1 run1))
                 (or (dataSendPointOf access2 run2) (dataWriteAccessOf access2 run2))
                 (= (accessedVariableTarget access1) (accessedVariableTarget access2))
                 (exists ((comSpec SenderComSpec))
                     (and (comSpecForPort comSpec (accessedVariablePort access1))
                          (hasTransmissionAcknowledge comSpec (accessedVariableTarget access1)))))
            false))))

;; InitEvent constraints (constr_1257-1260)
(declare-fun eventStartsRunnable (InitEvent RunnableEntity) Bool)
(declare-fun runnableHasWaitPoint (RunnableEntity WaitPoint) Bool)
(declare-fun runnableHasAsyncCallPoint (RunnableEntity AsynchronousServerCallPoint) Bool)
(declare-fun runnableHasAsyncResultPoint (RunnableEntity AsynchronousServerCallResultPoint) Bool)
(declare-fun eventHasDisabledMode (InitEvent ModeDeclaration) Bool)
(declare-fun runnableMinimumStartInterval (RunnableEntity) Real)

;; constr_1257: No WaitPoints
(assert (forall ((event InitEvent) (runnable RunnableEntity) (wp WaitPoint))
    (=> (eventStartsRunnable event runnable)
        (not (runnableHasWaitPoint runnable wp)))))

;; constr_1258: minimumStartInterval = 0
(assert (forall ((event InitEvent) (runnable RunnableEntity))
    (=> (eventStartsRunnable event runnable)
        (= (runnableMinimumStartInterval runnable) 0.0))))

;; constr_1259: No AsynchronousServerCallResultPoint
(assert (forall ((event InitEvent) (runnable RunnableEntity) (resultPoint AsynchronousServerCallResultPoint))
    (=> (eventStartsRunnable event runnable)
        (not (runnableHasAsyncResultPoint runnable resultPoint)))))

;; constr_1260: No disabledMode
(assert (forall ((event InitEvent) (mode ModeDeclaration))
    (not (eventHasDisabledMode event mode))))

;; EndToEndDescription constraints (constr_1261)
(declare-fun e2eCategory (EndToEndDescription) String)
(declare-fun dataIdMode (EndToEndDescription) Int)
(declare-fun hasDataIdNibbleOffset (EndToEndDescription) Bool)

(assert (forall ((desc EndToEndDescription))
    (=> (hasDataIdNibbleOffset desc)
        (and (= (dataIdMode desc) 3)
             (= (e2eCategory desc) "PROFILE_01")))))

;; ModeErrorBehavior defaultMode existence (constr_1263)
(declare-fun errorReactionPolicy (ModeErrorBehavior) String)
(declare-fun hasDefaultMode (ModeErrorBehavior) Bool)

(assert (forall ((meb ModeErrorBehavior))
    (=> (= (errorReactionPolicy meb) "defaultMode")
        (hasDefaultMode meb))))

;; SwRecordLayoutV index constraint (constr_1264)
(declare-fun swRecordLayoutVCategory (SwRecordLayoutV) String)
(declare-fun swRecordLayoutVIndex (SwRecordLayoutV) Int)

(assert (forall ((layout SwRecordLayoutV))
    (=> (and (not (= (swRecordLayoutVCategory layout) "VALUE"))
             (not (= (swRecordLayoutVCategory layout) "VAL_BLK")))
        (not (= (swRecordLayoutVIndex layout) 0)))))

;; ClientServerOperationMapping constraints (constr_1268-1270)
(declare-fun firstOperationOf (ClientServerOperationMapping) ClientServerOperation)
(declare-fun secondOperationOf (ClientServerOperationMapping) ClientServerOperation)
(declare-fun argumentDirection (ArgumentDataPrototype) String)
(declare-fun argumentsInOperation (ClientServerOperation) Int)
(declare-fun mappingHasArgumentMapping (ClientServerOperationMapping DataPrototypeMapping) Bool)
(declare-fun mappingFirstProto (DataPrototypeMapping) ArgumentDataPrototype)
(declare-fun mappingSecondProto (DataPrototypeMapping) ArgumentDataPrototype)

;; constr_1268: Direction preservation
(assert (forall ((opMap ClientServerOperationMapping) (argMap DataPrototypeMapping))
    (=> (mappingHasArgumentMapping opMap argMap)
        (= (argumentDirection (mappingFirstProto argMap))
           (argumentDirection (mappingSecondProto argMap))))))

;; constr_1269: Same number of arguments
(assert (forall ((opMap ClientServerOperationMapping))
    (= (argumentsInOperation (firstOperationOf opMap))
       (argumentsInOperation (secondOperationOf opMap)))))

;; constr_1270: Each argument mapped once
(assert (forall ((opMap ClientServerOperationMapping) (arg ArgumentDataPrototype))
    (let ((count (ite (exists ((map DataPrototypeMapping))
                          (and (mappingHasArgumentMapping opMap map)
                               (or (= (mappingFirstProto map) arg)
                                   (= (mappingSecondProto map) arg))))
                      1 0)))
        (<= count 1))))

;; Dynamic array size profile (constr_1322)
(declare-fun idtCategory (ImplementationDataType) String)
(declare-fun idtHasDynamicArraySizeProfile (ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun adtHasDynamicArraySizeProfile (ApplicationArrayDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun idtMappedToAdt (ImplementationDataType ApplicationArrayDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun representsSizeIndicatorArray (ImplementationDataType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((idt ImplementationDataType) (adt ApplicationArrayDataType))
    (=> (and (not (idtHasDynamicArraySizeProfile idt))
             (idtMappedToAdt idt adt)
             (adtHasDynamicArraySizeProfile adt))
        (and (= (idtCategory idt) "STRUCTURE")
             (representsSizeIndicatorArray idt)))))

    (=> (hasTransformationComSpecProps comSpec)
        (not (usesEndToEndProtection comSpec)))))

;; DiagnosticValueNeeds attributes (constr_1363)

(assert (forall ((needs DiagnosticValueNeeds) (dep SwcServiceDependency))
    (=> (diagValueNeedsInServiceDep needs dep)
        (and (not (hasDataLength needs))
             (not (hasDiagnosticValueAccess needs))))))

;; DiagnosticIoControlNeeds attributes (constr_1364)

(assert (forall ((needs DiagnosticIoControlNeeds) (dep SwcServiceDependency))
    (=> (diagIoControlNeedsInServiceDep needs dep)
        (and (not (hasFreezeCurrentStateSupported needs))
             (not (hasShortTermAdjustmentSupported needs))))))

;; MemorySection/SwAddrMethod option cardinality (constr_1381)
(declare-fun memorySectionOptions (MemorySection) (Set String))
(declare-fun swAddrMethodOptions (SwAddrMethod) (Set String))

(assert (forall ((ms MemorySection))
    (let ((opts (memorySectionOptions ms)))
        (let ((hasCoreGlobal (set.member "coreGlobal" opts))
              (hasCoreLocal (set.member "coreLocal" opts)))
            (not (and hasCoreGlobal hasCoreLocal))))))

(assert (forall ((sam SwAddrMethod))
    (let ((opts (swAddrMethodOptions sam)))
        (let ((hasCoreGlobal (set.member "coreGlobal" opts))
              (hasCoreLocal (set.member "coreLocal" opts)))
            (not (and hasCoreGlobal hasCoreLocal))))))

;; SwVariableRefProxy mutual exclusion (constr_1382)
(declare-fun hasAutosarVariable (SwVariableRefProxy) Bool)
(declare-fun hasMcDataInstanceVar (SwVariableRefProxy) Bool)

(assert (forall ((proxy SwVariableRefProxy))
    (not (and (hasAutosarVariable proxy) (hasMcDataInstanceVar proxy)))))

;; TYPE_REFERENCE CompuMethod/DataConstr existence (constr_1383)
(declare-fun hasCompuMethod (ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun hasDataConstr (ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun resolvedCategory (ImplementationDataType) String)

(assert (forall ((idt ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (and (= (idtCategory idt) "TYPE_REFERENCE")
             (or (hasCompuMethod idt) (hasDataConstr idt)))
        (= (resolvedCategory idt) "VALUE"))))

;; invalidValue restriction for certain categories (constr_1384)
(declare-fun protoTypedByAdt (DataPrototype ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun adtCategory (ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) String)
(declare-fun protoHasInvalidValue (DataPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((proto DataPrototype) (adt ApplicationPrimitiveDataType))
    (=> (and (protoTypedByAdt proto adt)
             (or (= (adtCategory adt) "CURVE")
                 (= (adtCategory adt) "MAP")
                 (= (adtCategory adt) "CUBOID")
                 (= (adtCategory adt) "CUBE_4")
                 (= (adtCategory adt) "CUBE_5")
                 (= (adtCategory adt) "COM_AXIS")
                 (= (adtCategory adt) "RES_AXIS")
                 (= (adtCategory adt) "VAL_BLK")))
        (not (protoHasInvalidValue proto)))))

;; ImplementationDataType initValue restriction (constr_1385)
(declare-fun protoTypedByIdt (DataPrototype ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun initValueIsApplicationValueSpec (DataPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((proto DataPrototype) (idt ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (protoTypedByIdt proto idt)
        (not (initValueIsApplicationValueSpec proto)))))

;; PortDefinedArgumentValue restriction (constr_1386)
(declare-fun portAPIOptionHasArgValue (PortAPIOption PortDefinedArgumentValue) Bool)
(declare-fun portAPIOptionPort (PortAPIOption) AbstractProvidedPortPrototype)
(declare-fun portTypedByCSInterface (AbstractProvidedPortPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((option PortAPIOption) (argValue PortDefinedArgumentValue))
    (=> (portAPIOptionHasArgValue option argValue)
        (portTypedByCSInterface (portAPIOptionPort option)))))

;; VariationPointProxy category VALUE restriction (constr_1388)
(declare-fun vppCategory (VariationPointProxy) String)
(declare-fun vppMixesPrePostBuild (VariationPointProxy) Bool)

(assert (forall ((vpp VariationPointProxy))
    (=> (= (vppCategory vpp) "VALUE")
        (not (vppMixesPrePostBuild vpp)))))

;; VariationPointProxy implementationDataType category restriction (constr_1389)
(declare-fun vppImplementationDataType (VariationPointProxy) ImplementationDataType)
(declare-fun hasVppImplementationDataType (VariationPointProxy) Bool)

(assert (forall ((vpp VariationPointProxy))
    (=> (hasVppImplementationDataType vpp)
        (let ((idt (vppImplementationDataType vpp)))
            (let ((cat (idtCategory idt)))
                (and (not (= cat "STRUCTURE"))
                     (not (= cat "ARRAY"))
                     (not (= cat "UNION"))
                     (not (= cat "FUNCTION_REFERENCE"))
                     (not (= cat "DATA_REFERENCE"))
                     (or (= cat "VALUE")
                         (and (= cat "TYPE_REFERENCE")
                              (= (resolvedCategory idt) "VALUE")))))))))

;; invalidValue vs CompuMethod interval (constr_1390)
(declare-fun handleInvalidValue (InvalidationPolicy) String)
(declare-fun dataElementInvalidValue (VariableDataPrototype) Real)
(declare-fun dataElementCompuMethod (VariableDataPrototype) CompuMethod)
(declare-fun valueInCompuMethodInterval (Real CompuMethod) Bool)
(declare-fun hasInvalidValueDef (VariableDataPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((policy InvalidationPolicy) (dataElem VariableDataPrototype))
    (=> (and (not (= (handleInvalidValue policy) "dontInvalidate"))
             (hasInvalidValueDef dataElem))
        (not (valueInCompuMethodInterval 
              (dataElementInvalidValue dataElem)
              (dataElementCompuMethod dataElem))))))

;; Unit compatibility for ApplicationValueSpecification (constr_1391)
(declare-fun avSpecSwValueContUnit (ApplicationValueSpecification) Unit)
(declare-fun protoDataTypeUnit (AutosarDataPrototype) Unit)
(declare-fun unitsCompatible (Unit Unit) Bool)
(declare-fun assignedToProto (ApplicationValueSpecification AutosarDataPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((avSpec ApplicationValueSpecification) (proto AutosarDataPrototype))
    (=> (assignedToProto avSpec proto)
        (unitsCompatible 
            (avSpecSwValueContUnit avSpec)
            (protoDataTypeUnit proto)))))

;; Unit compatibility for ApplicationRuleBasedValueSpecification (constr_1392)
(declare-fun arbvSpecSwValueContUnit (ApplicationRuleBasedValueSpecification) Unit)
(declare-fun assignedToProtoRuleBased (ApplicationRuleBasedValueSpecification AutosarDataPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((arbvSpec ApplicationRuleBasedValueSpecification) (proto AutosarDataPrototype))
    (=> (assignedToProtoRuleBased arbvSpec proto)
        (unitsCompatible 
            (arbvSpecSwValueContUnit arbvSpec)
            (protoDataTypeUnit proto)))))

;; RuleBasedValueCont.unit existence (constr_1393)
(declare-fun ruleBasedValueContHasUnit (RuleBasedValueCont) Bool)

(assert (forall ((rbvc RuleBasedValueCont))
    (ruleBasedValueContHasUnit rbvc)))

;; NvBlockDataMapping completeness (constr_1395)
(declare-fun nvDataInInterface (VariableDataPrototype NvDataInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun allSubElementsMapped (NvDataInterface) Bool)

(assert (forall ((iface NvDataInterface))
    (=> (exists ((vdp VariableDataPrototype))
            (nvDataInInterface vdp iface))
        (allSubElementsMapped iface))))

;; Variable-Size Array category restriction (constr_1396)
(declare-fun isNonTerminatingElement (ImplementationDataTypeElement) Bool)
(declare-fun modelsVariableSizeArray (ImplementationDataTypeElement) Bool)
(declare-fun elementCategory (ImplementationDataTypeElement) String)

(assert (forall ((elem ImplementationDataTypeElement))
    (=> (and (isNonTerminatingElement elem)
             (modelsVariableSizeArray elem))
        (not (= (elementCategory elem) "TYPE_REFERENCE")))))

;; TransformerHardErrorEvent mutual exclusion (constr_1397)
(declare-fun hasOperation (TransformerHardErrorEvent) Bool)
(declare-fun hasTrigger (TransformerHardErrorEvent) Bool)

(assert (forall ((event TransformerHardErrorEvent))
    (and (or (hasOperation event) (hasTrigger event))
         (not (and (hasOperation event) (hasTrigger event))))))

;; BaseTypeDirectDefinition byteOrder for UTF-16 (constr_1398)
(declare-fun baseTypeEncoding (BaseTypeDirectDefinition) String)
(declare-fun hasByteOrder (BaseTypeDirectDefinition) Bool)
(declare-fun byteOrderValue (BaseTypeDirectDefinition) String)

(assert (forall ((btdd BaseTypeDirectDefinition))
    (=> (= (baseTypeEncoding btdd) "UTF-16")
        (and (hasByteOrder btdd)
             (or (= (byteOrderValue btdd) "mostSignificantByteFirst")
                 (= (byteOrderValue btdd) "mostSignificantByteLast"))))))

;; ModeDeclarationGroup.category standardized values (constr_1399)
(declare-fun modeDeclarationGroupCategory (ModeDeclarationGroup) String)
(declare-fun hasCategoryMDG (ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)

(assert (forall ((mdg ModeDeclarationGroup))
    (=> (hasCategoryMDG mdg)
        (or (= (modeDeclarationGroupCategory mdg) "EXPLICIT_ORDER")
            (= (modeDeclarationGroupCategory mdg) "ALPHABETIC_ORDER")))))

;; DataTransformation reference uniqueness (constr_1400)
(declare-fun dtReferencedByDataProtoMapping (DataTransformation DataPrototypeMapping) Bool)
(declare-fun dtReferencedByISignal (DataTransformation ISignal) Bool)
(declare-fun dtReferencedByISignalGroup (DataTransformation ISignalGroup) Bool)

(assert (forall ((dt DataTransformation))
    (let ((inProtoMapping (exists ((dpm DataPrototypeMapping)) (dtReferencedByDataProtoMapping dt dpm)))
          (inISignal (exists ((isig ISignal)) (dtReferencedByISignal dt isig)))
          (inISignalGroup (exists ((isg ISignalGroup)) (dtReferencedByISignalGroup dt isg))))
        ;; At most one of these can be true
        (or (and inProtoMapping (not inISignal) (not inISignalGroup))
            (and (not inProtoMapping) inISignal (not inISignalGroup))
            (and (not inProtoMapping) (not inISignal) inISignalGroup)
            (and (not inProtoMapping) (not inISignal) (not inISignalGroup))))))

;; DataPrototypeMapping and DataTransformation restrictions (constr_1401)
(declare-fun vdpInPortProto (VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun dataProtoMappingRefsVdp (DataPrototypeMapping VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun dataProtoMappingRefsDataTransformation (DataPrototypeMapping DataTransformation) Bool)
(declare-fun dataMappingPointsToVdp (DataMapping VariableDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun dataMappingRefsISignalWithDT (DataMapping ISignal DataTransformation) Bool)

(assert (forall ((vdp VariableDataPrototype) (dpm DataPrototypeMapping) 
                 (dt1 DataTransformation) (dm DataMapping) 
                 (isig ISignal) (dt2 DataTransformation))
    (=> (and (vdpInPortProto vdp)
             (dataProtoMappingRefsVdp dpm vdp)
             (dataProtoMappingRefsDataTransformation dpm dt1)
             (dataMappingPointsToVdp dm vdp)
             (dataMappingRefsISignalWithDT dm isig dt2))
        false)))

;; Value axis units consistency (constr_2550)
(declare-fun valueAxisDataType (SwDataDefPropsContent) ApplicationPrimitiveDataType)
(declare-fun swDataDefPropsUnit (SwDataDefPropsContent) Unit)
(declare-fun compuMethodOfSwDataDefProps (SwDataDefPropsContent) CompuMethod)
(declare-fun inSameValueAxisContext (SwDataDefPropsContent SwDataDefPropsContent) Bool)

(assert (forall ((props1 SwDataDefPropsContent) (props2 SwDataDefPropsContent))
    (=> (inSameValueAxisContext props1 props2)
        (= (swDataDefPropsUnit props1) (swDataDefPropsUnit props2)))))

;; DataConstrRule.constrLevel values (constr_2561)
(declare-fun constrLevel (DataConstrRule) Int)
(declare-fun hasConstrLevel (DataConstrRule) Bool)

;; Note: AUTOSAR scope values are 0 and 1, others allowed but outside scope
;; This is more of a documentation constraint than enforceable restriction
(assert (forall ((rule DataConstrRule))
    (=> (hasConstrLevel rule)
        (>= (constrLevel rule) 0))))

;; ModeDeclarationGroup mapping uniqueness (constr_4002)
(declare-fun mappingInSet (ModeDeclarationGroup ImplementationDataType DataTypeMappingSet) Bool)

(assert (forall ((mdg ModeDeclarationGroup) (idt1 ImplementationDataType) 
                 (idt2 ImplementationDataType) (mappingSet DataTypeMappingSet))
    (=> (and (mappingInSet mdg idt1 mappingSet)
             (mappingInSet mdg idt2 mappingSet)
             (not (= idt1 idt2)))
        false)))

;; SwcModeSwitchEvent semantics (constr_4003)
(declare-fun eventActivation (SwcModeSwitchEvent) String)
(declare-fun eventReferencesModes (SwcModeSwitchEvent) (Set ModeDeclaration))
(declare-fun modesInSameGroup (ModeDeclaration ModeDeclaration) Bool)

(assert (forall ((event SwcModeSwitchEvent))
    (let ((modes (eventReferencesModes event)))
        (ite (= (eventActivation event) "onTransition")
            ;; Must reference exactly 2 different modes from same group
            (and (= (set.card modes) 2)
                 (exists ((mode1 ModeDeclaration) (mode2 ModeDeclaration))
                     (and (set.member mode1 modes)
                          (set.member mode2 modes)
                          (not (= mode1 mode2))
                          (modesInSameGroup mode1 mode2))))
            ;; Must reference exactly 1 mode
            (= (set.card modes) 1)))))

;; SenderReceiverAnnotation context (constr_4004)
(declare-fun portHasSRAnnotation (PortPrototype SenderReceiverAnnotation) Bool)
(declare-fun portTypedBySRInterface (PortPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((port PortPrototype) (annotation SenderReceiverAnnotation))
    (=> (portHasSRAnnotation port annotation)
        (portTypedBySRInterface port))))

;; ClientServerAnnotation context (constr_4005)
(declare-fun portHasCSAnnotation (PortPrototype ClientServerAnnotation) Bool)
(declare-fun portTypedByCSInterface (PortPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((port PortPrototype) (annotation ClientServerAnnotation))
    (=> (portHasCSAnnotation port annotation)
        (portTypedByCSInterface port))))

;; ParameterPortAnnotation context (constr_4006)
(declare-fun pportHasParamAnnotation (PPortPrototype ParameterPortAnnotation) Bool)
(declare-fun pportOwnedByParamSwcType (PPortPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((pport PPortPrototype) (annotation ParameterPortAnnotation))
    (=> (pportHasParamAnnotation pport annotation)
        (pportOwnedByParamSwcType pport))))

;; ModePortAnnotation context (constr_4007)
(declare-fun portHasModeAnnotation (PortPrototype ModePortAnnotation) Bool)
(declare-fun portTypedByModeSwitchInterface (PortPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((port PortPrototype) (annotation ModePortAnnotation))
    (=> (portHasModeAnnotation port annotation)
        (portTypedByModeSwitchInterface port))))

;; TriggerPortAnnotation context (constr_4008)
(declare-fun portHasTriggerAnnotation (PortPrototype TriggerPortAnnotation) Bool)
(declare-fun portTypedByTriggerInterface (PortPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((port PortPrototype) (annotation TriggerPortAnnotation))
    (=> (portHasTriggerAnnotation port annotation)
        (portTypedByTriggerInterface port))))

;; NvDataPortAnnotation context (constr_4009)
(declare-fun portHasNvDataAnnotation (PortPrototype NvDataPortAnnotation) Bool)
(declare-fun portTypedByNvDataInterface (PortPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((port PortPrototype) (annotation NvDataPortAnnotation))
    (=> (portHasNvDataAnnotation port annotation)
        (portTypedByNvDataInterface port))))

;; DelegatedPortAnnotation context (constr_4010)
(declare-fun portHasDelegatedAnnotation (PortPrototype DelegatedPortAnnotation) Bool)
(declare-fun portInCompositionSwcType (PortPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((port PortPrototype) (annotation DelegatedPortAnnotation))
    (=> (portHasDelegatedAnnotation port annotation)
        (portInCompositionSwcType port))))

;; ModeSwitchedAckEvent timeout equality (constr_4012)
(declare-fun waitPointForAckEvent (WaitPoint ModeSwitchedAckEvent) Bool)
(declare-fun ackRequestForEvent (ModeSwitchedAckRequest ModeSwitchedAckEvent) Bool)
(declare-fun waitPointTimeout (WaitPoint) Real)
(declare-fun ackRequestTimeout (ModeSwitchedAckRequest) Real)

(assert (forall ((wp WaitPoint) (event ModeSwitchedAckEvent) (req ModeSwitchedAckRequest))
    (=> (and (waitPointForAckEvent wp event)
             (ackRequestForEvent req event))
        (= (waitPointTimeout wp) (ackRequestTimeout req)))))

;; ValueSpecification fits into data type (constr_4035)
(declare-fun valueSpecAssignedToType (ValueSpecification AutosarDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun valueFitsIntoType (ValueSpecification AutosarDataType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((valSpec ValueSpecification) (dataType AutosarDataType))
    (=> (valueSpecAssignedToType valSpec dataType)
        (valueFitsIntoType valSpec dataType))))

;; RunnableEntity.reentrancyLevel shall not be set (constr_4082)
(declare-fun hasReentrancyLevel (RunnableEntity) Bool)

(assert (forall ((runnable RunnableEntity))
    (not (hasReentrancyLevel runnable))))

;; Mode switch interface local communication (constr_4000)
;; This constraint requires knowledge of physical topology
(declare-fun portOnEcu (PortPrototype EcuInstance) Bool)
(declare-fun portsConnected (PortPrototype PortPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((port1 PortPrototype) (port2 PortPrototype) 
                 (ecu1 EcuInstance) (ecu2 EcuInstance))
    (=> (and (portTypedByModeSwitchInterface port1)
             (portsConnected port1 port2)
             (portOnEcu port1 ecu1)
             (portOnEcu port2 ecu2))
        (= ecu1 ecu2))))



; --- SMT Declarations (Unique Sorts) ---
(declare-sort ModeDeclarationGroup 0)
(declare-sort ModeDeclaration 0)
(declare-sort SwAxisCont 0)
(declare-sort SwValueCont 0)
(declare-sort SwcServiceDependency 0)
(declare-sort ServiceNeeds 0) ; Generic sort for 'serviceNeeds'
(declare-sort ObdRatioServiceNeeds 0)
(declare-sort RoleBasedPortAssignment 0)
(declare-sort ObdRatioConnectionKindEnum 0)
(declare-sort RapidPrototypingScenario 0)
(declare-sort RptSystemRef 0)
(declare-sort RptHook 0)
(declare-sort System 0)
(declare-sort ApplicationDataType 0)
(declare-sort ImplementationDataType 0)
(declare-sort CompuMethod 0)
(declare-sort BaseType 0)
(declare-sort Range 0) ; Abstract sort for limits/ranges
(declare-sort SwDataDefPropsContent 0)
(declare-sort Value 0) ; Abstract sort for values (like InvalidValue)
(declare-sort DataConstr 0)
(declare-sort ApplicationPrimitiveDataType 0)

(declare-sort Unit 0)

; --- constr_2049 (ModeDeclarationGroup uniqueness) ---
(declare-fun getShortName (ModeDeclarationGroup) String)
(declare-fun getModeDeclarations (ModeDeclarationGroup) (Set ModeDeclaration))
; "A software component is not allowed to type... ModeDeclarationGroups with identical shortNames but different ModeDeclarations."
; This implies: (Same shortName) => (Same ModeDeclarations)
(assert (forall ((mdg1 ModeDeclarationGroup) (mdg2 ModeDeclarationGroup))
  (=> (= (getShortName mdg1) (getShortName mdg2))
      (= (getModeDeclarations mdg1) (getModeDeclarations mdg2)))
))

; --- constr_2052 (swArraySize vs swValuesPhys count consistency) ---
; Since set.card is disallowed, we declare abstract count/product functions.
(declare-fun getSwArraySizeProduct_Axis (SwAxisCont) Int)
(declare-fun getSwValuesPhysCount_Axis (SwAxisCont) Int)
(declare-fun getSwArraySizeProduct_VC (SwValueCont) Int)
(declare-fun getSwValuesPhysCount_VC (SwValueCont) Int)
; Constraint for SW-AXIS-CONT
(assert (forall ((ax SwAxisCont))
  (= (getSwArraySizeProduct_Axis ax) (getSwValuesPhysCount_Axis ax))
))
; Constraint for SW-VALUE-CONT
(assert (forall ((vc SwValueCont))
  (= (getSwArraySizeProduct_VC vc) (getSwValuesPhysCount_VC vc))
))

; --- constr_2053 (Consistency between role IUMPRNumerator and connectionType) ---
(declare-const apiUse ObdRatioConnectionKindEnum)
(declare-const observer ObdRatioConnectionKindEnum)
(declare-fun getServiceNeeds (SwcServiceDependency) ServiceNeeds)
(declare-fun is-ObdRatioServiceNeeds (ServiceNeeds) Bool)
(declare-fun getNeedsAsObdRatio (ServiceNeeds) ObdRatioServiceNeeds) ; Cast
(declare-fun getNeedsConnectionType (ObdRatioServiceNeeds) ObdRatioConnectionKindEnum)
(declare-fun getAssignmentRole (RoleBasedPortAssignment) String)

; "If... connectionType... is set to ObdRatioConnectionKindEnum.apiUse a RoleBasedPortAssignment... with the role value IUMPRNumerator shall be defined."
(assert (forall ((ssd SwcServiceDependency))
  (let ((needs (getServiceNeeds ssd)))
    (=> (and (is-ObdRatioServiceNeeds needs)
             (= (getNeedsConnectionType (getNeedsAsObdRatio needs)) apiUse))
        (exists ((rbpa RoleBasedPortAssignment)) (= (getAssignmentRole rbpa) "IUMPRNumerator"))
    )
  )
))
; "If... connectionType... is set to ObdRatioConnectionKindEnum.observer the role value IUMPRNumerator is not applicable."
(assert (forall ((ssd SwcServiceDependency) (rbpa RoleBasedPortAssignment))
  (let ((needs (getServiceNeeds ssd)))
    (=> (and (is-ObdRatioServiceNeeds needs)
             (= (getNeedsConnectionType (getNeedsAsObdRatio needs)) observer))
        (not (= (getAssignmentRole rbpa) "IUMPRNumerator"))
    )
  )
))

; --- constr_2056 (Consistency of RapidPrototypingScenario references) ---
(declare-fun getRptSystemRefs (RapidPrototypingScenario) (Set RptSystemRef))
(declare-fun getRptHooks (RapidPrototypingScenario) (Set RptHook))
(declare-fun getTargetSystem (RptSystemRef) System)
(declare-fun getTargetSystemFromHook (RptHook) System)
(declare-fun hasRptSystemRefs (RapidPrototypingScenario) Bool)
(declare-fun hasRptHooks (RapidPrototypingScenario) Bool)

; "all rptSystem references shall point to... one and only one System"
(assert (forall ((rps RapidPrototypingScenario))
  (forall ((ref1 RptSystemRef) (ref2 RptSystemRef))
    (=> (and (set.member ref1 (getRptSystemRefs rps))
             (set.member ref2 (getRptSystemRefs rps)))
        (= (getTargetSystem ref1) (getTargetSystem ref2))
    )
  )
))
; "if existent all rptArHook shall point to... one other and only one other System"
(assert (forall ((rps RapidPrototypingScenario))
  (forall ((hook1 RptHook) (hook2 RptHook))
    (=> (and (set.member hook1 (getRptHooks rps))
             (set.member hook2 (getRptHooks rps)))
        (= (getTargetSystemFromHook hook1) (getTargetSystemFromHook hook2))
    )
  )
))
; "...one other System" (Disjointness)
(assert (forall ((rps RapidPrototypingScenario))
  (forall ((ref RptSystemRef) (hook RptHook))
    (=> (and (set.member ref (getRptSystemRefs rps))
             (set.member hook (getRptHooks rps)))
        (not (= (getTargetSystem ref) (getTargetSystemFromHook hook)))
    )
  )
))

; --- constr_2544 (Limits need to be consistent) ---
(declare-fun getAppLimits (ApplicationDataType) Range)
(declare-fun getCompuMethodRange (CompuMethod) Range)
(declare-fun getImplInternalConstrs (ImplementationDataType) Range)
(declare-fun getBaseTypeLimits (BaseType) Range)
(declare-fun getInternalLimits (ApplicationDataType) Range) ; Derived internal limits
(declare-fun isInside (Range Range) Bool) ; Predicate for range inclusion
; Helper functions to model relationships
(declare-fun referencesCompuMethod (ApplicationDataType CompuMethod) Bool)
(declare-fun mapsToImplDataType (ApplicationDataType ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun mapsToBasicType (ImplementationDataType BaseType) Bool)

; 1. App limits inside CompuMethod range
(assert (forall ((adt ApplicationDataType) (cm CompuMethod))
  (=> (referencesCompuMethod adt cm)
      (isInside (getAppLimits adt) (getCompuMethodRange cm)))
))
; 2. Internal limits (derived) inside Impl limits
(assert (forall ((adt ApplicationDataType) (idt ImplementationDataType))
  (=> (mapsToImplDataType adt idt)
      (isInside (getInternalLimits adt) (getImplInternalConstrs idt)))
))
; 3. Impl limits inside BaseType limits
(assert (forall ((idt ImplementationDataType) (bt BaseType))
  (=> (mapsToBasicType idt bt)
      (isInside (getImplInternalConstrs idt) (getBaseTypeLimits bt)))
))

; --- constr_2545 (invalidValue shall fit in the specified ranges) ---
(declare-fun getInvalidValue (SwDataDefPropsContent) Value)
(declare-fun getImplRange (ImplementationDataType) Range)
(declare-fun isValueInRange (Value Range) Bool)
; Helper function linking SwDataDefProps to its ImplementationDataType
(declare-fun definesInvalidValue (SwDataDefPropsContent ImplementationDataType) Bool)

; "The invalidValue shall be in the range of the ImplementationDataType."
(assert (forall ((sdp SwDataDefPropsContent) (idt ImplementationDataType))
  (=> (definesInvalidValue sdp idt)
      (isValueInRange (getInvalidValue sdp) (getImplRange idt)))
))

; --- constr_2548 (Data constraint of value axis shall match) ---
(declare-fun getDataConstr (SwDataDefPropsContent) DataConstr)
(declare-fun getValueAxisDataType (SwDataDefPropsContent) ApplicationPrimitiveDataType)
(declare-fun getDataConstrFromAppType (ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) DataConstr)
; Predicate for "is compliant to" (e.g., subset)
(declare-fun isCompliant (DataConstr DataConstr) Bool)

; "SwDataDefProps.dataConstr... not allowed to relax SwDataDefProps.valueAxisDataType.swDataDefProps.dataConstr"
(assert (forall ((sdp SwDataDefPropsContent))
  (isCompliant (getDataConstr sdp) (getDataConstrFromAppType (getValueAxisDataType sdp)))
))

; --- constr_2549 (Units of input axis shall be consistent) ---
(declare-fun getAllUnits (SwAxisIndividual) (Set Unit))
(declare-fun areCompatible (Unit Unit) Bool)

; "The units specified in the context of an input axis shall be compatible"
(assert (forall ((axis SwAxisIndividual))
  (forall ((u1 Unit) (u2 Unit))
    (=> (and (set.member u1 (getAllUnits axis))
             (set.member u2 (getAllUnits axis)))
        (areCompatible u1 u2)
    )
  )
))

;; swImplPolicy for ParameterDataPrototype in perInstanceParameter role (constr_2045)
(declare-fun paramInPerInstanceRole (ParameterDataPrototype) Bool)
(declare-fun paramSwImplPolicy (ParameterDataPrototype) String)

(assert (forall ((param ParameterDataPrototype))
    (=> (paramInPerInstanceRole param)
        (= (paramSwImplPolicy param) "standard"))))

;; swImplPolicy for ParameterDataPrototype in constantMemory role (constr_2046)
(declare-fun paramInConstantMemoryRole (ParameterDataPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((param ParameterDataPrototype))
    (=> (paramInConstantMemoryRole param)
        (or (= (paramSwImplPolicy param) "standard")
            (= (paramSwImplPolicy param) "const")
            (= (paramSwImplPolicy param) "fixed")))))

;; swImplPolicy for ArgumentDataPrototype (constr_2047)
(declare-fun argSwImplPolicy (ArgumentDataPrototype) String)

(assert (forall ((arg ArgumentDataPrototype))
    (= (argSwImplPolicy arg) "standard")))

;; swImplPolicy for SwServiceArg (constr_2048)
(declare-fun serviceArgSwImplPolicy (SwServiceArg) String)

(assert (forall ((srvArg SwServiceArg))
    (or (= (serviceArgSwImplPolicy srvArg) "standard")
        (= (serviceArgSwImplPolicy srvArg) "const"))))

;; Different ModeDeclarationGroups with same name (constr_2049)
(declare-fun mdgShortName (ModeDeclarationGroup) String)
(declare-fun mdgModeDeclarations (ModeDeclarationGroup) (Set ModeDeclaration))
(declare-fun portTypedByModeSwitchIface (PortPrototype ModeSwitchInterface) Bool)
(declare-fun modeSwitchIfaceHasMDG (ModeSwitchInterface ModeDeclarationGroup) Bool)
(declare-fun portInComponent (PortPrototype SwComponentType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((swc SwComponentType) (port1 PortPrototype) (port2 PortPrototype)
                 (iface1 ModeSwitchInterface) (iface2 ModeSwitchInterface)
                 (mdg1 ModeDeclarationGroup) (mdg2 ModeDeclarationGroup))
    (=> (and (portInComponent port1 swc) (portInComponent port2 swc)
             (portTypedByModeSwitchIface port1 iface1)
             (portTypedByModeSwitchIface port2 iface2)
             (modeSwitchIfaceHasMDG iface1 mdg1)
             (modeSwitchIfaceHasMDG iface2 mdg2)
             (= (mdgShortName mdg1) (mdgShortName mdg2))
             (not (= mdg1 mdg2)))
        (= (mdgModeDeclarations mdg1) (mdgModeDeclarations mdg2)))))

;; SwAxisCont mandatory information (constr_2050)
(declare-fun hasSwAxisIndex (SwAxisCont) Bool)
(declare-fun hasSwArraysize (SwAxisCont) Bool)

(assert (forall ((axisCont SwAxisCont))
    (and (hasSwAxisIndex axisCont)
         (hasSwArraysize axisCont))))

;; SwValueCont mandatory swArraysize for certain categories (constr_2051)
(declare-fun appValueSpecCategory (ApplicationValueSpecification) String)
(declare-fun appValueSpecHasSwValueCont (ApplicationValueSpecification SwValueCont) Bool)
(declare-fun swValueContHasSwArraysize (SwValueCont) Bool)

(assert (forall ((avSpec ApplicationValueSpecification) (swValCont SwValueCont))
    (=> (and (appValueSpecHasSwValueCont avSpec swValCont)
             (or (= (appValueSpecCategory avSpec) "CURVE")
                 (= (appValueSpecCategory avSpec) "MAP")
                 (= (appValueSpecCategory avSpec) "CUBOID")
                 (= (appValueSpecCategory avSpec) "CUBE_4")
                 (= (appValueSpecCategory avSpec) "CUBE_5")
                 (= (appValueSpecCategory avSpec) "COM_AXIS")
                 (= (appValueSpecCategory avSpec) "RES_AXIS")
                 (= (appValueSpecCategory avSpec) "VAL_BLK")))
        (swValueContHasSwArraysize swValCont))))

;; swArraysize and swValuesPhys consistency (constr_2052)
(declare-fun swArraysizeValues (SwAxisCont) (Array Int Int))
(declare-fun swArraysizeDimensions (SwAxisCont) Int)
(declare-fun swValuesPhysCount (SwValues) Int)
(declare-fun axisContSwValuesPhys (SwAxisCont) SwValues)

(assert (forall ((axisCont SwAxisCont))
    (let ((dims (swArraysizeDimensions axisCont)))
        (let ((totalSize (ite (> dims 0)
                             (let ((product 1))
                                 (forall ((i Int))
                                     (=> (and (>= i 0) (< i dims))
                                         (= product (* product (select (swArraysizeValues axisCont) i)))))
                                 product)
                             1)))
            (= (swValuesPhysCount (axisContSwValuesPhys axisCont)) totalSize)))))

;; ObdRatioServiceNeeds consistency with IUMPRNumerator role (constr_2053)
(declare-fun obdRatioConnectionType (ObdRatioServiceNeeds) String)
(declare-fun swcDepHasObdRatioNeeds (SwcServiceDependency ObdRatioServiceNeeds) Bool)
(declare-fun swcDepHasRoleBasedPort (SwcServiceDependency RoleBasedPortAssignment) Bool)
(declare-fun roleBasedPortRole (RoleBasedPortAssignment) String)

(assert (forall ((dep SwcServiceDependency) (needs ObdRatioServiceNeeds))
    (=> (and (swcDepHasObdRatioNeeds dep needs)
             (= (obdRatioConnectionType needs) "apiUse"))
        (exists ((rbpa RoleBasedPortAssignment))
            (and (swcDepHasRoleBasedPort dep rbpa)
                 (= (roleBasedPortRole rbpa) "IUMPRNumerator"))))))

(assert (forall ((dep SwcServiceDependency) (needs ObdRatioServiceNeeds) (rbpa RoleBasedPortAssignment))
    (=> (and (swcDepHasObdRatioNeeds dep needs)
             (= (obdRatioConnectionType needs) "observer")
             (swcDepHasRoleBasedPort dep rbpa))
        (not (= (roleBasedPortRole rbpa) "IUMPRNumerator")))))

;; System in rptSystem role must have category RPT_SYSTEM (constr_2054)
(declare-fun systemCategory (System) String)
(declare-fun systemInRptSystemRole (System) Bool)

(assert (forall ((sys System))
    (=> (systemInRptSystemRole sys)
        (= (systemCategory sys) "RPT_SYSTEM"))))

;; RapidPrototypingScenario consistency (constr_2056)
(declare-fun scenarioRptSystemRefs (RapidPrototypingScenario) (Set System))
(declare-fun scenarioRptArHookRefs (RapidPrototypingScenario) (Set System))

(assert (forall ((scenario RapidPrototypingScenario))
    (and
        ;; All rptSystem refs point to one System
        (exists ((sys System))
            (forall ((s System))
                (=> (set.member s (scenarioRptSystemRefs scenario))
                    (= s sys))))
        ;; If rptArHook exists, all point to one other System
        (=> (> (set.card (scenarioRptArHookRefs scenario)) 0)
            (exists ((sys System))
                (and (forall ((s System))
                         (=> (set.member s (scenarioRptArHookRefs scenario))
                             (= s sys)))
                     (not (set.member sys (scenarioRptSystemRefs scenario)))))))))

;; RuleBasedAxisCont mandatory information (constr_2057)
(declare-fun hasSwAxisIndexRB (RuleBasedAxisCont) Bool)
(declare-fun hasSwArraysizeRB (RuleBasedAxisCont) Bool)

(assert (forall ((rbAxisCont RuleBasedAxisCont))
    (and (hasSwAxisIndexRB rbAxisCont)
         (hasSwArraysizeRB rbAxisCont))))

;; RuleBasedValueCont mandatory swArraysize for certain categories (constr_2058)
(declare-fun appRuleBasedValueSpecCategory (ApplicationRuleBasedValueSpecification) String)
(declare-fun appRuleBasedValueSpecHasSwValueCont (ApplicationRuleBasedValueSpecification RuleBasedValueCont) Bool)
(declare-fun ruleBasedValueContHasSwArraysize (RuleBasedValueCont) Bool)

(assert (forall ((arbvSpec ApplicationRuleBasedValueSpecification) (rbValCont RuleBasedValueCont))
    (=> (and (appRuleBasedValueSpecHasSwValueCont arbvSpec rbValCont)
             (or (= (appRuleBasedValueSpecCategory arbvSpec) "CURVE")
                 (= (appRuleBasedValueSpecCategory arbvSpec) "MAP")
                 (= (appRuleBasedValueSpecCategory arbvSpec) "CUBOID")
                 (= (appRuleBasedValueSpecCategory arbvSpec) "CUBE_4")
                 (= (appRuleBasedValueSpecCategory arbvSpec) "CUBE_5")
                 (= (appRuleBasedValueSpecCategory arbvSpec) "COM_AXIS")
                 (= (appRuleBasedValueSpecCategory arbvSpec) "RES_AXIS")
                 (= (appRuleBasedValueSpecCategory arbvSpec) "CURVE_AXIS")
                 (= (appRuleBasedValueSpecCategory arbvSpec) "VAL_BLK")
                 (= (appRuleBasedValueSpecCategory arbvSpec) "ARRAY")))
        (ruleBasedValueContHasSwArraysize rbValCont))))

;; AutosarParameterRef target must be or be nested in ParameterDataPrototype (constr_2535)
(declare-sort ApplicationCompositeElementDataPrototype 0)
(declare-fun paramRefTargetsParam (AutosarParameterRef) Bool)
(declare-fun paramRefTargetsNestedInParam (AutosarParameterRef ApplicationCompositeElementDataPrototype ParameterDataPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((paramRef AutosarParameterRef))
    (or (paramRefTargetsParam paramRef)
        (exists ((acedp ApplicationCompositeElementDataPrototype) (pdp ParameterDataPrototype))
            (paramRefTargetsNestedInParam paramRef acedp pdp)))))

;; AutosarVariableRef target must be or be nested in VariableDataPrototype (constr_2536)
(declare-sort VariableDataPrototype 0)
(declare-fun varRefTargetsVar (AutosarVariableRef) Bool)
(declare-fun varRefTargetsNestedInVar (AutosarVariableRef ApplicationCompositeElementDataPrototype VariableDataPrototype) Bool)

(assert (forall ((varRef AutosarVariableRef))
    (or (varRefTargetsVar varRef)
        (exists ((acedp ApplicationCompositeElementDataPrototype) (vdp VariableDataPrototype))
            (varRefTargetsNestedInVar varRef acedp vdp)))))

;; Limits consistency (constr_2544)
(declare-fun appDataTypeLimits (ApplicationDataType) (Set Real))
(declare-fun appDataTypeCompuMethod (ApplicationDataType) CompuMethod)
(declare-fun compuMethodDefinitionRange (CompuMethod) (Set Real))
(declare-fun appDataTypeMappedToImplDataType (ApplicationDataType ImplementationDataType) Bool)
(declare-fun implDataTypeInternalConstrs (ImplementationDataType) (Set Real))
(declare-fun implDataTypeLimits (ImplementationDataType) (Set Real))
(declare-fun implDataTypeBaseType (ImplementationDataType) BaseType)
(declare-fun baseTypeLimits (BaseType) (Set Real))

(assert (forall ((adt ApplicationDataType))
    (let ((limits (appDataTypeLimits adt))
          (compuRange (compuMethodDefinitionRange (appDataTypeCompuMethod adt))))
        ;; Limits within CompuMethod definition range
        (forall ((limit Real))
            (=> (set.member limit limits)
                (set.member limit compuRange))))))

(assert (forall ((adt ApplicationDataType) (idt ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (appDataTypeMappedToImplDataType adt idt)
        ;; Internal limits within or equal to internalConstrs
        (forall ((limit Real))
            (=> (set.member limit (appDataTypeLimits adt))
                (set.member limit (implDataTypeInternalConstrs idt)))))))

(assert (forall ((idt ImplementationDataType))
    (let ((limits (implDataTypeLimits idt))
          (baseTypeLims (baseTypeLimits (implDataTypeBaseType idt))))
        ;; ImplementationDataType limits within BaseType limits
        (forall ((limit Real))
            (=> (set.member limit limits)
                (set.member limit baseTypeLims))))))

;; invalidValue within ImplementationDataType range (constr_2545)
(declare-fun invalidValueForImplDataType (ImplementationDataType) Real)
(declare-fun hasInvalidValue (ImplementationDataType) Bool)

(assert (forall ((idt ImplementationDataType))
    (=> (hasInvalidValue idt)
        (set.member (invalidValueForImplDataType idt) (implDataTypeLimits idt)))))

;; dataConstr shall not relax valueAxisDataType.dataConstr (constr_2548)
(declare-fun swDataDefPropsDataConstr (SwDataDefPropsContent) DataConstr)
(declare-fun swDataDefPropsValueAxisDataType (SwDataDefPropsContent) ApplicationPrimitiveDataType)
(declare-fun valueAxisDataTypeDataConstr (ApplicationPrimitiveDataType) DataConstr)
(declare-fun dataConstrStricterOrEqual (DataConstr DataConstr) Bool)

(assert (forall ((swProps SwDataDefPropsContent))
    (=> (and (swDataDefPropsDataConstr swProps)
             (swDataDefPropsValueAxisDataType swProps))
        (dataConstrStricterOrEqual 
            (swDataDefPropsDataConstr swProps)
            (valueAxisDataTypeDataConstr (swDataDefPropsValueAxisDataType swProps))))))

;; Units of input axis consistency (constr_2549)
(declare-fun inputAxisUnit (SwAxisIndividual) Unit)
(declare-fun inputAxisCompuMethodUnit (SwAxisIndividual) Unit)
(declare-fun inputAxisInputVarTypeUnit (SwAxisIndividual) Unit)
(declare-fun inSameInputAxisContext (SwAxisIndividual SwAxisIndividual) Bool)

(assert (forall ((axis1 SwAxisIndividual) (axis2 SwAxisIndividual))
    (=> (inSameInputAxisContext axis1 axis2)
        (= (inputAxisUnit axis1) (inputAxisUnit axis2)))))

;; Units of value axis consistency (constr_2550)
(declare-fun valueAxisUnit (SwDataDefPropsContent) Unit)
(declare-fun inSameValueAxisContext (SwDataDefPropsContent SwDataDefPropsContent) Bool)

(assert (forall ((props1 SwDataDefPropsContent) (props2 SwDataDefPropsContent))
    (=> (inSameValueAxisContext props1 props2)
        (= (valueAxisUnit props1) (valueAxisUnit props2)))))
